01

UniMatSim：基于通用机器学习潜力的高通量材料模拟自动化框架#

原题：UniMatSim: A High-Throughput Materials Simulation Automation Framework Based on Universal Machine Learning Potentials
作者：Yanjin Xiang、Yihan Nie、Weiyi Wang、Yunzhi Gao、Haidi Wang、Wei Hu。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-19

AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：通用机器学习原子间势 (UMLIP) 的精度接近第一性原理计算，而成本却很低，显示出大规模材料模拟的巨大潜力。然而，碎片化的UMLIP生态系统缺乏统一的接口标准和集成框架，阻碍了它们在高吞吐量工作流程中的自动化部署。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了 UniMatSim，一个模块化的 Python 框架。它系统地集成了各种 UMLIP（例如 CHGNet、M3GNet、MACE），并自动化了从结构优化到稳定性验证的工作流程。该框架可以通过抽象…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：machine learning potential、first-principles calculations、interatomic potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

通用机器学习原子间势 (UMLIP) 的精度接近第一性原理计算，而成本却很低，显示出大规模材料模拟的巨大潜力。然而，碎片化的UMLIP生态系统缺乏统一的接口标准和集成框架，阻碍了它们在高吞吐量工作流程中的自动化部署。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了 UniMatSim，一个模块化的 Python 框架。它系统地集成了各种 UMLIP（例如 CHGNet、M3GNet、MACE），并自动化了从结构优化到稳定性验证的工作流程。该框架可以通过抽象接口实现无缝模型切换，整合任务编排，并为关键属性（弹性、声子、分子动力学）提供标准化模块，包括低维材料的自动处理。作为测试用例，我们使用 2D Lieb 晶格系统构建了一个多阶段高通量筛选工作流程，涵盖结构优化、弹性稳定性和声子谱计算。从 1,176 个候选组合开始，四模型共识管道产生 393 个稳定结构。这些通过磁态筛选和 DFT 能带结构计算进行细化，得到具有交错磁带特性的 59 个李布晶格候选者。结果表明 UniMatSim 显着提高了计算效率和再现性，为数据驱动的材料发现和设计提供了可靠的基础设施。

English Abstract

Universal machine learning interatomic potentials (UMLIPs) offer accuracy close to first-principles calculations at a fraction of the cost, showing significant potential for large-scale material simulations. However, the fragmented UMLIPs ecosystem lacks unified interface standards and integration frameworks, hindering their automated deployment in high-throughput workflows. To address this, we present UniMatSim, a modular Python framework. It systematically integrates various UMLIPs (e.g., CHGNet, M3GNet, MACE) and automates workflows from structural optimization to stability verification. The framework enables seamless model switching via abstracted interfaces, incorporates task orchestration, and provides standardized modules for key properties (elasticity, phonons, molecular dynamics), including automated handling for low-dimensional materials. As a test case, using the 2D Lieb lattice system, we constructed a multi-stage high-throughput screening workflow covering structural optimization, elastic stability, and phonon spectrum calculations. Starting from 1,176 candidate compositions, a four-model consensus pipeline yields 393 stable structures. These are refined by magnetic-state screening and DFT band-structure calculations to 59 Lieb-lattice candidates with staggered-magnetic-band characteristics. Results show UniMatSim significantly improves computational efficiency and reproducibility, providing a reliable infrastructure for data-driven materials discovery and design.

通过具有机器学习原子间势的大正则蒙特卡罗揭示 SrTiO₃ 晶界的结构、电子和静电景观#

原题：Uncovering the Structural, Electronic, and Electrostatic Landscapes of SrTiO₃ Grain Boundaries via Grand Canonical Monte Carlo with a Machine-Learning Interatomic Potential
作者：Xing He、Kairi Masuda、Aaron M. Schankler、Songsong Zhou、Andrew M. Rappe。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-19
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：钙钛矿的晶界表现出丰富的电子、极性和磁性特性，但识别其原子结构和成分仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们将大正则蒙特卡罗（GCMC）方法与通用机器学习原子间势相结合，探索 Σ5(310)[001] SrTiO₃ 晶界的耦合成分和结构相空间。由此产生的化学势相图确定了属于四个基序家族的十个稳定结构：大型 Sr 风筝、小型 Sr 风筝、不完整的 Sr 大风筝和富钛结构。将 GCMC 方法扩展到 Sigma5(210)[001] 和 Sigma17(…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、density functional theory、electronic structure、perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

钙钛矿的晶界表现出丰富的电子、极性和磁性特性，但识别其原子结构和成分仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们将大正则蒙特卡罗（GCMC）方法与通用机器学习原子间势相结合，探索 Σ5(310)[001] SrTiO₃ 晶界的耦合成分和结构相空间。由此产生的化学势相图确定了属于四个基序家族的十个稳定结构：大型 Sr 风筝、小型 Sr 风筝、不完整的 Sr 大风筝和富钛结构。将 GCMC 方法扩展到 Sigma5(210)[001] 和 Sigma17(410)[001] 晶界，揭示了晶界平面和倾角如何影响小型和大型风筝图案之间的竞争。预测的 Sigma5(310)[001] 晶界的密度泛函理论计算表明，成分和原子结构强烈控制导带和价带边缘态的空间分布、界面带排列和载流子局域化。出现了四种不同的电子状态：局域电子掺杂态、离域电子掺杂态、绝缘态和空穴吸引态。我们的静电学分析揭示了与局部原子密度和键合相关的重建 GB 核心的短程电场以及由空间电荷产生的核心外部的较长程场的共存。这些场还可以耦合到局部极性畸变并生成与成分相关的偏振图案。这些结果为探索氧化物钙钛矿晶界结构建立了一个有效的框架，并揭示了化学计量和原子重构如何与电子结构、静电和局部极化耦合，为功能钙钛矿的预测晶界工程提供了一条途径。

English Abstract

Grain boundaries in perovskites exhibit a rich variety of electronic, polar and magnetic properties, yet identifying their atomic structures and compositions remains challenging. Here, we combine the Grand Canonical Monte Carlo (GCMC) method with a universal machine-learning interatomic potential to explore the coupled compositional and structural phase space of Σ5(310)[001] SrTiO₃ grain boundaries. The resulting chemical potential phase diagram identifies ten stable structures belonging to four motif families: large Sr kite, small Sr kite, incomplete Sr large kite, and Ti-rich structures. Extending the GCMC method to the Sigma5(210)[001] and Sigma17(410)[001] grain boundaries reveals how grain boundary plane and inclination angle influence the competition between small- and large-kite motifs. Density functional theory calculations of the predicted Sigma5(310)[001] grain boundaries show that the composition and atomic structure strongly govern the spatial distribution of conduction and valence band edge states, interfacial band alignment, and carrier localization. Four distinct electronic regimes emerge: localized electron-doped, delocalized electron-doped, insulating, and hole-attracting states. Our electrostatics analysis reveals the coexistence of the short-range electric fields at the reconstructed GB core associated with local atomic density and bonding, and the longer-range fields outside the core arising from the space charge. These fields can also couple to local polar distortions and generate composition-dependent polarization patterns. These results establish an efficient framework for exploring oxide perovskite grain boundary structures and reveal how stoichiometry and atomic reconstruction couple to electronic structure, electrostatics, and local polarization, providing a route toward predictive grain boundary engineering in functional perovskites.

根据量子蒙特卡罗训练的机器学习原子间势绘制接近熔化状态的液态氢相图#

原题：The phase diagram of fluid hydrogen near melting from machine-learned interatomic potentials trained on quantum Monte Carlo
作者：Shubhang Goswami、Hongwei Niu、Ilnur Saitov、Mathieu Istas、Yubo Yang、Scott Jensen 等 9 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-20
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：随着温度或压力的升高，分子氢的解离可以通过相变逐渐或突然发生。尽管进行了大量的实验和理论工作，但在高于固体熔化的温度下稠密氢的相图仍不清楚。在这里，我们使用两个机器学习的原子间势对稠密氢进行经典和量子路径积分分子动力学模拟，这两个原子间势均经过量子蒙特卡罗能量和力的训练。由此产生的相图是使用最先进的技术构建的。两个模型都显示了从分子到原子液体的一级相变，终止于临界点，其位置是使用有限尺寸缩放技术确定的。三相点位于熔化曲线的最高温度处，位…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、molecular dynamics、phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

随着温度或压力的升高，分子氢的解离可以通过相变逐渐或突然发生。尽管进行了大量的实验和理论工作，但在高于固体熔化的温度下稠密氢的相图仍不清楚。在这里，我们使用两个机器学习的原子间势对稠密氢进行经典和量子路径积分分子动力学模拟，这两个原子间势均经过量子蒙特卡罗能量和力的训练。由此产生的相图是使用最先进的技术构建的。两个模型都显示了从分子到原子液体的一级相变，终止于临界点，其位置是使用有限尺寸缩放技术确定的。三相点位于熔化曲线的最高温度处，位于液-液线与熔化曲线的交叉点处，并且应该在斜坡压缩实验中产生清晰的特征。

English Abstract

Dissociation of molecular hydrogen increasing temperature or pressure may either occur gradually or abruptly, via a phase transition. Despite considerable experimental and theoretical efforts, the phase diagram of dense hydrogen at temperatures above solid melting is not precisely known. Here, we perform classical and quantum Path Integral molecular dynamics simulations of dense hydrogen using two machine-learned interatomic potentials both trained on quantum Monte Carlo energies and forces. The resulting phase diagram is constructed using state-of-the-art techniques. Both models show a first-order phase transition from the molecular to the atomic liquid terminating in critical points whose locations were determined using finite-size scaling techniques. Triple points, located at the highest temperature of the melting curve, are found at the intersection of the liquid-liquid lines with the melting curve and should lead to clear signatures in ramp compression experiments.

使用经典和机器学习的原子间势研究 LiF 中的动力学和传输#

原题：Dynamics and transport in LiF using classical and machine learned interatomic potentials
作者：Harvey L. Devereux、Margaret-Ann Withington、Alin M. Elena、Cillian Cockrell、Kostya Trachenko。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-18
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：我们对熔融氟化锂进行分子动力学模拟，将经典的 Buckingham 和 Born-Huggins-Mayer 势与机器学习的原子间势 - MACE-MP-0b3（小）基础模型势及其微调版本进行比较。我们发现，开箱即用的 MACE-MP-0b3 能够准确地再现与可极化电势相当的液态实验剪切粘度，尽管它本身不是可极化的。以前使用经典势计算的粘度预测不足，这归因于熔化温度受到抑制。受此启发，我们比较了这些电势的熔点。我们发现机器学习势解决了经…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们对熔融氟化锂进行分子动力学模拟，将经典的 Buckingham 和 Born-Huggins-Mayer 势与机器学习的原子间势 - MACE-MP-0b3（小）基础模型势及其微调版本进行比较。我们发现，开箱即用的 MACE-MP-0b3 能够准确地再现与可极化电势相当的液态实验剪切粘度，尽管它本身不是可极化的。以前使用经典势计算的粘度预测不足，这归因于熔化温度受到抑制。受此启发，我们比较了这些电势的熔点。我们发现机器学习势解决了经典势熔点的显着低估问题，尽管精炼势和基础模型之间存在显着差异。我们探讨了每个模型的微观结构和动力学，以进一步阐明经典势和机器学习势之间粘度的显着变化。我们发现，尽管它们的熔化温度较低，但经典势由于其固定电荷而产生相当僵硬、频率更高的单粒子和集体电荷传输动力学。然而，在长波长限制下，多体机器学习势具有更具弹性的集体运动，它控制动量的扩散，因此导致剪切粘度增强。

English Abstract

We perform molecular dynamics simulations of molten Lithium Fluoride, comparing the classical Buckingham and Born-Huggins-Mayer potentials with machine-learnt interatomic potentials - the MACE-MP-0b3 (small) foundation model potential, and a fine-tuned version thereof. We find that the MACE-MP-0b3, out-of-the-box, is able to accurately reproduce the experimental shear viscosity across the liquid state comparable with polarisable potentials despite not itself being polarisable. Previous viscosities computed using classical potentials are under-predicted, which has previously been attributed to a suppressed melting temperature. Prompted by this we compare the melting points of these potentials. We find that the machine-learnt potentials address the significant underestimation of the melting point of the classical potentials, though the refined potential and the foundation model differ significantly between themselves. We probe the microscopic structure and dynamics of each model to further elucidate the considerable change in viscosity between classical and machine-learnt potentials. We find that, despite their lower melting temperature, classical potentials produce considerably stiffer, higher frequency, single-particle and collective charge-transport dynamics on account of their fixed charges. However, in the long-wavelength limit, the many-body machine-learnt potentials feature more elastic collective motion that governs the diffusion of momentum and is therefore responsible for the enhanced shear viscosity.

在图神经网络中编码轨道投影状态密度以进行材料特性预测#

原题：Encoding Orbital-Projected Densities of States in Graph Neural Networks for Materials Property Prediction
作者：Paulo Pires、Pierre-Paul De Breuck、Mauro Fava、Hai-Chen Wang、Miguel A. L. Marques。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-20
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：图神经网络已成为从晶体结构预测材料特性的主要机器学习架构。然而，原子节点特征的初始化相对较少受到关注，传统方法依赖于静态元素描述符，这些描述符不携带有关其晶体主体中每个原子的量子力学电子环境的信息。在这里，我们表明，通过直接从密度泛函理论计算计算出的位点投影轨道态密度（pDOS）指纹来增强原子节点表示，可以在预测性能方面产生系统性和实质性的改进。在消息传递之前，这些表示与每个原子位点的 Pettifor 元素嵌入融合。对于超导临界温度…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural networks、density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

图神经网络已成为从晶体结构预测材料特性的主要机器学习架构。然而，原子节点特征的初始化相对较少受到关注，传统方法依赖于静态元素描述符，这些描述符不携带有关其晶体主体中每个原子的量子力学电子环境的信息。在这里，我们表明，通过直接从密度泛函理论计算计算出的位点投影轨道态密度（pDOS）指纹来增强原子节点表示，可以在预测性能方面产生系统性和实质性的改进。在消息传递之前，这些表示与每个原子位点的 Pettifor 元素嵌入融合。对于超导临界温度 T_c 和光学介电常数 ϵᵢₙfₜy，相对于元素描述符基线，pDOS 增强分别将预测误差减少了 22.9% 和 27.9%。这些改进与通过将训练集大小加倍所实现的改进相当。然而，收益取决于训练集的大小。对于 Heusler 化合物的磁交换能，数据集要小得多，改进会减少，表明当训练数据超过 pDOS 特征向量的长度时，pDOS 增强最有效。我们引入了一种可解释的光谱注意门控机制，该机制揭示了模型自主学习优先考虑与每个目标属性最物理相关的轨道通道和能量窗口。这些结果将 pDOS 增强图节点确立为一种广泛适用的策略，用于将第一原理电子结构知识注入图网络，为数据稀缺状态下的高精度属性预测开辟了一条实用途径。

English Abstract

Graph neural networks have become the dominant machine-learning architecture for predicting materials properties from crystal structures. Yet the initialization of atomic node features has received comparatively little attention, and conventional approaches rely on static elemental descriptors that carry no information about the quantum-mechanical electronic environment of each atom in its crystalline host. Here we show that augmenting atomic node representations with site-projected orbital density of states (pDOS) fingerprints, computed directly from density functional theory calculations, yields systematic and substantial improvements in predictive performance.These representations are fused with Pettifor elemental embeddings at each atomic site before message passing. For the superconducting critical temperature T_c and the optical dielectric constant ϵᵢₙfₜy,the pDOS augmentation reduces prediction errors by 22.9% and 27.9%, respectively, relative to the elemental-descriptor baseline. These improvements are comparable to those achieved by doubling the training-set size. The gains are, however, contingent on training-set size. For the magnetic exchange energies of Heusler compounds, a substantially smaller dataset, the improvement is reduced,indicating that pDOS augmentation is most effective when the training data exceeds the length of the pDOS feature vector. We introduce an interpretable spectral attention-gating mechanism that reveals that the model autonomously learns to prioritize the orbital channels and energy windows most physically relevant to each target property. These results establish pDOS-augmented graph nodes as a broadly applicable strategy for infusing first-principles electronic-structure knowledge into graph networks, opening a practical route to high-accuracy property prediction in data-scarce regimes.

中文摘要

用于提取电容的器件布局电磁仿真需要大量计算，这是精确设计电路器件的主要障碍。机器学习模型可以绕过这些模拟，但以前的方法仅限于仅使用特定组件的布局，并且需要对这些相同组件进行数千次模拟作为训练数据。我们推出的 QCap 模型可以在几秒钟内准确预测任意超导器件布局的麦克斯韦电容矩阵，而 Ansys Q3D 仿真则需要几分钟到几小时。我们开发了一种新颖的设计嵌入算法，可将布局转换为通用图形格式，使模型能够与使用任何工具创建的任何布局一起使用。为了实现高精度，我们通过电容矩模拟的快速方法获得粗略的物理先验，然后训练模型来纠正该先验。在从训练数据中保留的 4,009 个测试设计中，模型输出与 Q3D 模拟一致，中位误差为 0.177%，而在从未见过的组件系列上，其误差保持在 1% 以下。我们通过在笔记本电脑上在 12 秒内获得 9 超导量子位器件的电容矩阵（相比之下 Ansys 中的 19.9 小时和 228 GB 内存）误差低于百分之一，以及在笔记本电脑上在 3.4 分钟内获得（太大而无法模拟）100 量子位、781 导体器件的电容来演示模型的缩放。我们提供 SQuADDS 和 Hugging Face 上的开源模型和数据。我们的结果为大规模精确电路设计提供了强大的工具，并指导模型的开发以绕过其他物理模拟。

English Abstract

Electromagnetic simulations of device layouts to extract capacitance are computationally intensive, a major obstacle to accurate design of circuit devices. Machine learning models can bypass these simulations, but previous approaches have been restricted to layouts using only particular components, and require thousands of simulations of those same components as training data. We present QCap, a model that accurately predicts the Maxwell capacitance matrix of an arbitrary superconducting device layout in seconds, compared to minutes to hours of Ansys Q3D simulations. We develop a novel design-embedding algorithm that converts the layout into a universal graph format, allowing the model to work with any layout created with any tool. To achieve high accuracy, we obtain a coarse physics prior from a fast method of moments simulation of capacitance, then train the model to correct this prior. On 4,009 test designs withheld from training data the model outputs agree with Q3D simulations to a median error of 0.177%, and on never-seen component families it stays below 1% error. We demonstrate the model's scaling by obtaining the capacitance matrix of a 9-superconducting-qubit device in 12 seconds on a laptop (compared to 19.9 h and 228 GB of memory in Ansys) with sub-percent error, and capacitance of a (too large to simulate) 100-qubit, 781-conductor device in 3.4 min on a laptop. We provide the open-source models and data on SQuADDS and Hugging Face. Our results provide a powerful tool for large-scale accurate circuit design and guide the development of models to bypass other physical simulations.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

大多数能量材料（例如用作电池电极、光伏材料或光催化剂）的一个显著特征是其能够传导电荷，如电子和空穴。这种导电性的确切机制取决于材料，但通常由移动小极化子的存在主导，即通过其环境的极化而强烈局域化的电子或空穴。在计算方面，可以通过使用自相互作用校正或混合密度泛函理论（DFT）以及局部几何优化，以合理的精度处理小极化子。可以采用类似的方法来确定小极化子通过跃迁运动的能垒。遗憾的是，现实的能源材料往往具有非常大量的对称性不等价的极化子构型，以及相应的大量跃迁势垒，因此难以明确处理。因此，在本研究中，我们使用机器学习（ML）代理模型扩展了DFT计算的计算范围，然后将其输入动力学模型，以定量估算电池阳极材料（Li₄Ti₅O₁₂或LTO）在真实缺陷条件下的导电率。我们计算出的室温导电率为6.7 × 10⁻³ S/cm，完全在实验范围内。为此，在3649个对称性不等价的极化子势垒计算中，我们仅需要总共154个，从而使计算效率提高了20多倍。总体而言，我们的工作展示了如何使用经过DFT训练的ML代理模型来定量和高效地采样能量材料中小极化子的动力学。

English Abstract

One of the defining characteristics of most energy materials, used as e.g.~battery electrodes, photovoltaics, or photocatalysts, is their ability conduct charges such as electrons and holes. The exact mechanism of this conductivity depends on the material, but is often dominated by the existence of mobile small polarons, i.e.~electrons or holes strongly localised through the polarisation of their environment. Computationally, small polarons can be treated with reasonably accuracy through the use of self-interaction corrected or hybrid density functional theory (DFT) together with local geometry optimisation. Similar approaches can be employed to determine the energy barriers to movement of the polarons through hopping. Unfortunately, realistic energy materials tend have very large numbers of symmetry inequivalent polaronic configurations and correspondingly large numbers of hopping barriers, making them hard to tackle explicitly. In this work we therefore extend the computational reach of DFT calculations with machine-learned (ML) surrogate models, which then feed into a kinetic model to quantitatively estimate the conductivity of a battery anode material (Li₄Ti₅O₁₂ or LTO) at realistic, defected conditions. We calculate the conductivity at room temperature to be 6.7 × 10⁻³ S/cm which lies well in the experimental range. To this end, we only needed a total of 154 polaron barrier calculations, out of 3649 symmetry inequivalent ones, achieving an over 20-fold increase of computational efficiency. Overall, our work demonstrates how DFT-trained ML surrogates can be used to quantitatively and efficiently sample the dynamics of small polarons in energy materials.

中文摘要

固体引力波的机器学习主要集中在标量准粒子能量修正的预测上。在这里，我们引入了一个相位感知的多体变压器，它可以预测完整的 k 分辨复杂自能矩阵。作为原理证明，我们应用该模型来预测准粒子哈密顿量对角化之前和之后的准粒子能量，对于静态 COHSEX 近似中的 1745 种二维材料的数据集获得了 29 meV 的测试集平均绝对误差 (MAE)，对于在等离子体激元极模型中计算的 433 种材料的更有限的数据集获得了 60 meV 的测试集平均绝对误差 (MAE)。 COHSEX 预训练和微调可将此 MAE 降低至 42 meV。预测矩阵还准确地再现了 QP 波函数。

English Abstract

Machine learning of GW in solids has largely focused on prediction of scalar quasiparticle energy corrections. Here, we introduce a phase-aware many-body transformer that predicts the full k-resolved complex self-energy matrix. As a proof of principle, we apply the model to predict quasiparticle energies before and after diagonalization of the quasiparticle Hamiltonian, obtaining a test set mean absolute error (MAE) of 29 meV for a dataset of 1745 2D materials in the static COHSEX approximation and 60 meV for a more limited dataset of 433 materials calculated within a plasmon-pole model. COHSEX pretraining followed by finetuning reduces this MAE to 42 meV. The predicted matrices also accurately reproduce QP wavefunctions.

中文摘要

计算建模已成为材料科学的基石，原子模拟、高通量计算和人工智能的进步改变了材料的理解、发现和设计方式。这些发展建立在几代理论创新的基础上，这些创新将复杂的数学和物理原理转化为实用的预测工具。然而，计算方法日益多样化给寻求对其理论基础连贯理解的从业者带来了挑战。本专着借鉴了作者的研究经验，通过势能景观和统计抽样的概念，对计算材料科学提供了面向实践者的教学概述。它通过对经验力场、密度泛函理论、多体电子结构方法、统计采样和性能预测的系统处理，探讨了这些概念如何支撑结构稳定性、相行为以及电子和磁性能。它进一步研究了高通量工作流程、材料数据库和人工智能的概率基础。特别强调原子表示、图神经网络和生成模型，强调对称性、群论、微分几何和随机过程在现代材料建模中的作用。通过将现有的物理理论与新兴的数据驱动方法联系起来，本专着提供了计算材料科学的统一视角，并确定了基于物理的、高效且可靠的材料发现的机遇和挑战。

English Abstract

Computational modelling has become a cornerstone of materials science, with advances in atomistic simulations, high-throughput computation, and artificial intelligence transforming how materials are understood, discovered, and designed. These developments build upon generations of theoretical innovations that have translated complex mathematical and physical principles into practical predictive tools. However, the growing diversity of computational methods presents challenges for practitioners seeking a coherent understanding of their theoretical foundations. Drawing upon the author's research experience, this monograph provides a practitioner-oriented, pedagogical overview of computational materials science, unified through the concepts of potential-energy landscapes and statistical sampling. It explores how these concepts underpin structural stability, phase behaviour, and electronic and magnetic properties, through a systematic treatment of empirical force fields, density-functional theory, many-body electronic-structure methods, statistical sampling, and property prediction. It further examines high-throughput workflows, materials databases, and the probabilistic foundations of artificial intelligence. Particular emphasis is placed on atomistic representations, graph neural networks, and generative models, highlighting the roles of symmetry, group theory, differential geometry, and stochastic processes in modern materials modelling. By connecting established physical theories with emerging data-driven approaches, this monograph offers a unified perspective on computational materials science and identifies opportunities and challenges toward physics-informed, efficient, and reliable materials discovery.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

大面积多层二维 (2D) 材料中复杂起皱变形的原子连续模拟计算成本高昂。为了加速这些模拟，这项工作提出了一个机器学习（ML）框架，该框架首先构建变形场的低维表示，然后学习这个缩减空间中的变形。由于离散变形场的维数极高、需要保持表面连续性和规定的几何约束、起皱图案的随机性以及训练数据的有限可用性，变形二维膜的降维具有挑战性。我们使用二维函数主成分分析（FPCA）来应对这些挑战。所提出的 FPCA 表示将包含数百万自由度的变形场减少到只有数十维的潜在空间，同时保持变形表面的连续性。此外，通过在 FPCA 表示中适当选择基函数，可以准确满足规定的齐次边界约束。然后训练神经网络来预测系统的主要成分和负载参数。由此产生的 ML 预测变形场被用作原子连续解算器的高质量初始配置，该解算器随后执行能量最小化以获得平衡解。该框架展示了多层石墨烯经受单轴压缩、双轴压缩以及组合双轴压缩和简单剪切的情况。对于计算量最大的大应变情况，所提出的方法实现了至少 6.8 倍的加速，同时收敛到的最终能量与使用传统原子连续初始化获得的能量相当，在某些情况下低于使用传统原子连续初始化获得的能量。

English Abstract

Atomistic-continuum simulations of complex wrinkling deformations in large-area multilayer two-dimensional (2D) materials are computationally expensive. To accelerate these simulations, this work presents a machine-learning (ML) framework that first constructs a low-dimensional representation of the deformation field, then learns the deformation in this reduced space. Dimensionality reduction of deformed 2D membranes is challenging because of the extremely high dimensionality of discretized deformation fields, the need to preserve surface continuity and prescribed geometric constraints, the stochastic nature of wrinkling patterns, and the limited availability of training data. We address these challenges using two-dimensional functional principal component analysis (FPCA). The proposed FPCA representation reduces deformation fields containing millions of degrees of freedom to latent spaces with only tens of dimensions while preserving the continuity of the deformed surface. In addition, prescribed homogeneous boundary constraints are satisfied exactly through an appropriate choice of basis functions in the FPCA representation. A neural network is then trained to predict the principal components from the system and loading parameters. The resulting ML-predicted deformation fields are used as high-quality initial configurations for the atomistic-continuum solver, which subsequently performs energy minimization to obtain equilibrium solutions. The framework is demonstrated for multilayer graphene subjected to uniaxial compression, biaxial compression, and combined biaxial compression and simple shear. For the most computationally intensive large-strain cases, the proposed approach achieves speedups of at least 6.8 times while converging to final energies comparable to, and in some cases lower than, those obtained using conventional atomistic-continuum initialization.

中文摘要

钙钛矿太阳能电池的实验优化受到吸收体成分、器件层和加工的组合多样性的限制，这使得虚拟筛选对于在制造之前优先考虑配方具有吸引力。我们开发了一个代理引导的强化学习框架，将食谱生成视为虚拟筛选，丰富了高性能实验邻居附近的候选候选名单。冻结的表格先验数据拟合网络 (TabPFN) 指导配方生成，而 890 个测量设备的参考池则支持基于邻域的评估。对 1,846 种器件进行五倍评估后发现，前十名器件的平均功率转换效率 (PCE) 从 19.68% 提高到 23.74%。在每次运行 3,000 次替代评估下，将三个学习策略与六个基线进行比较。在前 100 名入围名单中，近端策略优化 (PPO) 和循环 PPO (RPPO) 的邻域支持命中率分别为 23% 和 24%（纯随机的 2.9% 的 7.9 倍和 8.3 倍），而在 K = 100 时，具有最高支持命中率（L-SHADE 和 Ax BO，并列）的基线为 10%（3.4 倍）。增益与弱支持分数。在 9,000 个食谱中，65.4% 与相似度为 0.80 的成分系列相匹配，下降到 11.7%（相似度为 0.90）。结果支持以富集为导向的优先顺序，但不支持实验性能、种子稳健性或匹配预算优势。

English Abstract

Experimental optimization of perovskite solar cells is constrained by combinatorial diversity in absorber compositions, device layers, and processing, which makes virtual screening attractive for prioritizing recipes before fabrication. We develop a surrogate-guided reinforcement-learning framework that casts recipe generation as virtual screening, enriching candidate shortlists near high-performing experimental neighbors. A frozen Tabular Prior-data Fitted Network (TabPFN) guides recipe generation, while a reference pool of 890 measured devices enables neighborhood-based assessment. Five-fold evaluation on 1,846 devices shows top-ten selection raises mean power conversion efficiency (PCE) from 19.68% to 23.74%. Three learned policies are compared with six baselines under 3,000 surrogate evaluations per run. In top-100 shortlists, Proximal policy optimization (PPO) and recurrent PPO (RPPO) achieve neighborhood-supported hit rates of 23% and 24% (7.9- and 8.3-fold Pure Random's 2.9%), versus 10% (3.4-fold) for the baselines with the highest supported hit rate (L-SHADE and Ax BO, tied) at K = 100. Gains coexist with weak-support fractions. Of 9,000 recipes, 65.4% match a composition family at similarity 0.80, falling to 11.7% at 0.90. Results support enrichment-oriented prioritization but not experimental performance, seed robustness, or matched-budget advantage.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

第一原理结构松弛和能带计算需要大量的计算和专业知识。在这里，我们介绍 Perfumer，一个基于大型语言模型的代理框架，它将 Quantum ESPRESSO 与两个预测模型结合起来。在测试的结构中，使用 Perfumer-relax 模型进行预松弛，然后进行 QE 细化，其总体加速比单独使用 QE 提高了 2.75 倍。 Perfumer-band 模型将周期图神经网络与潜在 Hermitian 谱头相结合，在任何请求的波矢量处返回 12 个能带能量，保留倒数晶格周期性和时间反转对称性，而无需构造哈密顿量。在 125,153 个自行设计的能带结构上进行训练后，Perfumer-band 模型在 2,607 种测试材料（包括镧系元素、锕系元素和磁性化合物）上达到了 0.157 eV 的每种材料平均绝对误差中值，这些测试材料来自跨越 85 个元素的保留成分族，每种材料在两个消费级 CPU 内核上花费了约 97 毫秒。该代理将自然语言请求转变为松弛带工作流程，从单个上传的结构到自动构建的新候选并返回预测的属性。

English Abstract

First-principles structural relaxation and band calculations require substantial computation and expertise. Here we present Perfumer, a large language model-based agentic framework that couples Quantum ESPRESSO with two predictive models. Across tested structures, pre-relaxation with Perfumer-relax model followed by QE refinement yielded a 2.75-fold overall speedup over QE alone. Perfumer-band model combines a periodic graph neural network with a latent Hermitian spectral head returns 12 band energies at any requested wavevector, preserving reciprocal lattice periodicity and time-reversal symmetry without constructing Hamiltonian. Trained on 125,153 self-curated band structures, Perfumer-band model reaches a median per-material mean absolute error of 0.157 eV on 2,607 test materials, including lanthanide, actinide and magnetic compounds, from held-out composition families spanning 85 elements, taking about 97 ms per material on two consumer CPU cores. The agent turns natural-language requests into relaxation-to-band workflows, from a single uploaded structure to automatically constructed new candidates and returns predicted properties.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

自旋 1/2 戈薇海森堡反铁磁体是否具有带隙 Z2 或无带隙 U(1) 狄拉克自旋液体仍然是受阻磁学中的一个核心悬而未决的问题。在这里，我们通过使用不预设任何特定自旋液体结构的神经网络量子态直接计算三重态间隙来解决这个问题。将基于时间反转对称变压器的 ansatz 应用于多达 768 个位点的簇，我们在具有周期性边界条件的二维晶格上获得了最先进的变分能量，并估计热力学极限下每个位点的能量为 -0.43873(12)。磁序参数和三重态激发间隙的有限尺寸缩放表明，两者在热力学极限下都外推到零。总之，这些结果提供了无间隙自旋液体基态的独立且直接的证据，与 U(1) 狄拉克自旋液体一致。更广泛地说，他们证明神经网络量子态可以解决受挫量子磁体的基态特性，这对传统数值方法仍然具有挑战性。

English Abstract

Whether the spin-1/2 kagome Heisenberg antiferromagnet hosts a gapped Z₂ or a gapless U(1) Dirac spin liquid remains a central open question in frustrated magnetism. Here, we address this question by directly computing the triplet gap with a neural-network quantum state that does not presuppose any specific spin-liquid structure. Applying a time-reversal symmetric transformer-based ansatz to clusters of up to 768 sites, we obtain state-of-the-art variational energies on two-dimensional lattices with periodic boundary conditions and estimate an energy per site of -0.43873(12) in the thermodynamic limit. Finite-size scaling of the magnetic order parameter and triplet excitation gap shows that both extrapolate to zero in the thermodynamic limit. Together, these results provide independent and direct evidence of a gapless spin-liquid ground state, consistent with the U(1) Dirac spin liquid. More broadly, they demonstrate that neural-network quantum states can resolve ground-state properties of frustrated quantum magnets that remain challenging for conventional numerical methods.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基于分子基态神经网络量子态 (NQS) 的成功，自然激发态 (NES) 等多态框架 [Pfau et al., Science 2024, 385, eadn0137] 将 NQS 扩展到化学上重要的激发态。然而，如果不控制总自旋，此类计算可能需要包含其他自旋的许多干预状态，特别是在具有密集低能谱的强相关系统中。因此，我们概括了之前开发的自旋适应神经网络回流（SA-NNBF）ansatz [Li et al., arXiv:2604.06841 (2026)]，以提高其在指定自旋扇区内激发态计算的变分灵活性。我们在选定的轨道空间和自旋扇区内建立了这种扩展 ansatz 的普遍性，并使用数值基准来评估参数分配如何影响 ansatz 的准确性。借助自旋适应 ansatz，我们能够直接优化 O2 的第一激发态（单线态），而无需使用任何多态框架。将扩展 ansatz 与 NES 相结合，我们获得了 O2 的低位三重态和 [Fe2S2(SCH3)4]2- 团簇的单重态，而不包括其他自旋的介入态。单态和 NES 计算都实现了相对于精确能量的化学精度。我们的工作提供了一个实用的 NQS 框架，用于针对强相关系统中指定自旋的激发态。

English Abstract

Building on the success of neural-network quantum states (NQS) for molecular ground states, multi-state frameworks such as natural excited states (NES) [Pfau et al., Science 2024, 385, eadn0137] extend NQS to chemically important excited states. However, without control over total spin, such calculations can require the inclusion of many intervening states of other spins, particularly in strongly correlated systems with dense low-energy spectra. We therefore generalize our previously developed spin-adapted neural network backflow (SA-NNBF) ansatz [Li et al., arXiv:2604.06841 (2026)] to improve its variational flexibility for excited-state calculations within a prescribed spin sector. We establish the universality of this extended ansatz within the chosen orbital space and spin sector, and use numerical benchmarks to assess how parameter allocation affects the ansatz's accuracy. With the spin-adapted ansatz, we are able to directly optimize the first excited state (a singlet) of O₂ without using any multi-state frameworks. Combining the extended ansatz with NES, we obtain low-lying triplet states of O₂ and singlet states of the [Fe₂S₂(SCH₃)₄]²⁻ cluster without including intervening states of other spins. Both the single-state and NES calculations achieve chemical accuracy relative to the exact energies. Our work provides a practical NQS framework for targeting excited states of prescribed spin in strongly correlated systems.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

磁性晶体的电子结构共同取决于其自旋构型和现场哈伯德校正U，需要对每种组合进行单独的DFT+U计算。我们引入了 U-HamGNN，这是一个等变网络，它直接采用这两个输入并预测跨不同晶体化学的自旋解析哈密顿量。光谱混合优化器，通过梯度点沿其方向的一致性来对每个更新方向进行加权，在 10 轮基准中，哈密顿误差比 Muon 低 8%，比 AdamW 低 26%。预测的能带在训练窗口内及其之外再现了 U 相关的带隙打开和交磁自旋分裂；从哈密顿量导出的交换耦合和磁阶调用与校准集上的 DFT+U 一致。这种通用模型为磁研究提供了新工具。

English Abstract

The electronic structure of a magnetic crystal depends jointly on its spin configuration and the on-site Hubbard correction U, requiring a separate DFT+U calculation for each combination. We introduce U-HamGNN, an equivariant network that takes these two inputs directly and predicts spin-resolved Hamiltonians across diverse crystal chemistries. A spectral-hybrid optimizer that weights each update direction by how consistently the gradient points along it reaches 8% lower Hamiltonian error than Muon and 26% lower than AdamW in a ten-epoch benchmark. The predicted bands reproduce U-dependent gap opening and altermagnetic spin splitting within the trained window and beyond it; exchange couplings and magnetic-order calls derived from the Hamiltonian agree with DFT+U on a calibration set. This universal model provides a new tool for magnetic research.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了一种机器学习 (ML) 电荷密度模型 DeltaChargeNet，它与全势紧束缚松饼轨道 (FP-TB-MTO) 方法集成，用于材料和器件的电子结构和量子输运模拟。 ML 模型以低线性缩放成本预测原子结构的电子密度，而 FP-TB-MTO 提供系统哈密顿量的精确紧束缚表示。为了实现具有界面和无序的复杂设备的高保真机器学习密度，我们训练了一个等变图卷积网络来预测 Δρ（DFT 和原子叠加密度之间的差异），在非均匀系统所需的电荷中性约束下。辅助 ML 残余电荷模型可补偿主 Δ ρ 模型中的误差。然后在全相对论 FP-TB-MTO 框架内结合非平衡格林函数方法使用预测的密度。我们在块状 HgCdTe 无序合金（包括空位缺陷）和 Au/HgCdTe/Au 无序器件上验证了我们的方法。对于块状 HgCdTe，最大能带能量误差低于 10 meV。对于器件，电荷中性约束会抑制长波长电荷偏压，否则会使器件中心附近的静电势扭曲 0.68-1.88 eV，而残余补偿则纠正剩余的 0.16-0.46 eV 势垒失配，这对于再现正确的势垒轮廓至关重要。总之，这些校正在完全自洽 DFT 的 0.1 eV 范围内再现了层分辨电势，包括界面台阶和块状平台。最后，我们在 11220 个原子红外探测器中演示了量子传输。通过将第一原理精度与线性缩放推理相结合，DeltaChargeNet 无需完整的 DFT 自洽场环即可实现大规模设备仿真。

English Abstract

We present a machine-learning (ML) charge density model, DeltaChargeNet, integrated with the full-potential tight-binding muffin-tin orbital (FP-TB-MTO) method for electronic structure and quantum transport simulations of materials and devices. The ML model predicts electron density from atomic structures with low linear-scaling cost, while FP-TB-MTO provides an accurate tight-binding representation of the system Hamiltonian. To achieve high-fidelity ML density for complex devices with interfaces and disorder, we train an equivariant graph convolutional network to predict Δ ρ-the difference between DFT and atomic superposition densities-under a charge neutrality constraint required for inhomogeneous systems. A secondary ML residual charge model compensates for errors in the primary Δ ρ model. The predicted density is then used within the full-relativistic FP-TB-MTO framework combined with the non-equilibrium Green's function method. We validate our method on bulk HgCdTe disordered alloys (including vacancy defects) and Au/HgCdTe/Au disordered devices. The maximum band energy error is below 10 meV for bulk HgCdTe. For devices, the charge neutrality constraint suppresses long-wavelength charge bias that would otherwise distort the electrostatic potential by 0.68-1.88 eV near the device center, while residual compensation corrects the remaining 0.16-0.46 eV potential mismatch, essential for reproducing the correct potential barrier profile. Together, these corrections reproduce the layer-resolved potential, including interface steps and bulk-like plateaus, within 0.1 eV of fully self-consistent DFT. Finally, we demonstrate quantum transport in a 11220-atom infrared detector. By combining first-principles accuracy with linear-scaling inference, DeltaChargeNet enables large-scale device simulations without the full DFT self-consistent field loop.

CGTransformer：用于晶体生成和逆设计的拓扑感知自回归框架#

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：crystal structure prediction。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

自回归语言模型提供了晶体生成和逆向设计的统一途径，但其性能取决于周期性结构的序列化方式。对称等效且密切相关的晶体可以在基于坐标的序列化下接收不同的原子顺序，从而引入与其结构连接性无关的可变性。在这里，我们提出了 CGTransformer（Crystal Graph Transformer），这是一种自回归生成框架，它通过图嵌入排序策略来解决这一挑战。使用商图拓扑和重心嵌入，元素对称图 (ESG) 将周期性晶体网络转换为拓扑引导的一维序列，保留关键的局部和对称相关的结构信息。 CGTransformer 在比较的 MP-20 基线中实现了高生成有效性和最高的单次晶体结构预测 (CSP) 匹配率 (63.24%)，而相同的主干比较表明，与基于坐标的排序相比，结构恢复得到了改善。此外，我们还演示了宽带隙半导体的属性条件逆向设计，其中 ESG 实现了比基于坐标的基线更高的稳定、独特和新颖 (S.U.N.) 率以及更强的目标属性对齐。这些结果将拓扑引导的序列构建确立为在固定模型容量下改进晶体生成和属性导向设计的有效途径。

English Abstract

Autoregressive language models offer a unified route to crystal generation and inverse design, but their performance depends on how periodic structures are serialized. Symmetry equivalent and closely related crystals can receive different atomic orders under coordinate-based serialization, introducing variability unrelated to their structural connectivity. Here, we present CGTransformer (Crystal Graph Transformer), an autoregressive generative framework that addresses this challenge via a graph-embedded sorting strategy. Using quotientgraph topology and barycentric embeddings, Element Symmetry Graph (ESG) converts periodic crystal networks into topology-guided one-dimensional sequences that retain key local and symmetry-related structural information. CGTransformer achieves high generation validity and the highest single shot crystal structure prediction (CSP) match rate among the compared MP-20 baselines (63.24%), while same backbone comparisons show improved structure recovery over coordinate-based ordering. Furthermore, we demonstrate property-conditioned inverse design for wide-bandgap semiconductors, where ESG achieves a much higher stable, unique and novel (S.U.N.) rate and stronger target-property alignment than the coordinate-based baseline. These results establish topology-guided sequence construction as an effective route to improving crystal generation and property-directed design at fixed model capacity.

通过随机实时动力学实现高精度神经网络量子态#

原题：High-Accuracy Neural-Network Quantum States via Randomized Real-Time Dynamics
作者：John M. Martyn、Rod Rofougaran、Samuel Stein、Di Luo。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-20

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：神经网络量子态（NQS）在能量最小化过程中表现出收敛到基态的差异：虽然能量随变分波函数的误差二次收敛，但其他可观测量仅线性收敛，这限制了物理预测的准确性。在这里，我们展示了如何通过引入一种提高 NQS 准确性的新算法来缩小这一差距。我们的方法称为动态平均，通过对实时演变的状态（或可观察的）进行平均来估计期望值。利用量子信息的见解，我们证明了任意基态可观测量中误差的高达二次抑制，从而匹配能量的快速收敛。重要的是，这种提高的精度既不需要进一…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

神经网络量子态（NQS）在能量最小化过程中表现出收敛到基态的差异：虽然能量随变分波函数的误差二次收敛，但其他可观测量仅线性收敛，这限制了物理预测的准确性。在这里，我们展示了如何通过引入一种提高 NQS 准确性的新算法来缩小这一差距。我们的方法称为动态平均，通过对实时演变的状态（或可观察的）进行平均来估计期望值。利用量子信息的见解，我们证明了任意基态可观测量中误差的高达二次抑制，从而匹配能量的快速收敛。重要的是，这种提高的精度既不需要进一步的能量最小化，也不需要增加模型的复杂性，而是利用随机化对量子态的影响。我们在不可积自旋模型上演示了动态平均，它加速了收敛并大大减少了可观测值的误差。

English Abstract

Neural-network quantum states (NQSs) exhibit a disparity in converging to the ground state during energy minimization: while the energy converges quadratically with the error in the variational wave function, other observables converge only linearly, which limits the accuracy of physical predictions. Here we show how to close this gap by introducing a new algorithm that improves the accuracy of NQSs. Our method, termed dynamical averaging, estimates expectation values by averaging over a state (or observable) evolved in real-time. Using insights from quantum information, we prove an up-to-quadratic suppression of error in arbitrary ground state observables, thus matching the fast convergence of the energy. Importantly, this improved accuracy requires neither further energy minimization nor increased model complexity, but rather exploits the effect of randomization on quantum states. We demonstrate dynamical averaging on non-integrable spin models, where it accelerates convergence and substantially reduces errors in observables.

强朗道能级混合下的非阿贝尔量子霍尔态：使用第一性原理人工智能寻找任意子#

作者：Pierre-Antoine Graham、Ahmed Abouelkomsan、Liang Fu。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-20

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：非阿贝尔任意子是构成容错拓扑量子计算基础的奇异粒子，据预测，它们会在偶数分母填充因子的分数量子霍尔态中出现。事实证明，确定实现了哪种拓扑序是一个具有挑战性的问题，需要对真实的朗道能级混合进行准确的非微扰处理，而这迄今为止在理论研究中尚付阙如。本文首次提出了非阿贝尔量子霍尔问题的完整解，该解包括所有电子和所有朗道能级，适用于填充因子ν = 3/2的自旋极化电子。我们开发了一种动量分辨的神经网络变分波函数，该波函数仅在微观哈密顿量上进行优化…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

非阿贝尔任意子是构成容错拓扑量子计算基础的奇异粒子，据预测，它们会在偶数分母填充因子的分数量子霍尔态中出现。事实证明，确定实现了哪种拓扑序是一个具有挑战性的问题，需要对真实的朗道能级混合进行准确的非微扰处理，而这迄今为止在理论研究中尚付阙如。本文首次提出了非阿贝尔量子霍尔问题的完整解，该解包括所有电子和所有朗道能级，适用于填充因子ν = 3/2的自旋极化电子。我们开发了一种动量分辨的神经网络变分波函数，该波函数仅在微观哈密顿量上进行优化，从而可以直接获得拓扑简并基态。在没有任何关于预期结果的物理学输入的情况下，它发现了一个简并基态流形，其纠缠揭示了具有量子维数sqrt2的任意子，这是伊辛型非阿贝尔序的标志。通过直接计算与试探波函数的重叠，我们进一步表明，朗道能级混合更倾向于反普法夫态，而非其他候选态。我们的结果表明，在强朗道能级混合的条件下，神经网络波函数在从第一性原理出发寻找和表征非阿贝尔任意子方面具有强大的能力，这与许多近期实验相关。

English Abstract

Non-Abelian anyons, strange particles forming the basis of fault-tolerant topological quantum computing, are predicted to emerge in fractional quantum Hall states at even-denominator filling factors. Identifying which topological order is realized has proven to be a challenging problem that requires an accurate, non-perturbative treatment of realistic Landau level mixing, which has so far been missing from theoretical studies. Here we present the first complete solution of non-Abelian quantum Hall problem that includes all electrons and all Landau levels, for spin-polarized electrons at filling factor ν = 3/2. We develop a momentum-resolved neural-network variational wave function, optimized solely on the microscopic Hamiltonian, which gives direct access to the topologically degenerate ground states. Without any physics input about the expected outcome, it discovers a degenerate ground-state manifold whose entanglement reveals anyons with quantum dimension sqrt2, the hallmark of Ising-type non-Abelian order. By directly computing the overlap with trial wave functions, we further show that Landau level mixing favors the anti-Pfaffian over other candidate states. Our results demonstrate the power of neural-network wave functions in finding and characterizing non-Abelian anyons from first principles, in regimes of strong Landau level mixing, relevant to many recent experiments.

Magflow：工业级 6D 磁悬浮的可微分模拟#

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

六自由度磁悬浮原则上可以实现无摩擦运输和微米级定位，但由于其复杂且不稳定的动力学，低级控制本身就具有挑战性。工业系统通常依赖于深度的硬件-软件协同设计，其中动子和定子的几何形状被联合设计，以实现电磁力计算的紧凑、手工近似，从而保持控制在千赫兹速率下的计算易处理性。然而，这种方法对操作工作空间和支持的移动器拓扑范围施加了过于保守的限制。我们没有将底层物理简化为手工制作的近似值，而是直接计算物理。我们推出 Magflow，一种用于 6D 磁悬浮的完全可微分、GPU 并行模拟器。它用于训练工业级 6D 磁悬浮系统的第一个神经控制器，该系统纯粹是从模拟物理中学习的。我们展示了学习的神经控制器将零样本从模拟转移到真实硬件，同时满足 4 kHz 的硬实时约束。学习后的控制器实现了新的行为，甚至悬浮了一个不存在手工解决方案的移动器拓扑。这些结果表明，协同设计并不是 6D 磁悬浮控制的物理必要性。更广泛地说，从模拟物理中进行控制学习可以在要求苛刻的工业领域找到超出控制工程范围的解决方案。源代码和演示视频可在 https://sites.google.com/view/magflow 上公开获取。

English Abstract

Magnetic levitation with six-degrees-of-freedom enables frictionless transport and micrometer-scale positioning in principle, but low-level control is inherently challenging due to its complex and unstable dynamics. Industrial systems typically rely on deep hardware-software co-design, where mover and stator geometries are jointly designed to enable a compact, hand-crafted approximation of the electromagnetic force computation, thereby keeping control computationally tractable at kilohertz rates. However, this approach imposes overly conservative constraints on the operating workspace and the range of supported mover topologies. Instead of reducing the underlying physics to a hand-crafted approximation, we compute the physics directly. We introduce Magflow, a fully differentiable, GPU-parallel simulator for 6D magnetic levitation. It is used to train the first neural controller for an industrial-grade 6D magnetic levitation system that is learned purely from simulated physics. We show that the learned neural controller transfers zero-shot from simulation to real hardware while satisfying hard real-time constraints at 4 kHz. The learned controller achieves qualitatively new behaviors and even levitates a mover topology for which no hand-crafted solution exists. These results demonstrate that co-design is not a physical necessity for 6D magnetic levitation control. More broadly, control learning from simulated physics can find solutions beyond the reach of control engineering in demanding industrial domains. Source code and demonstration videos are publicly available at https://sites.google.com/view/magflow.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了一个数据驱动的框架，用于使用库普曼算子分析和动态模式分解（Koopman-DMD）重建能带结构。该方法不是从显式哈密顿量导出光谱，而是直接从时空数据（包括波函数和可观测量）重建能带色散和模态动力学。该框架在 Hamiltonian Floquet-Bloch 分解和 Koopman-DMD 之间建立了对应关系，由此提取的 DMD 模式对频率、衰减或增长率、空间剖面和投影权重进行编码。这些量允许重建谱函数、局域态密度以及离域到局域的度量，例如逆参与比。此外，这些扩展的 DMD 模式还可以推断量子几何和拓扑特性，包括量子度量、贝里曲率和几何相位。原型一维和二维紧束缚模型的应用，包括无序 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger 模型及其 Floquet 和非 Hermitian 变体、石墨烯和 Haldane 模型，表明 Koopman-DMD 为凝聚态、光子学和相关领域的波传播、局域化和拓扑相的数据驱动分析提供了统一的途径。

English Abstract

We present a data-driven framework for reconstructing band structures using Koopman operator analysis and dynamic mode decomposition (Koopman-DMD). Instead of deriving spectra from an explicit Hamiltonian, the approach reconstructs band dispersion and modal dynamics directly from spatiotemporal data, including wavefunctions and observables. This framework establishes a correspondence between Hamiltonian Floquet-Bloch decomposition and Koopman-DMD, whereby the extracted DMD modes encode frequencies, decay or growth rates, spatial profiles and projection weights. These quantities allow the reconstruction of spectral functions, local density of states, and delocalized-to-localized measures such as the inverse participation ratio. Also, these extended DMD modes enable inference of quantum-geometric and topological properties, including the quantum metric, Berry curvature and geometric phases. Applications to prototypical one- and two-dimensional tight-binding models, including disordered Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model and its Floquet and non-Hermitian variants, graphene and Haldane models, demonstrate that Koopman-DMD provides a unified route for the data-driven analysis of wave propagation, localization, and topological phases in condensed matter, photonics, and related fields.

原题：Learning Spin Hamiltonians from Interacting Magnon Spectra
作者：Ang-Kun Wu、David A. Dahlbom、Kipton Barros、Cristian D. Batista、Hao Zhang。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-20

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

学习逆映射的神经网络继承了用于生成其训练数据的正向求解器的近似值。我们通过合成中子散射谱的自旋哈密顿量推理证明了这一点，并展示了快速多体求解器如何克服线性自旋波理论（LSWT）的局限性。我们考虑在略高于饱和度的场上的一系列各向异性三角晶格自旋哈密顿量，其中磁振子相互作用以 LSWT 无法捕获的方式移动并拓宽光谱。使用截断希尔伯特空间阶梯（THL）方法，我们在一磁振子空间和二磁振子空间内非微扰地计算动态自旋结构因子（DSSF）。 THL 足够快，可以生成大量 DSSF 谱训练集，使我们能够训练准确预测哈密顿参数的神经网络。通过对几种神经网络架构进行实证基准测试，我们发现一维卷积网络表现最好。然后，学习的前向解码器根据预测参数重建 DSSF，提供快速的自我一致性检查和预测响应的可视化。当相互作用很强时，在 LSWT 和 THL 谱上训练的网络会推断出明显不同的参数。 Fisher 信息矩阵的分析表明，测量协议（包括解析哪些自旋相关分量和动量切割）决定了可以识别哪些耦合。因此，将机器学习与快速多体理论相结合对于从散射数据进行定量哈密顿推理至关重要。

English Abstract

A neural network that learns an inverse map inherits the approximations of the forward solver used to generate its training data. We demonstrate this in the inference of spin Hamiltonians from synthetic neutron-scattering spectra and show how a fast many-body solver can overcome the limitation of linear spin-wave theory (LSWT). We consider a family of anisotropic triangular-lattice spin Hamiltonians at fields just above saturation, where magnon interactions shift and broaden the spectrum in ways that LSWT cannot capture. Using the truncated Hilbert-space ladder (THL) method, we compute the dynamical spin structure factor (DSSF) nonperturbatively within the one- and two-magnon space. THL is fast enough to generate large training sets of DSSF spectra, enabling us to train neural networks that accurately predict Hamiltonian parameters. By empirically benchmarking several neural-network architectures, we find that a one-dimensional convolutional network performs best. A learned forward decoder then reconstructs the DSSF from the predicted parameters, providing a rapid self-consistency check and a visualization of the predicted response. When interactions are strong, networks trained on LSWT and THL spectra infer markedly different parameters. Analysis of the Fisher information matrix shows that the measurement protocol, including which spin-correlation components are resolved and the momentum cut, determines which couplings can be identified. Combining machine learning with fast many- body theory is therefore essential for quantitative Hamiltonian inference from scattering data.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

管理横向二维过渡金属二硫属化物 (TMD) 异质结构中界面的热流是下一代纳米电子和光电器件的关键挑战。为了解决这个问题，我们使用 DeePMD-kit 在密度泛函理论 (DFT) 数据上训练了深度学习原子间势 (DLP)，然后将其与非平衡分子动力学 (NEMD) 模拟配对，以研究横向 MoSe2/MoS2 异质结构中的界面热导 (ITC)，同时考虑扶手椅和锯齿形界面方向。我们训练的势与 DFT 能量、力和声子色散关系密切匹配，这证实了其热传输模拟的可靠性。检查从 10 到 40 nm 的电池长度并推断到无限长度，扶手椅界面显示出比之字形界面 (387 MW/m²K) 更高的 ITC (473 MW/m²K)，并且扶手椅界面在考虑的每个长度上都保持较高。随着温度升高，两者都会进一步增加。在 300 K 时，状态的振动密度在扶手椅界面处显示出比锯齿形界面处更大的声子谱重叠。总之，这些结果为研究横向 TMD 异质结构中的热传输提供了坚实的机器学习框架，并将界面方向确定为控制这些结处散热的有效手段。

English Abstract

Managing heat flow across interfaces in lateral 2D transition-metal dichalcogenide (TMD) heterostructures is a key challenge for next-generation nanoelectronic and optoelectronic devices. To tackle this, we trained a deep learning interatomic potential (DLP) using DeePMD-kit on density functional theory (DFT) data, then paired it with non-equilibrium molecular dynamics (NEMD) simulations to study interfacial thermal conductance (ITC) in lateral MoSe₂/MoS₂ heterostructures, considering both armchair and zigzag interface orientations. The potential we trained closely matches DFT energies, forces, and phonon dispersion relations, which confirms its reliability for thermal transport simulations. Examining cell lengths from 10 to 40 nm and extrapolating to infinite length, the armchair interface shows a higher ITC (473 MW/m²K) than the zigzag interface (387 MW/m²K), and the armchair interface remains higher at every length considered. Both increase further as temperature rises. At 300 K, the vibrational density of states shows greater phonon spectral overlap at the armchair interface than at the zigzag interface. Together, these results offer a solid machine-learning framework for studying heat transport in lateral TMD heterostructures, and identify interface orientation as an effective means of controlling heat dissipation at these junctions.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在高熵钙钛矿氧化物 (HEPO) 的计算研究中，奇数周期超级晶胞（例如 3 × 3 × 5）通常用于等摩尔五组分组合物。然而，已知此类单元会限制八面体倾斜，因为它们无法适应所有标准倾斜模式（Glazer 倾斜系统）。这种限制对 HEPO 的影响有多大，其八面体对局部阳离子环境有反应，目前尚不清楚。在这里，我们表明超晶胞周期性系统地偏置计算的八面体倾斜和能量学。使用机器学习原子间势，我们比较了 13 个 HEPO 的奇数周期和倾斜相当细胞。即使在构型平均之后，奇数周期电池也会产生更小、更不相干的倾斜和更高的能量。在约 1,700 个原子的细胞中，所有成分中都存在倾斜差异，大多数成分中都存在能量差异。这种偏差延伸到了缺陷，奇数周期晶胞中强烈倾斜的成分具有较低的氧空位形成能。基于这些发现，提出了一种简单的等摩尔超晶胞设计规则来解除这一限制。

English Abstract

Odd-period supercells such as 3 × 3 × 5 are often used for equimolar five-component compositions in computational studies of high-entropy perovskite oxides (HEPOs). However, such cells are known to constrain octahedral tilting because they cannot accommodate all standard tilt patterns (Glazer tilt systems). How strongly this constraint affects HEPOs, whose octahedra respond to local cation environments, remains unclear. Here we show that supercell periodicity systematically biases computed octahedral tilting and energetics. Using a machine-learning interatomic potential, we compared odd-period and tilt-commensurate cells for 13 HEPOs. Odd-period cells yielded smaller and less coherent tilts and higher energies, even after configurational averaging. In ~1,700-atom cells, tilt differences persisted in all compositions and energy differences in most. The bias extended to defects, with lower oxygen vacancy formation energies in odd-period cells for strongly tilted compositions. Based on these findings, a simple design rule for equimolar supercells is proposed to lift this constraint.

中文摘要

有效发现前体添加剂对于提高钙钛矿太阳能电池的性能至关重要，但巨大的化学空间使得传统的试错筛选效率低下。在这里，我们展示了 LEAP（LLM 驱动的钙钛矿主动学习探索），这是一个将领域专业语言模型与主动学习相结合的闭环框架，在低数据条件下优先考虑钙钛矿前体添加剂。该模型从文献中提取与机制相关的知识，并将其转换为可解释的分子描述符，以进行不确定性贝叶斯优化。根据从未见过的文献进行的评估，专用模型比经过测试的通用模型提供了更多机制一致的答案。在专家指导的概念验证研究中，两种后轮添加剂将平均器件功率转换效率提高到 20.13% 和 20.87%，而对照组为 19.25%，最好的器件达到 21.32%。由于只有有限数量的候选者进行了前瞻性测试，这些结果提供了初步证据，表明基于文献的分子表征、贝叶斯优化和专家评估可以支持钙钛矿太阳能电池的添加剂发现。

English Abstract

Efficient discovery of precursor additives is essential for improving the performance of perovskite solar cells, yet the large chemical space makes conventional trial-and-error screening inefficient. Here we show that LEAP (LLM-driven Exploration via Active Learning for Perovskites), a closed-loop framework combining a domain-specialized language model with active learning, prioritizes perovskite precursor additives under low-data conditions. The model extracts mechanism-relevant knowledge from the literature and converts it into interpretable molecular descriptors for uncertainty-aware Bayesian optimization. On an evaluation drawn from unseen literature, the specialized model provides more mechanism-consistent answers than the tested general-purpose models. In an expert-guided proof-of-concept study, two later-round additives increase average device power conversion efficiencies to 20.13% and 20.87%, compared with 19.25% for the control, and the best device reaches 21.32%. Because only a limited number of candidates are tested prospectively, these results provide preliminary evidence that literature-grounded molecular representations, Bayesian optimization, and expert assessment can support additive discovery for perovskite solar cells.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

圆形电子正电子对撞机电磁热量计的提案是一种高颗粒闪烁晶体设计，它使用硅光电倍增管来测量光子的物理信号。探索了使用深度和传统机器学习开发粒子识别方法的潜力。人们发现一种对比机器学习技术可以在没有明确标签的情况下分离喷淋和“穿通”介子。然后使用结果训练监督粒子识别方法，使用图神经网络和增强决策树来区分电子、簇射强子和最小电离粒子类事件。在这两种情况下，分类器的接收器下面积工作特性曲线均为 0.996。 Shapley 附加解释分析表明，增强型决策树分类器符合每种事件类型的预期。然后，粒子识别能力通过来自 CERN 质子同步加速器 T9 光束线的实验数据得到证明。

English Abstract

A proposal for the Circular Electron Positron Collider electromagnetic calorimeter is a highly granular scintillating crystal design that uses silicon photomultipliers to measure physics signals from photons. The potential to develop particle identification methods using deep and traditional machine learning is explored. A contrastive machine learning technique was found to enable the separation of showering and 'punch-through' pions without explicit labels. A supervised particle identification method was then trained using the results, using both a graph neural network and a boosted decision tree to distinguish electron, showering hadron, and minimum-ionizing-particle-like events. The classifier achieved an area-under-the-receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.996 for both cases. A Shapley additive explanations analysis showed the boosted decision tree classifier matched expectations for each event type. The particle identification capability is then demonstrated on experimental data taken from the CERN Proton Synchrotron T9 beamline.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们介绍了一种使用神经网络量子态求解局部有序量子自旋系统的方法。首先，我们的方法依赖于选择由经典或量子参考态的磁性纹理构造的自旋基础。其次，该方法通过适应变分状态流形的量子态几何的正则黎曼梯度下降来提高收敛性并抑制参数空间漂移。结合这些技术，可以对具有 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用的二维和三维手性自旋系统进行稳定的基态计算。由此产生的变分基态能量与高保真密度矩阵重整化群计算的那些具有竞争力，并且对于周期性和层状几何形状，提供了显着更严格的基态能量上限。

English Abstract

We introduce an approach to solving locally ordered quantum spin systems using neural network quantum states. First, our approach rests on choosing a spin basis constructed from the magnetic texture of a classical or quantum reference state. Second, the method improves convergence and suppresses parameter-space drift through a regularized Riemannian gradient descent adapted to the quantum state geometry of the variational state manifold. Combining these techniques enables stable ground-state calculations for two- and three-dimensional chiral spin systems with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions. The resulting variational ground-state energies are competitive with those of high-fidelity density matrix renormalization group calculations and, for periodic and layered geometries, provide significantly tighter upper bounds on the ground-state energy.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：artificial neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

CuO2 基高温超导性的一个长期谜团是时间反转 (T) 对称性破缺的机制和后果。尽管理论假设集中于持续电子电流作为潜在原因，但这些对于直接实验探针来说是不可见的。最近，原子级超电流可视化技术的发展打破了这一僵局。在这里，通过对 Bi2Sr2CaCu2O₈ 中电子态 N(r,E) 的能量分辨密度进行成像，我们寻找无序 d 波超导体中流动超电流的特定特征。采用人工神经网络（ANN）进行复杂量子物质可视化的策略，我们设计了双神经网络（VMAP）来有效实现d波超电流可视化，实现磁场|B|下超电流速度V(r)的成像。 ≤ Bi2Sr2CaCu2O₈ 中的 μT。每个超电流回路周围的 N(r,E) 的具体特征与将 N(r,E) 转换为 V(r) 的量子“罗塞塔石碑”之间的对应关系，强调了 V(r) 检测和可视化的有效性。然后，对于空穴密度 0.17 ≤ p ≤ 0.22，我们观察到统计上可重复的超电流网络。至关重要的是，这些超电流在每个网络的交汇处都满足基尔霍夫定律。此外，无序 d 波超导体中超电流速度 V 的理论预测与测量的 1250 m/s < V < 4000 m/s 以及报告的磁场幅度 |B| 一致。约 0.1 mT 因此，这些超电流环路网络的检测和可视化为铜酸盐中 T 对称性破缺提供了独特的视角。它还强调了人工神经网络技术在高数据量量子物质研究中的物理发现的加速应用。

English Abstract

A persistent mystery of CuO₂-based high temperature superconductivity has been the mechanism and consequences of time reversal (T)-symmetry breaking. Although theoretical hypotheses focus upon persistent electronic currents as a potential cause, these have been invisible to direct experimental probes. That impasse has recently been removed by development of techniques for atomic-scale supercurrent visualization. Here, by imaging the energy-resolved density of electronic states N(r,E) in Bi₂Sr₂CaCu₂O₈, we search for the specific signatures of flowing supercurrents in a disordered d-wave superconductor. Employing the strategy of artificial neural networks (ANN) for complex quantum matter visualization, we designed a dual-ANN (VMAP) to efficiently implement d-wave supercurrent visualization, achieving imaging of supercurrent speeds V(r) at magnetic fields |B| ≤ μ T in Bi₂Sr₂CaCu₂O₈. Correspondence between specific characteristics of N(r,E) around each supercurrent loop and a quantum "Rosetta Stone" which translates N(r,E) to V(r), emphasizes the validity of V(r) detection and visualization. Then, for hole-densities 0.17 ≤ p ≤ 0.22 we observe statistically repeatable networks of supercurrent flows. Crucially, these supercurrents satisfy Kirchhoff's law at junctions within each network. Further, theoretical predictions for supercurrent speeds V in disordered d-wave superconductors are consistent with measured 1250 m/s < V < 4000 m/s, and with reported magnetic field amplitudes |B| approx 0.1 mT Thus, detection and visualization of these supercurrent loop networks provides unique perspectives on T-symmetry breaking in cuprates. It also emphasizes the accelerating utility of ANN techniques for physics discovery from high data volume quantum matter research.

基础张量网络在量子晶格系统中推广#

原题：Foundation Tensor Networks Generalize Across Quantum Lattice Systems
作者：Ryan T. Grimm、Michael Lindsey。arXiv · physics，2026-11-16

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

神经量子态和张量网络已成为模拟量子多体系统的领先技术。然而，无序系统在这两个框架中仍然极具挑战性，因为它们需要为无序的每次实现计算基态。最近，神经量子态已被用来构建直接从哈密顿量生成基态的基础模型。尽管如此，他们仍然受到需要进行大量采样来估计可观测值的阻碍。为了解决这些困难，我们引入了基础张量网络（FTN），它使用神经网络从哈密顿量生成基态波函数的张量网络表示。为了加速训练，我们开发了一种名为 textttGridBP 的技术，该技术基于迭代一维置信传播，可将计算能量期望值的成本降低到仅两次张量收缩。我们使用 FTN 通过有限尺寸尺度分析来计算量子 Edwards-Anderson 模型中玻璃化转变的关键参数。为了促进快速平均，我们使用矩生成函数来评估阶参数，从而降低单个张量收缩的成本。我们通过对超过 500,000 个无序实现进行平均，获得了关键参数的高保真度估计。

English Abstract

Neural quantum states and tensor networks have emerged as leading techniques for simulating quantum many-body systems. However, disordered systems remain extremely challenging in both frameworks as they require computation of the ground state for every realization of the disorder. Recently, neural quantum states have been used to construct foundation models that generate the ground state directly from the Hamiltonian. Still, they are hindered by the need to perform extensive sampling to estimate observables. To address these difficulties, we introduce the foundation tensor network (FTN), which uses a neural network to generate the tensor network representation of the ground-state wavefunction from the Hamiltonian. To accelerate training, we develop a technique, called textttGridBP, based on iterated one-dimensional belief propagation that reduces the cost of computing the energy expectation value to just two tensor contractions. We use FTNs to calculate the critical parameters of the glass transition in the quantum Edwards-Anderson model via finite-size scaling analysis. To facilitate rapid averaging, we use a moment-generating function to evaluate the order parameter, lowering the cost to a single tensor contraction. We obtain high-fidelity estimates for the critical parameters by averaging over 500,000 disorder realizations.

中文摘要

扩展通用量子计算并使用受保护的逻辑量子位实现量子机器学习仍然具有挑战性。为了利用现有容错量子平台的计算潜力，我们引入了拓扑量子库计算，这是一种利用受保护的存储自由度和主机系统的内在动态进行信息处理的新颖方法。使用一维 Kitaev 链，我们展示了长寿命的马约拉纳边缘模式和快速批量响应如何在同一多体系统中支持这些互补功能。我们确定了管理对受保护内存的访问的权衡，并展示了匹配的接口如何实现可编程写入和交互，从而实现从大量到边缘的自主信息整合。这些机制提高了需要长期保留时间信息的任务的性能。我们的结果将拓扑确立为量子库计算的新资源，并为近期量子计算和量子机器学习开辟了道路。

English Abstract

Scaling universal quantum computation and implementing quantum machine learning with protected logical qubits remain challenging. To harness the computational potential of existing fault-tolerant quantum platforms, we introduce topological quantum reservoir computing, a novel approach using protected degrees of freedom for storage and the host system's intrinsic dynamics for information processing. Using a one-dimensional Kitaev chain, we show how long-lived Majorana edge modes and fast bulk responses support these complementary functions within the same many-body system. We identify the tradeoffs governing access to protected memory and show how matched interfaces enable programmable writing and interactions enable autonomous information consolidation from the bulk to the edge. These mechanisms improve performance on tasks requiring long-term retention of temporal information. Our results establish topology as a new resource for quantum reservoir computing and open a route toward near-term quantum computing and quantum machine learning.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

了解化学反应性需要揭示一组给定反应物可进入哪些途径、它们如何竞争以及连续反应如何重塑混合物。自动鞍点搜索可以仅从反应物中进行探索，但随着连续步骤中竞争路径的积累，其计算成本急剧上升。生成过渡状态的机器学习方法通常需要预定义的产品，但识别这些产品是探索的中心目标。在这里，我们介绍 ReactNexus，这是一个生成框架，可以直接从反应物图生成过渡态候选，而不指定产物，预测它们连接的物种，并利用反应性机器学习潜力来细化路径来指导网络探索。其模型在量子化学数据库上进行训练，该数据库包含来自 110 万条反应记录的 1.3 亿个结构，涵盖 11 种元素以及多种电荷和自旋态。在保留反应中，93.6% 的生成候选在密度泛函理论水平上精炼为有效的过渡态，83.3% 将提供的反应物与不同的产物连接起来。通过分解、复杂重排和有机催化，ReactNexus 揭示了竞争机制如何塑造产物形成，揭示了先前研究中遗漏的途径。在多位点亲核芳香取代中，ReactNexus 鉴定了二芳基化产物，这些产物解释了最初未分配的 LC-MS 信号，分离后的结构分配得到了 NMR 的支持。 ReactNexus 还预测了在初始条件下未检测到的其他产品系列；随后的高温实验提供了支持其形成的 LC-MS 证据。这项工作强调了生成模型将机械分析扩展到日益复杂的反应混合物的潜力。

English Abstract

Understanding chemical reactivity requires uncovering which pathways are accessible from a given set of reactants, how they compete, and how successive reactions reshape the mixture. Automated saddle-point searches can explore from reactants alone, but their computational cost rises sharply as competing pathways accumulate across successive steps. Machine-learning approaches to transition state generation typically require predefined products, yet identifying those products is a central goal of exploration. Here we introduce ReactNexus, a generative framework that generates transition-state candidates directly from reactant graphs without specifying products, predicts the species they connect, and refines pathways with a reactive machine-learned potential to guide network exploration. Its models were trained on a quantum-chemical database of 130 million structures from 1.1 million reaction records, spanning 11 elements and multiple charge and spin states. On held-out reactions, 93.6% of generated candidates refined to valid transition states at the density functional theory level, and 83.3% connected the supplied reactants to distinct products. Across decomposition, complex rearrangements, and organocatalysis, ReactNexus reveals how competing mechanisms shape product formation, uncovering pathways missed in previous studies. In a multisite nucleophilic aromatic substitution, ReactNexus identified diarylation products that explained initially unassigned LC-MS signals, with structural assignments supported by NMR after isolation. ReactNexus also predicted an additional product family not detected under the initial conditions; subsequent higher-temperature experiments provided LC-MS evidence supporting its formation. This work highlights the potential of generative models to extend mechanistic analysis to increasingly complex reaction mixtures.

用于碰撞等离子体不确定性量化的微观-宏观张量神经替代物#

原题：Micro-Macro Tensor Neural Surrogates for Uncertainty Quantification in Collisional Plasma

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

等离子体动力学方程对模型参数和数据中的微观扰动表现出明显的敏感性，这使得可靠且高效的不确定性量化 (UQ) 对于预测模拟至关重要。然而，不确定性采样的成本、高维相空间和多尺度刚度对传统数值方法的计算效率和误差控制提出了严峻的挑战。在存在碰撞的情况下，这些方面进一步得到强调，其中高维非局部碰撞积分和守恒特性构成了严格的限制。为了克服这个问题，我们在 Vlasov-Poisson-Landau (VPL) 系统中为昆士兰大学提出了一种方差减少的蒙特卡罗框架，其中神经网络代理取代了 Landau 碰撞项的多重昂贵评估。该方法将高保真、渐近保持的 VPL 求解器与基于 Vlasov-Poisson-Fokker-Planck (VPFP) 和 Euler-Poisson (EP) 方程的廉价、强相关代理耦合起来。对于替代模型，我们引入了可分离物理信息神经网络（SPINN）的推广，开发了一类基于各向异性微观-宏观分解的张量神经网络，以降低速度力矩成本、模型复杂性和维数灾难。为了进一步增加与 VPL 的相关性，我们校准了 VPFP 模型并设计了一个渐近保持的 SPINN，其小克努森极限和大克努森极限分别恢复了 EP 和 VP 系统。数值实验表明，与标准蒙特卡罗相比，方差显着减少，高保真样本数量少得多，统计结果准确，挂钟时间更短，同时保持对随机维度的鲁棒性。

English Abstract

Plasma kinetic equations exhibit pronounced sensitivity to microscopic perturbations in model parameters and data, making reliable and efficient uncertainty quantification (UQ) essential for predictive simulations. However, the cost of uncertainty sampling, the high-dimensional phase space, and multiscale stiffness pose severe challenges to both computational efficiency and error control in traditional numerical methods. These aspects are further emphasized in presence of collisions where the high-dimensional nonlocal collision integrations and conservation properties pose severe constraints. To overcome this, we present a variance-reduced Monte Carlo framework for UQ in the Vlasov--Poisson--Landau (VPL) system, in which neural network surrogates replace the multiple costly evaluations of the Landau collision term. The method couples a high-fidelity, asymptotic-preserving VPL solver with inexpensive, strongly correlated surrogates based on the Vlasov--Poisson--Fokker--Planck (VPFP) and Euler--Poisson (EP) equations. For the surrogate models, we introduce a generalization of the separable physics-informed neural network (SPINN), developing a class of tensor neural networks based on an anisotropic micro-macro decomposition, to reduce velocity-moment costs, model complexity, and the curse of dimensionality. To further increase correlation with VPL, we calibrate the VPFP model and design an asymptotic-preserving SPINN whose small- and large-Knudsen limits recover the EP and VP systems, respectively. Numerical experiments show substantial variance reduction over standard Monte Carlo, accurate statistics with far fewer high-fidelity samples, and lower wall-clock time, while maintaining robustness to stochastic dimension.

从功率到光谱：现成的光电二极管仅通过其偏置就变成了光谱仪#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

计算光谱仪用光谱响应可以变化的探测器取代色散光学，但每个报道的单探测器设备都通过专用制造获得这种响应：二维异质结构、分级外延结、有机堆栈、滤波器或光子电路。我们展示了一种通用的、免制造的替代方案，其中标准商用光电二极管仅通过其电力驱动即可成为光谱仪，无需修改设备，无需滤光片，也无需对光进行调制。其机制是偏置调制光电色散（OED）：不同波长的光在不同深度被吸收，正弦偏置改变了每个深度产生的载流子的收集方式，以便每个光谱在一个偏置周期内被编码为光电流波形的形状。应用于室温下未修改的 InGaAs PIN 光电二极管时，由神经网络解码的 10 us 波形可重建电信 C+L 波段 80 nm 上的一到三个激光线的光谱，将线定位到 0.3-0.4 nm，在大约 99% 的测量中对它们进行计数并识别其功率水平，线位移跟踪到数十皮米。由于 OED 依赖于通用半导体传输物理学，因此该方法与材料无关，并且原则上适用于任何现成的光电二极管，从而将已部署为功率监视器的探测器转变为低成本光谱传感器。

English Abstract

Computational spectrometers replace dispersive optics with detectors whose spectral response can be varied, but every reported single-detector device obtains this response through dedicated fabrication: two-dimensional heterostructures, graded epitaxial junctions, organic stacks, filters or photonic circuits. We demonstrate a generic, fabrication-free alternative in which a standard commercial photodiode becomes a spectrometer through its electrical drive alone, requiring no modification of the device, no optical filter and no modulation of the light. The mechanism is bias-modulated optoelectronic chromatic dispersion (OED): light of different wavelengths is absorbed at different depths, and a sinusoidal bias changes how carriers generated at each depth are collected, so that every spectrum is encoded in the shape of the photocurrent waveform over one bias period. Applied to an unmodified InGaAs PIN photodiode at room temperature, a 10 us waveform decoded by a neural network reconstructs spectra of one to three laser lines across the 80 nm of the telecom C+L band, locating the lines to 0.3-0.4 nm, counting them in about 99% of measurements and identifying their power levels, with line shifts tracked to tens of picometres. Because OED relies on universal semiconductor transport physics, the approach is material-agnostic and applies in principle to any off-the-shelf photodiode, turning detectors already deployed as power monitors into low-cost spectral sensors.

中文摘要

反应堆电子反中微子通常通过逆β衰变相互作用来检测，先是瞬发正电子，然后是延迟中子俘获。在浅层或地上分段有机闪烁体探测器中，相关快中子是主要背景，因为质子反冲可以模拟瞬发 IBD 信号。我们使用用于反应堆反中微子探测器的印度闪烁体矩阵作为案例研究来研究基于拓扑的机器学习。多层感知器根据详细的 Geant4 模拟重建的拓扑可观测量进行训练。 mathrmF₁ 分数和独立验证样本上的 bootstrap 重采样产生 PIBD>0.40 的固定阈值。在不进一步优化独立测试样本的情况下，MLP 保留 (83.4± 0.7)% 的 IBD 事件并拒绝 (77.3± 0.7)% 的快中子事件。在使用独立 AmBe 快中子能谱的受控能谱基准中，该选择将净候选能谱变形减少了 84%。随后将能量相关信号接受度和残余快中子分量合并到分箱似然中，将注入的 IBD 信号强度恢复为 mathrmhatα=0.993⁺⁰.⁰53₋₀.₀₈，经验拟合优度概率为 0.434。 ISMRAN 探测器的预期无菌中微子灵敏度在 13 m 和 18 m 基线处进行评估，每个基线暴露两年。对于标称 pre-MLP S/B=1:2 基准和背景模型，在联合 (mathrmEₚᵣₒₘₚₜ× PIBD) 似然中保留分类器分数可提高 95% C.L.相对于相同快中子污染的纯能量似然，在 0.1≤Δ m₄₁²≤1~eV² 范围内，对 sin²2θ₁₄ 的敏感性对数平均提高 7.9%。

English Abstract

Reactor electron antineutrinos are commonly detected through the inverse beta decay interaction, with a prompt positron followed by delayed neutron capture. In shallow or above-ground segmented organic scintillator detectors, correlated fast neutrons are a dominant background because proton recoils can mimic the prompt IBD signal. We study topology-based machine learning using the Indian Scintillator Matrix for Reactor Anti-Neutrinos detector as a case study. A multilayer perceptron is trained on reconstructed topological observables from detailed Geant4 simulations. The mathrmF₁ score and bootstrap resampling on an independent validation sample yield a fixed threshold of PIBD>0.40. Applied without further optimization to an independent testing sample, the MLP retains (83.4± 0.7)% of IBD events and rejects (77.3± 0.7)% of fast neutron events. In a controlled spectral benchmark using an independent AmBe fast neutron spectrum, the selection reduces the net candidate-spectrum deformation by 84%. The energy dependent signal acceptance and residual fast neutron component are subsequently incorporated into a binned likelihood, which recovers the injected IBD signal strength as mathrmhatα=0.993⁺⁰.⁰⁵³₋₀.₀₄₈, with an empirical goodness-of-fit probability of 0.434. The expected sterile-neutrino sensitivity for the ISMRAN detector is evaluated at baselines of 13 m and 18 m, with two years of exposure at each baseline. For the nominal pre-MLP S/B=1:2 benchmark and the background model, retaining the classifier score in a joint (mathrmEₚᵣₒₘₚₜ× PIBD) likelihood improves the 95% C.L. sensitivity to sin²2θ₁₄ by a logarithmically averaged 7.9% over 0.1≤Δ m₄₁²≤1~eV² relative to an energy-only likelihood with the same fast neutron contamination.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

低压电容耦合等离子体（CCP）中放电模式和加热机制转变的系统动力学研究在计算上是昂贵的，因为粒子在细胞/蒙特卡罗碰撞（PIC/MCC）模拟必须解决固有的慢离子弛豫问题。在这封信中，我们提出了弱电离玻尔兹曼-泊松放电系统的相似性降低的离子动力学表示和机器学习（ML）重建方法。利用放电相似性，三维将由气体压力 p、间隙距离 d 和驱动频率 f 组成的控制参数空间简化为二维相似空间 (pd, fd)。双分支 ML 模型预测参考尺度下的离子密度分布和条件离子速度分布。以 1D3V 氩气放电为范式，我们使用重建的离子动力学状态来初始化 PIC/MCC 模拟，然后在短暂的演化后放松到自洽的周期性稳态。与传统的均匀初始化相比，该方法实现了最大计算加速56.8倍，中值加速27.7倍。此外，复杂非线性放电转变的物理机制，包括α-γ模式转变、局域到非局域电子碰撞加热模式转变和低密度跳跃，都准确地映射在相似空间内。相似变换将这些边界扩展到尺度上，为射频的快速动力学表征和优化开辟了新途径。等离子体源。

English Abstract

Systematic kinetic investigations of discharge-mode and heating-mechanism transitions in low-pressure capacitively coupled plasmas (CCPs) are computationally expensive because particle-in-cell/Monte Carlo collision (PIC/MCC) simulations must resolve the inherently slow ion relaxation.In this Letter, we propose a similarity-reduced ion-kinetic representation and a machine learning (ML) reconstruction method for weakly ionized Boltzmann-Poisson discharge systems.Using discharge similarity, the three-dimensional control parameter space, comprising gas pressure p, gap distance d, and driving frequency f, is reduced into a two-dimensional similarity space (pd, fd).A dual-branch ML model predicts the ion density profile at the reference scale and the conditional ion velocity distribution.Taking a 1D3V argon discharge as the paradigm, we use the reconstructed ion kinetic state to initialize the PIC/MCC simulation, which then relaxes to a self-consistent periodic steady state after only a brief evolution.Compared to conventional uniform initializations, this approach achieves a maximum computational speedup of 56.8 times and a median speedup of 27.7 times.Furthermore, the physical regimes of complex nonlinear discharge transitions, including the alpha--gamma mode transition, local-to-nonlocal electron collisional heating mode transition and low-density jumps, are accurately mapped within the similarity space.Similarity transformations extend these boundaries across scales, opening a new avenue for rapid kinetic characterization and optimization of radio-frequency plasma sources.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

通过粉末 X 射线衍射 (pXRD) 自主确定晶体结构仍然是自驱动材料实验室的一个关键瓶颈。我们将 pXRD 结构确定的任务制定为可验证的代理搜索问题：可以在推理过程中通过正向模拟轮廓一致性来评估候选结构，并在评估过程中通过与已知参考的结构匹配来评估候选结构。为了衡量进展情况，我们引入了 pXRD-Bench，这是一套包含 200 个模拟单相问题的套件，涵盖分布结构确定、具有挑战性的多态结构以及受控的成分和拓扑分布变化。代理适应将选定的结构恢复率从 27% 提高到 69%，并通过重复采样和验证者引导的推理时间缩放获得进一步收益。我们通过从自身生成的合成问题中学习的反射进化框架，将这种方法扩展到实验多相 pXRD。可更换的实验室规范定义了样品系列和测量条件。进化代理使用已知的生成结构和测量参数来诊断求解器故障并开发持久指导和可执行工具，然后根据测量模式将其提供给代理。这提供了可验证的训练经验，而不需要收集已解决的实验测量结果。在固态合成的 15 个测量模式上，改进的软件包提高了平均轮廓一致性，并转移了模拟替换程序，可恢复所提供的参考库中不存在其结构的相。这些结果表明，从可验证的合成经验中学习如何能够改善事先未知结构的实验样品的代理表征。

English Abstract

Autonomous crystal-structure determination from powder X-ray diffraction (pXRD) remains a key bottleneck for self-driving materials laboratories. We formulate the task of pXRD structure determination as a verifiable agentic search problem: candidate structures can be assessed during inference through forward-simulated profile agreement and during evaluation through structural matching to a known reference. To benchmark progress, we introduce pXRD-Bench, a suite of 200 simulated single-phase problems spanning in-distribution structure determination, challenging and polymorphic structures, and controlled compositional and topological distribution shifts. Agent adaptation raises selected structural recovery from 27% to 69%, with further gains from repeated sampling and verifier-guided inference-time scaling. We extend this approach to experimental multiphase pXRD through a reflexive evolution framework that learns from self-generated synthetic problems. A replaceable laboratory specification defines the sample family and measurement conditions. An evolver agent uses the known generating structures and measurement parameters to diagnose solver failures and develop persistent guidance and executable tools, which are then provided to agents on measured patterns. This supplies verifiable training experience without requiring a collection of solved experimental measurements. On 15 measured patterns from solid-state synthesis, the evolved package improves average profile agreement and transfers an analogue-substitution procedure that recovers a phase whose structure is absent from the supplied reference library. These results demonstrate how learning from verifiable synthetic experience can improve agentic characterization of experimental samples whose structures are not known in advance.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、spin hamiltonian。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

具有铁磁 (FM) 或反铁磁 (AFM) 特征的 Kitaev 耦合在实际材料中形成不同的磁挫败和相位，但它们在单一材料中的共存仍未被探索。在这里，我们提出 Jahn-Teller 畸变作为实现这种不等价键共存的途径。第一性原理计算将单层 CrI2 识别为这样的系统，分别在短键和长键上具有 AFM 和 FM Kitaev 耦合。这些相反的符号对于 Hubbard U、泛函和应变的变化来说是强有力的。最小的紧束缚模型将这种符号对比与 Jahn-Teller 分裂 t2g 轨道及其跳跃到 e_g 轨道联系起来。我们进一步采用簇扩展方法并开发了一种现实的机器学习自旋哈密顿量，它再现了实验观察到的固有螺旋几何形状和传播方向，并揭示了高度不均匀的自旋旋转，转动角度范围从 48.0^circ 到 93.9^circ。固有螺旋各向异性和强非谐自旋旋转都是由混合符号基塔耶夫相互作用产生的。这些发现不仅确立了 Jahn-Teller 畸变作为混合符号 Kitaev 交换的微观途径，而且为探索新型磁相开辟了一条新途径。

English Abstract

Kitaev couplings with ferromagnetic (FM) or antiferromagnetic (AFM) character shape magnetic frustration and phases differently in real materials, yet their coexistence within a single material remains unexplored. Here, we propose Jahn-Teller distortion as a route to realizing such coexistence on inequivalent bonds. First-principles calculations identify monolayer CrI₂ as such a system, with AFM and FM Kitaev couplings on the short and long bonds, respectively. These opposite signs are robust against variations in Hubbard U, functionals, and strain. A minimal tight-binding model links this sign contrast to Jahn-Teller-split t₂g orbitals and their hopping to e_g orbitals. We further adopt the cluster expansion method and develop a realistic machine-learned spin Hamiltonian, which reproduces the experimentally observed proper-screw geometry and propagation direction and reveals highly nonuniform spin rotation, with turning angles ranging from 48.0^circ to 93.9^circ. Both proper-screw anisotropy and strong anharmonic spin rotation arise from the mixed-sign Kitaev interaction. These findings not only establish Jahn-Teller distortion as a microscopic route to mixed-sign Kitaev exchange, but also open a new avenue for exploring novel magnetic phases.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

手性晶体表现出独特的电子、磁性和光学特性，使其对拓扑和自旋电子学应用具有吸引力。然而，迄今为止，有限的已确定的手性半导体，尤其是具有立方对称性的手性半导体，限制了它们的技术应用。在这项工作中，我们对立方空间群 P4₃₃υ（编号 212）中的半导体手性化合物进行了高通量计算搜索。通过将机器学习技术与密度泛函理论相结合，我们获得了 24,227 个包含最多 5 个元素的立方手性结构，并在凸包上识别出 368 个热力学稳定半导体，在 50~meV/原子内识别出 3,048 个热力学稳定半导体。我们分析了它们的结构基序、稳定性和电子能带结构，突出了重复出现的原型家族，其中一些显示了固有的中能带特征。我们的结果大大扩展了立方手性半导体的已知相空间，并可能指导未来手性材料的发现和设计工作。

English Abstract

Chiral crystals show unique electronic, magnetic, and optical characteristics that make them attractive for topological and spintronic applications. Nevertheless, the limited set of identified chiral semiconductors, especially those with cubic symmetry, has so far constrained their technological application. In this work, we perform a high-throughput computational search for semiconducting chiral compounds in the cubic space group P4₃₃₂ (No. 212). By integrating machine-learning techniques with density functional theory, we obtain 24,227 cubic chiral structures containing up to five elements, and identify 368 thermodynamically stable semiconductors on the convex hull and 3,048 within 50~meV/atom. We analyze their structural motifs, stability, and electronic band structures, highlighting recurring prototype families, several of which show intrinsic intermediate-band features. Our results substantially expand the known phase space of cubic chiral semiconductors and may guide future efforts in the discovery and design of chiral materials.

Physics-Spawned Neural Integration of Rotating Shallow-Water Dynamics#

AI×科学交叉2方向匹配0

📄 摘要：Equation-derived neural integration can execute prescribed dynamics without training, while informative ensemble learning can estimate uncertain parameters and identify governing interactions without requiring differenti…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Equation-derived neural integration can execute prescribed dynamics without training, while informative ensemble learning can estimate uncertain parameters and identify governing interactions without requiring differentiability. Together, these approaches motivate Physics-Spawned Informative Networks (ΦSpIN), a framework for prescribed and adaptive hybrid dynamical systems. As a first step toward flood modeling applications, we present ΦSpIN-SW, its prescribed-physics realization for the rotating shallow-water equations. Compatible C-grid operators on spherical Voronoi meshes define a computational graph that retains non-polynomial operations and advances the state through recurrent neural integration compiled with JAX. Analytical benchmarks characterize numerical errors, and global and coastal experiments closely reproduce reference solutions generated using the Model Prediction Across Scales (MPAS) model. At approximately 14~km global resolution, A100, H200 and B200 GPUs attain 9-, 19- and 34-fold higher timestep throughput than a 96-core MPAS-SW CPU reference under matched workloads. These results extend training-free neural integration beyond polynomial dynamics to complex geophysical simulation, establishing a computational foundation for subsequent adjoint-free ensemble estimation of physical and neural parameters and informative adaptation of operator structure.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发
