阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

扭转两个原子层会产生几何莫尔图案，但键合引起的界面重建从根本上将其转变为有序位错网络——这种区别在弱键合范德华系统中被掩盖。尽管由扭曲引起的横向应变调制产生的面内拓扑涡旋纳米结构与独立钙钛矿层1、2、3和2D双层4中的周期性莫尔图案有关，但它们与扭曲层中界面位错网络的耦合仍未解决。在这里，我们证明了扭曲的独立 SrTiO3(STO) 层经历界面重构为螺旋位错网络，并伴随着面内拓扑涡流的出现。与之前的报告不同，这些漩涡与位错网络相关，而不是与几何莫尔图案相关。四维扫描透射电子显微镜（4DSTEM）揭示了与界面位错网络对齐的光束偏转纹理。经过第一性原理计算训练的机器学习原子间势以及相场建模，证实了位错网络内的竞争应变稳定了极涡-反涡对；第一性原理计算进一步揭示了具有明确周期性的电子超晶格的出现。我们的结果将扭转控制的位错网络建立为设计氧化物材料中局部极性和电子结构的新的多功能途径。

English Abstract

Twisting two atomic layers produces a geometric moiré pattern but bonding-induced interfacial reconstruction fundamentally transforms this into an ordered dislocation network—a distinction obscured in weakly bonded van der Waals systems. Although in-plane topological vortex nanostructures arising from twisting-induced lateral strain modulation have been linked to periodic moiré patterns in freestanding perovskite layers1,2,3and 2D bilayers4, their coupling to the interfacial dislocation network in twisted layers remains unresolved. Here we demonstrate that twisted freestanding SrTiO3(STO) layers undergo interfacial reconstruction into a network of screw dislocations, accompanied by the emergence of in-plane topological vortices. Unlike in previous reports, these vortices are associated with the dislocation network rather than with geometric moiré patterns. Four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4DSTEM) reveals a beam deflection texture in registry with the interfacial dislocation network. A machine learning interatomic potential, trained on first-principles calculations, together with phase-field modelling, confirms that competing strains within the dislocation network stabilize polar vortex–antivortex pairs; first-principles calculations further reveal the emergence of an electronic superlattice with a well-defined periodicity. Our results establish twist-controlled dislocation networks as a new and versatile route to designing local polar and electronic structures in oxide materials.

中文摘要

对称分析、第一性原理计算、中子散射和光电发射光谱的结合显示了化学计量金属 EuSi 的近费米能级电子结构中的 p 波磁性。

English Abstract

One active frontier of quantum-matter research is the realization ofp-wave symmetry-breaking phases in fermionic systems, long exemplified by the A phase of superfluid helium-3 (refs.1,2,3). Achievingp-wave superconductivity4and its magnetic analogue5—characterized by a time-reversal-symmetric,p-wave spin-polarized Fermi surface—remains a grand challenge6,7. Despite recent progress in identifying candidatep-wave magnets5,7,8,9,10, spin-resolved spectroscopic evidence for their electronic structures remains limited. Here we report angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) and spin-ARPES evidence forp-wave magnetism in the near-Fermi-level electronic structure of stoichiometric metallic EuSi, supported by symmetry analysis, first-principles calculations and neutron scattering. Neutron diffraction reveals a chiral spiral phase between 33.4 K and 62 K that fulfils archetypalp-wave magnetism criteria: it preserves the combined symmetry of time reversal and lattice translation, while breaking inversion symmetry. Spin-integrated ARPES reveals a pronounced magnetism-driven reconstruction of the electronic structure, with energy separations exceeding 100 meV, and spin-ARPES resolvesSypolarization in near-Fermi-level spectral features. The extractedSyexhibits a sign-changing modulation across successive magnetic Brillouin zones and is suppressed near the nodal regions, consistent with the predictedp-wave spin texture and unfolded spin-polarization calculations. Magnetic force microscopy and micro-ARPES further reveal approximately 10-μm chiral magnetic domains. These results identify EuSi as a stoichiometric metallic platform for investigatingp-wave magnetism in electronic band structures and spin-symmetry-based functionalities in spintronics10, topological matter7, superconductivity11and beyond.

中文摘要

滑动多铁性允许对磁性进行电控制。在这里，作者发现了具有混合宇称自旋分裂的非常规磁体中的滑动多铁性，使得偶数和奇数宇称自旋分裂的铁电切换成为可能，这两者都表现在自旋电流中。

English Abstract

In this work, we introduce a class of hybrid-parity sliding multiferroics in which the spontaneous ferroelectric polarization is coupled to certain nonrelativistic spin splitting components through interlayer sliding, allowing these components to be reversibly switched in an electrical way. Symmetry analysis identifies coplanar magnets as natural platforms for realizing this form of sliding multiferroicity. First-principles calculations establish bilayer VBr2as a representative example, demonstrating the coupled reversal of the out-of-plane ferroelectric polarization and the signs of both even- and odd-parity nonrelativistic spin splitting components via an interlayer-sliding pathway. The signs of these nonrelativistic spin splitting components are locked to the sliding-switchable ferroelectric polarization and encoded in the spin-current responses, providing a signature of the coupled ferroic switching. Our findings expand the scope of sliding multiferroics and the functionality of sliding ferroelectrics for low-energy, nonvolatile logic devices.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在具有竞争性最近邻和次近邻交换的受挫自旋链中，矢量自旋手性决定了电极化，因此铁电畴壁是手性的畴壁。半经典图表明由于自旋的拓扑约束而产生的非局域切换动力学，但人们对量子自旋链中的此类畴壁知之甚少。使用密度矩阵重正化群计算受挫的自旋 1/2 XXZ 链（其中键交替 δ 打开自旋间隙），我们表明壁随后变成了量子粒子。在有限链中，它具有盒子中粒子的能级和波函数，并且它在电场下移动，就像粒子在恒定力下一样。在无限链中，随着 δ 减小，其质量增加两个数量级以上，并在 δ^*approx0.012 处发散，此时带的底部变得平坦；低于 δ^* 时，能带有两个远离其中心的简并极小值。大质量是由于面内和面外交换能量几乎抵消而产生的。

English Abstract

In frustrated spin chains with competing nearest- and next-nearest-neighbor exchange, the vector spin chirality sets the electric polarization, so a ferroelectric domain wall is a domain wall of the chirality. The semiclassical picture suggests nonlocal switching dynamics due to topological constraints on the spins, but little is known about such domain walls in a quantum spin chain. Using density-matrix renormalization group calculations for a frustrated spin-1/2 XXZ chain in which a bond alternation δ opens a spin gap, we show that the wall then becomes a quantum particle. In a finite chain, it has the energy levels and wave functions of a particle in a box, and it moves under an electric field as a particle under a constant force does. In the infinite chain, its mass grows by more than two orders of magnitude as δ decreases and diverges at δ^*approx0.012, where the bottom of its band becomes flat; below δ^* the band has two degenerate minima away from its center. The large mass results from a near cancellation of in-plane and out-of-plane exchange energies.

中文摘要

具有强相关电子的层状铁磁体为探索磁性、自旋轨道耦合和非平凡能带拓扑之间的相互作用提供了一个平台。 Ni₁/4TaSe2 是相关范德华铁磁体，转变温度为 58 K，其成分与 Ni 耗尽时出现的超导相接近。在这里，我们结合自旋和角分辨光电子能谱、圆二色性测量和密度泛函理论计算来研究其自旋分辨电子结构。我们观察到跨越费米能级的自旋极化态以及铁磁态中时间反转对称性破缺的特征。自旋分辨圆二色性测量进一步揭示了与有限自旋贝里曲率一致的螺旋度相关的自旋响应，并暗示了局部拓扑特征。总之，这些结果在 Ni₁/₄TaSe2 中的铁磁性、自旋极化电子态和拓扑特征之间建立了实验联系，为研究这些特性与 Ni 耗尽时超导性的出现之间的关系奠定了基础。

English Abstract

Layered ferromagnets with strongly correlated electrons provide a platform for exploring the interplay among magnetism, spin-orbit coupling, and nontrivial band topology. Ni₁/₄TaSe₂ is a correlated van der Waals ferromagnet with a transition temperature of 58 K that lies close in composition to a superconducting phase that emerges upon Ni depletion. Here, we combine spin- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, circular-dichroism measurements, and density functional theory calculations to investigate its spin-resolved electronic structure. We observe spin-polarized states crossing the Fermi level and signatures of broken time-reversal symmetry in the ferromagnetic state. Spin-resolved circular-dichroism measurements further reveal a helicity-dependent spin response consistent with a finite spin Berry curvature and suggestive of local topological character. Together, these results establish an experimental connection between ferromagnetism, spin-polarized electronic states, and topological signatures in Ni₁/₄TaSe₂, providing a foundation for investigating how these properties may relate to the emergence of superconductivity upon Ni depletion.

English Abstract

Two-dimensional (2D) ferromagnetic topological materials provide appealing platforms for realizing spin-polarized topological states, yet the coexistence of Weyl fermions and robust high-temperature ferromagnetism remains elusive. Here, based on first-principles calculations and Monte Carlo simulations, we predict monolayer BaMn₄O₈ to be a dynamically, thermally, and mechanically stable ferromagnetic Weyl half-semimetal. textcolorblue{An anisotropic Monte Carlo simulation on a 20×20×1 lattice yields a finite-size transition estimate of approximately 1446~K.} The spin-resolved electronic structure reveals a fully spin-polarized state, in which the spin-up channel hosts linear Weyl band crossings near the Fermi level, whereas the spin-down channel remains insulating. Corresponding Fermi-arc edge states connecting the projected Weyl points (WPs) are clearly observed along the (100) boundary, confirming the nontrivial topology. Importantly, both the Weyl points and their associated Fermi arcs remain robust in the presence of spin--orbit coupling (SOC). textcolorblue{These results identify monolayer BaMn₄O₈ as a candidate for fully spin-polarized topological transport.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

布洛赫波函数和晶体对称性的量子几何在凝聚态物理学中发挥着核心作用，塑造了固体的电子拓扑和物理响应。特别是，通过量子振荡探测的克莱默-外尔费米子、自旋分裂费米面以及量子几何非线性霍尔和本征自旋霍尔响应引起了人们的广泛关注。尽管在单独理解这些现象方面取得了进展，但它们与拓扑表面态、费米弧和手性依赖的量子几何输运的共存仍然很少，因为明显的响应涉及适当的对称性、SOC 和费米表面几何形状。在这里，我们利用第一性原理计算和对称性分析，考虑了两种结构对映体，探索了实验合成的 WAl2 中的这种协同作用。我们观察到了 12 个具有量化手性电荷 |C|=1 和 |C|=3 的 Kramers-Weyl (KW) 交叉，并伴随着非平凡的表面态和费米弧。明显的金属特征产生了六个表现出反对称 SOC 的费米表面片，具有独特的极值轨道和通过计算的德哈斯-范阿尔芬 (dHvA) 量子振荡解析的候选自旋分裂对。该电子结构还表现出由于贝里曲率的不对称分布而产生的大的本征贝里曲率偶极子（BCD），从而产生非线性霍尔响应。两种对映体的相反结构旋手性反转了手性和BCD，反转了费米弧和非线性霍尔电流的方向。 BCD 达到 |Dzz|约 0.338，产生非线性霍尔电流密度 |jₓ|约 5.04×101⁰~mahrmA,m⁻2 和 |j_y|约 2.52×101⁰~mahrmA,m⁻2。我们观察到 SHC 的增强与 Γ 处的三电荷 KW 点相关，这表明较高的陈数在 SHC 中的作用。

English Abstract

Quantum geometry of Bloch wave functions and crystalline symmetries play a central role in condensed matter physics, shaping the electronic topology and physical responses of solids. In particular, Kramers-Weyl fermions, spin-split Fermi surfaces probed through quantum oscillations and quantum geometric nonlinear Hall and intrinsic spin Hall responses have garnered significant attention. Despite progress in understanding these phenomena individually, their coexistence with topological surface states, Fermi arcs and chirality dependent quantum geometric transport remains scarce, as pronounced responses involve suitable symmetries, SOC and Fermi surface geometry. Here, we explore this synergy in experimentally synthesized WAl₂, considering both structural enantiomers using first-principles calculations and symmetry analysis. We observed twelve Kramers-Weyl (KW) crossings with quantized chiral charges |C|=1 and |C|=3, accompanied by nontrivial surface states and Fermi arcs. The pronounced metallic character gives rise to six Fermi surface sheets exhibiting antisymmetric SOC, with distinct extremal orbits and candidate spin-split pairs resolved through calculated de Haas-van Alphen (dHvA) quantum oscillations. The electronic structure also exhibits large intrinsic Berry curvature dipole (BCD) arising from the asymmetric distribution of Berry curvature, giving rise to a nonlinear Hall response. The opposite structural handedness of the two enantiomers reverses the chiralities and BCD, reversing the direction of Fermi arcs and nonlinear Hall current. BCD attains |Dzz|approx0.338, yielding nonlinear Hall current densities |jₓ|approx5.04×10¹⁰~mathrmA,m⁻² and |j_y|approx2.52×10¹⁰~mathrmA,m⁻². We observe enhanced SHC associated with triply charged KW point at Γ, suggesting role of higher Chern number in the SHC.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

天然矿物 Zn₆Cu₃(TeO₆)2(OH)₆ · (AgₓPb_yClₓ₊₂y) 是一种结构理想的 kagome 磁体，为探索无序框架中几何挫败性、手性和可调性的相互作用提供了一个平台。在这里，我们提出了对其电子和磁性特性的（第一个）全面的从头开始研究。电子结构以局域半填充的 Cu dₓ²₋y² 轨道为主，这些轨道通过电子关联而变得绝缘。将低能物理映射到海森堡模型表明，磁性主要由两种交换相互作用控制：最近邻层内 kagome 耦合和次近邻层间耦合。它们的竞争稳定了非常规的三维手性磁态。每个 kagome 层都有 sqrt3×sqrt3 阶，而相邻层旋转 60^circ，沿晶体 c 轴产生右手螺旋。这种内在的手性顺序自然地从晶体结构和磁相互作用中出现，使羽蛇纹石成为完美戈薇晶格上手性磁性的独特实现。与此同时，交换相互作用的小能量尺度使材料接近竞争的磁态，这表明适度的压力、化学替代或结构修饰可能会强烈增强挫败感，抑制长程有序，并有可能推动系统走向量子自旋液体状态。

English Abstract

The natural mineral quetzalcoatlite Zn₆Cu₃(TeO₆)₂(OH)₆ · (AgₓPb_yClₓ₊₂y) is a structurally ideal kagome magnet, providing a platform for exploring the interplay of geometric frustration, chirality, and tunability in a disorder-free framework. Here, we present the (first) comprehensive ab initio study of its electronic and magnetic properties. The electronic structure is dominated by localized half-filled Cu dₓ^₂₋y^₂ orbitals that become insulating through electronic correlations. Mapping the low-energy physics onto a Heisenberg model reveals that the magnetism is governed primarily by two exchange interactions: a nearest-neighbor intralayer kagome coupling and a next-nearest-neighbor interlayer coupling. Their competition stabilizes an unconventional three-dimensional chiral magnetic state. Each kagome layer hosts a sqrt3×sqrt3 order, while adjacent layers are rotated by 60^circ, producing a right-handed spiral along the crystallographic c-axis. This intrinsic chiral order emerges naturally from the crystal structure and magnetic interactions, establishing quetzalcoatlite as a distinctive realization of chiral magnetism on a perfect kagome lattice. At the same time, the small energy scale of the exchange interactions places the material close to competing magnetic regimes, suggesting that moderate pressure, chemical substitution, or structural modifications may strongly enhance frustration, suppress long-range order, and potentially drive the system toward a quantum spin-liquid state.

交变磁感应磁光效应#

原题：Altermagnetism-induced magneto-optical effects
作者：Jun Ōiké、Robert Peters、Koki Shinada。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-18
⚡ 磁性/铁电

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：尽管交变磁体的净磁化强度为零，但它打破了时间反转对称性，从而实现了时间反转奇响应，同时保留了反铁磁体的优点。然而，磁光 (MO) 效应在交流磁体中并不普遍，因为 MO 效应通常源自磁偶极级（铁磁性），而磁偶极级在交流磁体中通常不存在。在这项工作中，我们通过建立八极 MO 效应来解决这一限制，该效应是通过空间色散产生的磁性八极秩序。由于这种独特的起源，即使在共线磁体中，八极 MO 效应也不一定需要自旋轨道耦合，并且在反铁磁体中无需外部磁场…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、spin-orbit coupling。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

尽管交变磁体的净磁化强度为零，但它打破了时间反转对称性，从而实现了时间反转奇响应，同时保留了反铁磁体的优点。然而，磁光 (MO) 效应在交流磁体中并不普遍，因为 MO 效应通常源自磁偶极级（铁磁性），而磁偶极级在交流磁体中通常不存在。在这项工作中，我们通过建立八极 MO 效应来解决这一限制，该效应是通过空间色散产生的磁性八极秩序。由于这种独特的起源，即使在共线磁体中，八极 MO 效应也不一定需要自旋轨道耦合，并且在反铁磁体中无需外部磁场即可产生，而传统 MO 效应是对称禁止的。这种反铁磁体包括 d 波交变磁体，如 RuO2 和 KV2Se2O，某些 g 波和 i 波交变磁体，包括 CrSb，以及非共面反铁磁体，如 MnTe2 和具有全进全出磁序的烧绿石铱酸盐。从实验角度来看，八极 MO 效应可以达到可检测的幅度，并为磁域成像提供潜力，数值计算显示 100 nm 厚的样品在可见光范围内发生 μrad 级法拉第旋转，以及相反域之间的符号反转。此外，八极磁矩效应与 f-sum 规则相关，可以通过光学测量定量估计自旋磁八极矩。这些结果将 MO 效应的范围扩展到广泛的反铁磁体，包括交流磁体，并为探索磁性八极秩序开辟了一条无场光学途径。

English Abstract

Altermagnets break time-reversal symmetry despite their zero net magnetization, enabling time-reversal-odd responses while retaining the advantages of antiferromagnets. However, magneto-optical (MO) effects are not universal among altermagnets because MO effects typically arise from magnetic dipole order (ferromagnetism), which is generally absent in altermagnets. In this work, we address this limitation by establishing octupolar MO effects, which arise from magnetic octupole order through spatial dispersion. Owing to this distinct origin, octupolar MO effects do not necessarily require spin-orbit coupling even in collinear magnets and can arise without an external magnetic field in antiferromagnets where conventional MO effects are symmetry-forbidden. Such antiferromagnets include d-wave altermagnets such as RuO₂ and KV₂Se₂O, certain g- and i-wave altermagnets, including CrSb, as well as noncoplanar antiferromagnets such as MnTe₂ and pyrochlore iridates with all-in-all-out magnetic order. From an experimental perspective, octupolar MO effects can reach detectable magnitudes and offer potential for magnetic-domain imaging, as supported by numerical calculations showing μrad-scale Faraday rotation for a 100-nm-thick sample in the visible-light range and a sign reversal between opposite domains. Furthermore, octupolar MO effects are linked to an f-sum rule, enabling a quantitative estimate of the spin magnetic octupole moment from optical measurements. These results extend the scope of MO effects to a broad class of antiferromagnets, including altermagnets, and open a field-free optical route to exploring magnetic octupole order.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、spin hamiltonian。

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们报告了对蜂窝磁体 α-Na2PrO3 单晶的角度相关压电悬臂梁扭矩测量，建议显示通过自旋轨道纠缠、赝自旋 1/2 Pr⁴⁺ 磁矩之间的各向异性交换相互作用稳定的非常规磁性。以前的粉末磁化率观察到在低温下向具有弱铁磁矩的反铁磁序转变的特征。根据三个正交晶面的扭矩角依赖性，我们推断出一种零场倾斜反铁磁结构，其力矩几乎垂直于蜂窝层，但倾斜较弱，以便沿面内单斜 a 轴产生自发磁化，具有两个与时间反转相关的磁畴。当沿变化符号的场投影被解释为具有相反排列的铁磁矩的两个简并域之间的场感应切换时，扭矩角依赖性的急剧跳跃。我们提出了一种最小自旋哈密顿量，包括易轴交换各向异性和允许对称性的 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) 耦合，其平均场计算再现了观察到的与角度相关的扭矩的主要特征。几乎垂直施加于层的高达 35 T 的扭矩测量观察到由于接近上述哈密顿量预测的场引起的自旋翻转转变而导致的强烈异常。我们对比了垂直于蜂窝层的不同正交晶面中磁场的预测磁相图，并讨论了DM相互作用如何将一些转变转化为平滑交叉，并稳定了连接垂直于层的场中的自旋翻转转变与垂直于a的面内场的磁化饱和的相界线；在没有 DM 相互作用的情况下，这样的相界线不存在。

English Abstract

We report angle-dependent piezocantilever torque measurements on single crystals of the honeycomb magnet α-Na₂PrO₃, proposed to display unconventional magnetism stabilized by anisotropic exchange interactions between spin-orbit entangled, pseudospin 1/2 Pr⁴⁺ magnetic moments. Previous powder susceptibility observed signatures of a transition at low temperatures to antiferromagnetic order with a weak ferromagnetic moment. From the torque angular dependence in three orthogonal crystallographic planes, we infer a zero-field canted antiferromagnetic structure, with moments almost normal to the honeycomb layers, but weakly canted such as to give rise to a spontaneous magnetization along the in-plane monoclinic a-axis, with two magnetic domains related by time reversal. Sharp jumps in the torque angular dependence when the field projection along a changes sign are interpreted as field-induced switching between the two degenerate domains with oppositely aligned ferromagnetic moments. We propose a minimal spin Hamiltonian including easy-axis exchange anisotropy and symmetry-allowed Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) couplings, for which mean-field calculations reproduce the main features of the observed angle-dependent torque. Torque measurements up to 35 T applied almost normal to the layers observe a strong anomaly attributed to proximity to the field-induced spin-flop transition predicted for the above Hamiltonian. We contrast the predicted magnetic phase diagrams for magnetic fields in different orthogonal crystallographic planes normal to the honeycomb layers and discuss how DM interactions convert some transitions into smooth crossovers and stabilize a phase boundary line connecting the spin-flop transition in field normal to the layers to magnetization saturation for in-plane field normal to a; such a phase boundary line does not exist in the absence of DM interactions.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

具有耦合电序和磁序的多铁性材料具有通过用电场代替电流进行磁开关来实现低功耗存储器和逻辑器件的潜力。将多铁性材料的维数从 3D 降低到 2D，通过减少静电屏蔽和增强原子薄极限下的量子涨落，实现对磁电耦合的前所未有的控制，从而在这个已经蓬勃发展的领域开辟了新的前沿，这是块体对应物无法达到的。一个著名的例子是几层 NiI2 多铁性材料，它表现出共存的手性自旋结构和（反）铁电性，并伴有强磁电耦合；通过同时打破时间反转和空间反转对称性，NiI2显示出新的物理性质，例如通过巨磁电耦合对手性磁性和极化进行独特的相互控制、超快电磁子激发和奇宇称p波磁性。然而，在明确识别内在多铁性方面存在实验挑战。本综述批判性地审视了二维多铁材料的当前前景，重点是实验方法而不是材料分类学。我们评估了磁性和铁电性的不同传统探针，并确定了单一技术方法的关键局限性。我们强调磁光电互相关测量在建立内在多铁性行为中的重要性，并概述了基础研究和器件应用的未来研究方向。

English Abstract

Multiferroic materials, with coupled electric and magnetic orders, hold the potential to enable low-power memory and logic devices by replacing electrical currents with electric fields for magnetic switching. Reducing the dimensionality of multiferroics, from 3D to 2D, has opened new frontiers in this already burgeoning field by enabling unprecedented control over magnetoelectric coupling that is beyond the reach of bulk counterparts, owing to reduced electrostatic screening and enhanced quantum fluctuations at the atomically thin limit. A notable example is a few-layer NiI2 multiferroic, which exhibits coexisting chiral spin textures and (anti)ferroelectricity, accompanied by strong magnetoelectric coupling; by simultaneously breaking time-reversal and spatial-inversion symmetry, NiI2 shows new physical properties such as unique mutual control of chiral magnetism and polarization with giant magnetoelectric coupling, ultrafast electromagnon excitations and odd-parity p-wave magnetism. However, there are experimental challenges in unambiguously identifying intrinsic multiferroicity. This Review critically examines the current landscape of 2D multiferroic materials, with an emphasis on experimental methodologies rather than material taxonomy. We assess different conventional probes for magnetism and ferroelectricity and identify key limitations in single-technique approaches. We emphasize the importance of magneto-opto-electric cross-correlative measurements in establishing intrinsic multiferroic behaviour and outline future research directions for both fundamental studies and device applications.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：spin-orbit coupling、effective hamiltonian、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

自旋轨道耦合和电子关联在广泛的化学和物理现象中发挥着重要作用。在这项工作中，我们系统地评估了单参考代数图解构造与状态相互作用（SI-ADC）相结合的性能，用于描述存在自旋轨道耦合的电子结构。我们发现二阶 SI-ADC 方法 (SI-ADC(2)) 实现的零场分裂精度与更高级别和计算要求更高的方法相当，包括基于自旋轨道有效哈密顿量的变分四分量 ADC 和双分量 ADC 方法。我们通过评估各种自旋轨道耦合特性，包括电子亲和势、电离势、中性激发能、核心电离能和磁 g 张量，进一步证明了 SI-ADC 的广泛适用性，适用于从小分子到过渡金属配合物和 f 块化合物的系统。在这些应用中，SI-ADC(2) 方法提供一致的准确结果，这可能部分受益于电子相关效应和自旋轨道效应之间有利的误差消除。相比之下，SI-ADC(3) 方法通常不太准确，特别是对于含有过渡金属和重元素的系统。总体而言，这些结果将 SI-ADC 确立为一种有效且广泛适用的框架，可将自旋轨道耦合纳入电子和光谱特性的 ADC 计算中。

English Abstract

Spin-orbit coupling and electron correlation play important roles in a broad range of chemical and physical phenomena. In this work, we systematically assess the performance of single-reference algebraic diagrammatic construction combined with state interaction (SI-ADC) for describing electronic structure in the presence of spin-orbit coupling. We find that second-order SI-ADC methods (SI-ADC(2)) achieve zero-field-splitting accuracies comparable to those of higher-level and more computationally demanding approaches, including variational four-component ADC and two-component ADC methods based on spin-orbit effective Hamiltonians. We further demonstrate the broad applicability of SI-ADC by evaluating a diverse range of spin-orbit-coupled properties, including electron affinities, ionization potentials, neutral excitation energies, core-ionization energies, and magnetic g-tensors, for systems ranging from small molecules to transition-metal complexes and f-block compounds. Across these applications, SI-ADC(2) methods provide consistently accurate results, likely benefiting in part from favorable error cancellation between electron-correlation and spin-orbit effects. In contrast, SI-ADC(3) methods are generally less accurate, particularly for systems containing transition-metal and heavy elements. Overall, these results establish SI-ADC as an efficient and broadly applicable framework for incorporating spin-orbit coupling into ADC calculations of electronic and spectroscopic properties.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在这项研究中，我们展示了通过 MBE 在 (001) 取向 SrTiO3 基底上生长的反铁磁 (LaVO3)m/(PrVO3)n 超晶格的原子级工程。我们的工作实现了精确的逐层控制，揭示了如何系统地调整界面驱动的结构扭曲。 X 射线衍射和倒易空间映射证实了超晶格的外延应变状态和高结晶质量。高分辨率扫描透射电子显微镜进一步揭示了层分辨原子位移，其中 La3+ 和 Pr3+ 表现出受超晶格周期性调制的反极运动。 LaVO3 和 PrVO3 的交替堆叠产生独特的反铁磁转变温度，可以通过调整它们的相对比例来调节。尽管在组成材料中观察到不同的自旋排序温度，但这种行为仍然存在。层分辨光谱测量表明，La 和 Pr 5d 轨道与 O 2p 轨道的杂化也受到调制，并且与氧八面体旋转直接相关。通过确定轨道杂化并将阳离子位移映射为代理，我们可以间接可视化氧八面体旋转和反极稀土位移之间的三线性耦合。值得注意的是，立方 SrTiO3 基底和斜方钒酸盐层之间的界面氧八面体连通性显着影响结构变形。这些扭曲在界面附近几乎被淬灭，在几个晶胞上松弛，展示了应变和界面效应如何通过超晶格传播。这项研究说明了如何设计人造超晶格来控制原子尺度的结构扭曲，提供了一种通过钙钛矿氧化物中的应变扭曲关系来调整磁性的途径。

English Abstract

In this study, we demonstrate the atomic-scale engineering of antiferromagnetic (LaVO3)m/(PrVO3)n superlattices grown by MBE on (001)-oriented SrTiO3 substrates. Our work achieved a precise layer-by-layer control, revealing how interface-driven structural distortions can be systematically tuned. X-ray diffraction and reciprocal space mapping confirm both the epitaxial strain state and the high crystalline quality of the superlattices. High-resolution scanning transmission electron microscopy further uncovers layer-resolved atomic displacements, where La3+ and Pr3+ exhibit antipolar motions modulated by the superlattice periodicity. The alternate stackings of LaVO3 and PrVO3 yield a unique antiferromagnetic transition temperature, which can be tuned by adjusting their relative proportions. This behavior exists despite the distinct spin ordering temperatures observed in the constituent materials. Layer-resolved spectroscopic measurements reveal that the hybridization of La and Pr 5d orbitals with O 2p orbitals is also modulated and directly correlated to the oxygen octahedra rotations. By determining the orbital hybridization and mapping the cation displacements as proxies, we can visualize indirectly the trilinear coupling between oxygen octahedra rotations and antipolar rare earth displacements. Notably, the interfacial oxygen octahedra connectivity between cubic SrTiO3 substrate and orthorhombic vanadate layers significantly influences the structural distortions. These distortions, almost quenched near the interface, relax over few unit cells, demonstrating how strain and interfacial effects propagate through the superlattice. This study illustrates how artificial superlattices can be engineered to control structural distortions at the atomic scale, offering a pathway to tune the magnetic properties through strain-distortion relationships in perovskite oxides.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

反铁磁磁振子为高速、节能的信息处理提供了一个有前景的平台。摆线磁序中的固有奇宇称自旋分裂提供了补偿磁体中不消失磁振子输运的途径。在这里，我们提出了一种基于铁电反铁磁体 BiFeO3 的磁振子场效应器件（类似于晶体管），其中栅极电场重新配置域边界以放大/调制磁振子输运。这些边界在低信号状态和高信号状态之间切换非局部逆自旋霍尔响应，并且明显依赖于磁振子传播的方向。源漏反转、磁场测量、对称分析和第一原理计算揭示了与跨域边界的自旋角动量反转相关的不可逆磁振子输运。我们的结果建立了一种电可编程、非易失性磁振子晶体管。

English Abstract

Antiferromagnetic magnons offer a promising platform for highspeed, energy-efficient information processing. Intrinsic odd-parity spin splitting in a cycloidal magnetic order offers a route to nonvanishing magnon transport in compensated magnets. Here, we propose a magnonic field-effect device (akin to a transistor) based on a ferroelectric antiferromagnet BiFeO3 in which a gate electric field reconfigures domain boundaries to amplify/modulate magnon transport. These boundaries switch the nonlocal inverse-spin-Hall response between low- and high-signal states, with a pronounced dependence on the direction of magnon propagation. Source-drain reversal, magnetic-field measurements, symmetry analysis, and first-principles calculations reveal nonreciprocal magnon transport associated with the reversal of spin angular momentum across the domain boundaries. Our results establish an electrically programmable, non-volatile magnon transistor.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们报告说，两个铁电铌酸锂单晶之间形成的扭曲界面包含连贯键合和弹性变形的纳米级结构螺旋阵列，通过螺旋位错彼此分开。螺旋在四维扫描透射电子显微镜 (4D STEM) 和相关微分相差 (DPC) 中产生显着的对比度。通过对电子穿过界面时沿螺旋路径传播的轨迹进行建模，这些图像已成功合理化，但隐含的结构螺旋也已使用电子叠层描记法直接成像。重要的是，由于铌酸锂是单轴铁电体，因此通过考虑相关压电张量和挠电系数推断，这些结构螺旋中的扭转应变也必然会导致偶极螺旋的形成。螺旋在拓扑上与更常见的涡旋不同，重要的是，它们是手性的。通过观察样品倒转前后的 DPC 图像相同，可以确认螺旋（因此也是手性）特征。手性也通过二次谐波光学圆偏振二色性测量来确定，其中观察到异常大的界面信号。必须强调的是，观察到的螺旋阵列与莫尔图案不同。它们促进晶格旋转，结合塑性和弹性变形，并且不仅仅是晶格的叠加，相对于彼此扭曲。因此，紧密结合的扭曲氧化物界面将具有与二维范德华材料的扭曲堆叠明显不同的特性，从而开辟了一种新的、独特的扭曲电子学。

English Abstract

We report that twisted interfaces, formed between two ferroelectric lithium niobate single crystals, contain arrays of coherently bonded and elastically deformed nanoscale structural helices, separated from one another by screw dislocations. The helices generate remarkable contrast in four dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D STEM) and through related differential phase contrast (DPC). Such images have been successfully rationalised by modelling the trajectory of electrons that channel along helical pathways as they traverse the interface, but the structural helices implied have also been directly imaged using electron ptychography. Importantly, as lithium niobate is a uniaxial ferroelectric, the torsional strain in these structural helices necessarily also causes dipolar helices to form, inferred through consideration of pertinent piezoelectric tensors and flexoelectric coefficients. Helices are topologically distinct from the more commonly reported vortices and, importantly, they are chiral. Helical (and hence chiral) character was confirmed by observing that DPC images were identical before and after the sample was inverted. Chirality was also established through second harmonic optical circularly-polarised dichroism measurements, in which unusually large interfacial signals were seen. It must be stressed that the arrays of helices observed are distinct from moiré patterns; they facilitate lattice rotation combining plastic and elastic deformation and are not simply a superposition of lattices, twisted with respect to one another. Thus, intimately bonded twisted oxide interfaces will have distinctly different characteristics from those of twisted stacks of two-dimensional van der Waals materials, opening up a new and distinct kind of twistronics.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、density functional theory、monte carlo simulation。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

超戈麦晶格上的反铁磁体提供了实现强磁挫败的三维途径，但通过完善的微观哈密顿量通过实验实现的方法仍然很少。石榴石是一个广泛的材料家族，其中磁性离子可以形成两个互穿的超戈格姆晶格，从而产生自旋玻璃、自旋液体和隐序物理。在这里，我们将铁铝硅酸盐石榴石铁铝榴石 Fe₃Al2Si₃O₁2 确定为 spin-2 hyperkagome 反铁磁体。利用密度泛函理论能量图，我们确定了高自旋 Fe²⁺ 矩的海森堡哈密顿量，并发现了占主导地位的最近邻反铁磁交换，以及显着的长程相互作用。该哈密顿量的经典蒙特卡罗模拟，结合自旋长度的量子到经典重新缩放，显示出在 Tᵣₘ Napprox9.8K̃ 处向反铁磁序的转变，与比热实验非常一致。由此产生的非共线磁态自然可以通过磁环八极序来描述，我们将其与高度失挫的超卡戈姆反铁磁体钆镓石榴石 Gd₃Ga₅O₁2 的已知结果联系起来。我们还使用半经典动力学计算磁中子衍射振幅以及静态和动态结构因子，为未来的中子散射实验提供定量预测。我们的结果表明铁铝榴石是石榴石家族中受阻超戈梅磁性的化学可实现实现。

English Abstract

Antiferromagnets on the hyperkagome lattice provide a three-dimensional route to strong magnetic frustration, but experimentally accessible realizations with well-established microscopic Hamiltonians remain scarce. Garnets are a broad family of materials in which magnetic ions can form two interpenetrating hyperkagome lattices, giving rise to spin glass, spin-liquid and hidden-order physics. Here we identify the iron aluminosilicate garnet almandine, Fe₃Al₂Si₃O₁₂, as a spin-2 hyperkagome antiferromagnet. Using density functional theory energy mapping, we determine a Heisenberg Hamiltonian for the high-spin Fe²⁺ moments and find dominant nearest-neighbor antiferromagnetic exchange, as well as significant longer-range interactions. Classical Monte Carlo simulations of this Hamiltonian, combined with quantum-to-classical rescaling of the spin length, show a transition to antiferromagnetic order at Tᵣₘ Napprox9.8K̃, in excellent agreement with specific heat experiments. The resulting non-collinear magnetic state is naturally described by magnetic toroidal octupolar order, which we connect to known results for the highly frustrated hyperkagome antiferromagnet gadolinium gallium garnet, Gd₃Ga₅O₁₂. We also calculate magnetic neutron diffraction amplitudes as well as static and dynamic structure factors using semiclassical dynamics, providing quantitative predictions for future neutron scattering experiments. Our results establish almandine as a chemically accessible realization of frustrated hyperkagome magnetism in the garnet family.

中文摘要

斯格明子是一种新兴的多体激发，它是多分量量子类霍尔系统和解域量子临界性的核心。在一篇姊妹文章（X. Wang 等人，arXiv：2507.22971）中，我们使用数值精确的行列式量子蒙特卡罗计算研究了可调谐相互作用陈能带的微观时间反转对称模型，并提出了鲁棒斯格明子激发出现的证据。这些带电激发出现在多体绝缘体附近，并相应填充陈能带，当远离绝缘相进行掺杂时，会导致超导性的开始。在这里，我们提出了量子蒙特卡罗结果以及对作为两个不同调谐参数的函数的交织相之间出现的量子相变的补充场论分析。我们的数值结果与绝缘陈反铁磁体和全能隙超导体之间的单连续量子相变一致，在假定的临界点处出现 SO(5) 对称性，高度暗示了解禁量子（伪）临界性。我们还详细比较了与中性集体模式、单电子和复合自旋极化子激发相关的动量分辨谱函数，这些函数是使用蒙特卡罗计算和建立在自洽 Hartree-Fock 计算之上的 Bethe-Salpeter 分析相结合获得的。最后我们对一些有趣的开放性问题进行了简要展望。

English Abstract

Skyrmions are emergent many-body excitations that lie at the heart of both multi-component quantum Hall-like systems and deconfined quantum criticality. In a companion article (X. Wang et al., arXiv:2507.22971), we studied a microscopic time-reversal symmetric model of tunable interacting Chern bands using numerically exact determinant quantum Monte Carlo calculations, and presented evidence for the emergence of robust skyrmion excitations. These charged excitations emerge in the vicinity of a many-body insulator at a commensurate filling of the Chern bands, and lead to the onset of superconductivity when doped away from the insulating phase. Here, we present quantum Monte-Carlo results and a complementary field-theoretical analysis for the quantum phase transition(s) that arise between the intertwined phases as a function of two distinct tuning parameters. Our numerical results are consistent with a single continuous quantum phase transition between an insulating Chern antiferromagnet and a fully gapped superconductor, with an emergent SO(5) symmetry at the putative critical point, highly suggestive of deconfined quantum (pseudo-)criticality. We also present a detailed comparison between the momentum-resolved spectral functions associated with the neutral collective modes, single electron and composite spin-polaron excitations obtained using a combination of Monte-Carlo computations and a Bethe-Salpeter analysis built on top of the self-consistent Hartree-Fock calculation. We end with a brief outlook on some of the interesting open problems.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：ferroelectricity、phase transition。

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

近年来，二维 (2D) 铁电性已在各种 2D 材料中得到了广泛研究，而本征 2D 反铁电性仍然异常罕见，限制了其在下一代光子和电子器件中的应用。在这里，我们报告了在层状过渡金属卤氧化物 NbOCl2 中观察到的室温稳定反铁电性，并证明了该材料中可逆的、刺激驱动的反铁电到铁电（AFE-to-FE）相变。二次谐波产生 (SHG) 映射揭示了生长的 NbOCl2 薄片中 SHG 强度的明确的条状空间分布，跨越厚度从几层到块状结构。这种独特的倍频信号源自反铁电相的存在以及单个晶体内反铁电相和铁电相的共存，这一点可以通过入射角相关倍频测量和拉曼光谱的组合来证明。通过将 NbOCl2 与一维银纳米线集成，我们实现了空间选择性、应变介导的 AFE 到 FE 相变。此外，超低电场（~20 kV/cm）会引起可逆的倍频响应，这与从中心对称反铁电态（SHG-silent）到非中心对称铁电态（SHG-active）的切换一致。这项研究不仅扩展了室温二维反铁电体家族，还建立了动态操纵非线性光的通用平台，为电场可编程非线性光子学和光学信息处理铺平了道路。

English Abstract

In recent years, two-dimensional (2D) ferroelectricity has been extensively investigated across a wide range of 2D materials, while intrinsic 2D antiferroelectricity remains exceptionally rare, limiting its implementation in next-generation photonic and electronic devices. Here, we report the observation of room-temperature-stable antiferroelectricity in the layered transition metal oxyhalide NbOCl2, and demonstrate a reversible, stimuli-driven antiferroelectric-to-ferroelectric (AFE-to-FE) phase transition in this material. Second-harmonic generation (SHG) mapping reveals a well-defined stripe-like spatial distribution of SHG intensity across as-grown NbOCl2 flakes spanning thickness from few-layer to bulk-like configurations. This distinctive SHG signature originates from the existence of antiferroelectric phase and the coexistence of antiferroelectric and ferroelectric phases within individual crystals, as evidenced by a combined incident-angle-dependent SHG measurement and Raman spectroscopy. By integrating NbOCl2 with one-dimensional silver nanowires, we achieve spatially selective, strain-mediated AFE-to-FE phase transition. Moreover, an ultralow electric field (~20 kV/cm) induces a reversible SHG response, consistent with switching from the centrosymmetric antiferroelectric state (SHG-silent) to the non-centrosymmetric ferroelectric state (SHG-active). This study not only expands the family of room-temperature 2D antiferroelectrics but also establishes a versatile platform for dynamic manipulation of nonlinear light, paving the way toward electric-field-programmable nonlinear photonics and optical information processing.

无机卤化物钙钛矿中本征自旋霍尔电导率的起源和空穴密度依赖性#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite、first-principles calculations、spin-orbit coupling。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

金属卤化物钙钛矿具有半导体中最强的自旋轨道耦合，但其中心对称体相是否可以将充电电流转换为自旋电流以及控制响应的因素尚不清楚。在这里，我们通过对四种全无机化合物 CsBX3（B = Pb、Sn；X = Br、I）的第一性原理计算确定了控制其本征自旋霍尔电导率的因素。电导率源自自旋轨道分裂卤化物 p 能带的自旋贝里曲率，比价带最大值低 1.1-2.6 eV，并且当费米能级位于 III-V 族和 IV 族半导体内部时，其电导率超过 III-V 族和 IV 族半导体。由于价带最大值是 s-p 单线态，仅以二阶位移，因此这些贡献在未掺杂的化合物中被抵消，其响应与传统半导体类似。通过空穴掺杂，响应恢复，并且在通过双电层门控 CsPbI3 可达到的密度下，CsPbI3 获得了 p-GaAs 的自旋霍尔电导率。因此，响应是由卤化物 p 带相对于价带最大值的位置决定的，而不是由原子自旋轨道常数决定的，原子自旋轨道常数适用于任何具有轨道单线带边缘的半导体。这些结果表明 CsPbI3 作为钙钛矿自旋轨道扭矩器件的候选自旋电流门控源。

English Abstract

Metal halide perovskites have spin-orbit coupling among the strongest in semiconductors, yet whether their centrosymmetric bulk phases can convert charge current into spin current, and what controls the response, is unknown. Here we identify, from first-principles calculations on the four all-inorganic compounds CsBX3 (B = Pb, Sn; X = Br, I), the factors that govern their intrinsic spin Hall conductivity. The conductivity arises from the spin Berry curvature of the spin-orbit-split halide-p bands, 1.1-2.6 eV below the valence band maximum, and exceeds that of III-V and group-IV semiconductors when the Fermi level lies inside them. Because the valence band maximum is an s-p singlet, shifted only in second order, these contributions cancel in the undoped compounds, which respond like conventional semiconductors. With hole doping the response recovers, and at densities reachable by electric double-layer gating CsPbI3 attains the spin Hall conductivity of p-GaAs. The response is therefore determined by the position of the halide-p bands relative to the valence band maximum, not by the atomic spin-orbit constant, a criterion that applies to any semiconductor with an orbital-singlet band edge. These results point to CsPbI3 as a candidate gate-controlled source of spin current for perovskite spin-orbit torque devices.

吸引晶格费米极化子的相图和长冈铁磁性的不稳定性#

原题：Phase diagram of the attractive lattice Fermi polaron and the instability of Nagaoka ferromagnetism
作者：Gerard Pascual、Kris Van Houcke。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-20
⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉2方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：在方晶格上，有吸引力的极化子不仅与二聚体竞争，还与杨的 η 二聚体竞争，后者是映射到长冈铁磁体上的精确本征态。通过以图解蒙特卡罗为基准的双粒子空穴 Ansätze，我们发现在低填充下基态从极化子演化为二聚体再演化为 η 二聚体，并且超过三相点时，直接从极化子演化为 η 二聚体。填充率高于 25% 时，它仍然是极化子。由于 η 二聚体是精确的，因此它的边界是长冈铁磁性的严格不稳定标准，比之前的最佳边界更尖锐，并且在低填充时为对数边界。

中文摘要

在方晶格上，有吸引力的极化子不仅与二聚体竞争，还与杨的 η 二聚体竞争，后者是映射到长冈铁磁体上的精确本征态。通过以图解蒙特卡罗为基准的双粒子空穴 Ansätze，我们发现在低填充下基态从极化子演化为二聚体再演化为 η 二聚体，并且超过三相点时，直接从极化子演化为 η 二聚体。填充率高于 25% 时，它仍然是极化子。由于 η 二聚体是精确的，因此它的边界是长冈铁磁性的严格不稳定标准，比之前的最佳边界更尖锐，并且在低填充时为对数边界。

English Abstract

On the square lattice, the attractive polaron competes not only with the dimeron but also with Yang's η dimer, an exact eigenstate that maps onto the Nagaoka ferromagnet. With two-particle--hole Ansätze benchmarked by diagrammatic Monte Carlo, we find that the ground state evolves from polaron to dimeron to η dimer at low filling, and, beyond a triple point, directly from polaron to η dimer. Above 25% filling it stays a polaron. As the η dimer is exact, its boundary is a rigorous instability criterion for Nagaoka ferromagnetism, sharper than the previous best and logarithmic at low filling.

基于钙钛矿结构氧化物的[111]取向铁电超晶格的性能#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基于钙钛矿氧化物并涉及单原子取代的十五种独立的[111]取向铁电超晶格（SL）的基态结构——BaTiO₃/SrTiO₃，PbTiO₃/SrTiO₃，PbTiO₃/PbZrO₃，SrZrO₃/SrTiO₃， PbZrO₃/BaZrO₃、BaTiO₃/BaZrO₃、PbTiO₃/BaTiO₃、BaTiO₃/CaTiO₃、KNbO₃/KTaO₃、KNbO₃/NaNbO₃、SrTiO₃/SrHfO₃、BaTiO₃/BaSnO₃、 PbTiO₃/CaTiO₃、SrTiO₃/CaTiO₃和PbZrO₃/SrZrO₃---根据第一原理计算。结果表明，在十一个考虑的 SL 中，基态是铁电的。超晶格结构的一个显着特征是铁电畸变和与八面体旋转相关的畸变均由布里渊区 Γ~点处的声子描述。论证了考虑与八面体旋转相关的结构扭曲的重要性，在某些情况下，八面体旋转会导致 SL 铁电特性的显着变化。比较了在[111]和[100]方向生长的超晶格的热力学稳定性。对超晶格力常数的分析有助于解释超晶格和相应固溶体的铁电性质的特性。

English Abstract

The ground-state structures of fifteen free-standing [111]-oriented ferroelectric superlattices (SLs) based on perovskite oxides and involving a single-atom substitution---BaTiO₃/SrTiO₃, PbTiO₃/SrTiO₃, PbTiO₃/PbZrO₃, SrZrO₃/SrTiO₃, PbZrO₃/BaZrO₃, BaTiO₃/BaZrO₃, PbTiO₃/BaTiO₃, BaTiO₃/CaTiO₃, KNbO₃/KTaO₃, KNbO₃/NaNbO₃, SrTiO₃/SrHfO₃, BaTiO₃/BaSnO₃, PbTiO₃/CaTiO₃, SrTiO₃/CaTiO₃, and PbZrO₃/SrZrO₃---are calculated from first principles. It is shown that in eleven considered SLs the ground state is ferroelectric. A distinctive feature of the structure of the superlattices is that both the ferroelectric distortions and the distortions associated with octahedral rotations are described by phonons at the Γ~point of the Brillouin zone. The importance of taking into account structural distortions associated with octahedral rotations, which in some cases lead to significant changes in the ferroelectric properties of SLs, is demonstrated. The thermodynamic stability of superlattices grown in the [111] and [100] directions is compared. An analysis of the force constants of the superlattices helps to explain the peculiarities of the ferroelectric properties of the superlattices and the corresponding solid solutions.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基于第一性原理计算的磁相互作用可以转化为全局自旋模型（其中相互作用与构型无关），也可以转化为局部自旋模型（其中有效参数明确取决于底层的自旋构型）。尽管这两种观点往往会得出看似不一致的结果，但我们证明，通过将分析限制在横向自旋涨落上，可以使它们协调一致。我们开发了一种投影方案，该方案可系统地从局部相互作用矩阵中去除虚假的纵向贡献，从而在投影的局部相互作用与潜在的全局多自旋哈密顿量之间建立起严密的联系。将我们的方法应用于原型系统（i）Au(111)上的Cr三聚体，（ii）Cu(001)上的Fe和Cr二聚体，我们表明投影的局部相互作用参数定量地再现了从全局四自旋模型中获得的参数。这项工作提供了一个统一的框架，可以在同等基础上解释局部和全局自旋模型，并解决了在非共线框架中识别有效相互作用（如Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI））方面长期存在的模糊性。

English Abstract

Magnetic interactions based on first-principles calculations can be casted either into global spin models, where the interactions are configuration-independent, or into local spin models, where the effective parameters depend explicitly on the underlying spin configuration. Although these two perspectives often yield seemingly inconsistent results, we demonstrate that they can be reconciled by restricting the analysis to transverse spin fluctuations. We develop a projection scheme that systematically removes spurious longitudinal contributions from local interaction matrices, thereby establishing a rigorous connection between the projected local interactions and the underlying global multispin Hamiltonian. Applying our approach to the prototype systems (i) a Cr trimer on Au(111) (ii) Fe and Cr dimers on Cu(001), we show that the projected local interaction parameters quantitatively reproduce those obtained from the global four-spin model. This work provides a unified framework to interpret local and global spin models on equal footing and resolves long-standing ambiguities in the identification of effective interactions such as the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI) in a non-collinear framework.

双层范德华材料系列中 500 K 以上的鲁棒反铁磁有序化#

原题：Robust Antiferromagnetic Ordering above 500 K in a Family of Bilayer van der Waals Materials

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：van der waals materials、density functional theory、magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

对于低维自旋电子学应用来说，非常需要将层状材料中的磁序稳定在室温以上。然而，在层状磁体中实现室温反铁磁性仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们提出了一种层状半导体卤氧化物材料系列，Sr3Fe2O5X2（X = F、Cl、Br），它表现出强大的高温反铁磁有序性。通过原子解决 Sr3Fe2O5Br2 的范德华能隙并合成厘米级 Sr3Fe2O5Cl2 单晶，证明了该系统的二维性质和可扩展性。与温度相关的中子散射和磁化测量决定了它们的 G 型磁结构和转变温度。值得注意的是，Sr3Fe2O5F2 (sim580 K) 的尼尔温度是所有报道的层状反铁磁体中最高的。密度泛函理论计算表明，沿面外和面内方向的强最近邻超交换相互作用，特别是双层基序内沿晶格 c 方向的磁耦合，是 Sr3Fe2O5X2 中尼尔温度高的主要原因。相比之下，双层间自旋耦合非常弱，可以忽略不计，对反铁磁序的稳定性没有太大影响。因此，理论计算预测，即使在单层极限下，这种强大的反铁磁有序也可以在室温下持续甚至增强。我们的研究结果强调铁基层状卤氧化物作为探索二维极限内的超室温反铁磁性的有前途的平台，在信息存储和基于自旋的设备等领域具有巨大的潜力。

English Abstract

Stabilizing magnetic order above room temperature in layered materials is highly desirable for low-dimensional spintronics applications. However, realizing room-temperature antiferromagnetism in layered magnets remains challenging. Here, we present a layered semiconducting oxyhalide material family, Sr₃Fe₂O₅X₂ (X = F, Cl, Br), which exhibits robust high-temperature antiferromagnetic ordering. The two-dimensional nature and scalability of this system are demonstrated by atomically resolving the van der Waals gap of Sr₃Fe₂O₅Br₂ and synthesizing centimeter-scale single crystals of Sr₃Fe₂O₅Cl₂. Temperature-dependent neutron scattering and magnetization measurements determine their G-type magnetic structure and transition temperature. Remarkably, the Néel temperature of Sr₃Fe₂O₅F₂ (sim580 K) is the highest among all reported layered antiferromagnets. Density functional theory calculations demonstrate that strong nearest-neighbor superexchange interactions along both out-of-plane and in-plane directions, particularly the magnetic couplings along lattice c direction within the bilayer motif, are primarily responsible for the high Néel temperatures in Sr₃Fe₂O₅X₂. In contrast, inter-bilayer spin couplings are negligibly weak, without much influence on the stabilization of the antiferromagnetic order. Therefore, theoretical calculations predict that this robust antiferromagnetic order could persist or even strengthens at room-temperature even at the monolayer limit. Our results highlight iron-based layered oxyhalides as a promising platform for exploring beyond-room-temperature antiferromagnetism in the two-dimensional limit, holding immense potential in areas like information storage and spin-based devices.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

层间激子的一个基本特性是其偶极特性，可实现电可调性、增强激子-激子相互作用并延长寿命。它对于研究集体现象和实现新型光电器件具有重要意义。然而，在基于二维半导体的同质或异质结构中，由于空间限制的电荷和能带跃迁的性质，偶极矩的大小和方向通常被锁定。在这里，我们使用少层 R-WSe2 的栅极相关光致发光来利用滑动铁电性来控制新兴层杂化激子的激子偶极子构型。滑动铁电控制能够实现混合偶极激子扩散传输的显着开/关切换，同时进一步促进新兴四极激子的渐进和突然偶极重构。我们的光学探针揭示了垂直排列的界面偏振的中间状态，其大小证实了多层中的累积偏振。总的来说，我们的工作将滑动铁电作为调整层间耦合和层杂化的强大工具。这些结果代表了朝着铁电控制激子互连和新型多位光学存储器迈出的一步。

English Abstract

A fundamental property of interlayer excitons is their dipolar character, enabling electrical tunability, enhanced exciton-exciton interactions and extended lifetime. It is important for studying collective phenomena and realizing novel optoelectronic devices. However, in homo- or heterostructures based on two-dimensional semiconductors, the magnitude and orientation of the dipole moment are commonly locked due to spatially confined charges and nature of the band transition. Here, we use gate-dependent photoluminescence of few-layer R-WSe2 to harness sliding ferroelectricity for controlling the exciton dipole configuration of emerging layer-hybridised excitons. The sliding-ferroelectric control enables a pronounced on/off switching of the diffusive transport of hybrid dipolar excitons, while further facilitating gradual and abrupt dipole reconfigurations with emerging quadrupolar excitons. Our optical probe reveals intermediate states of vertically aligned interfacial polarisation with magnitudes that confirm cumulative polarisation in multilayers. Overall, our work presents sliding ferroelectricity as a powerful tool to tune interlayer coupling and layer hybridisation. These results represent a step toward ferroelectric-controlled exciton interconnects and novel multi-bit optical memories.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、spin-orbit coupling。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们在基于势的约束密度泛函理论 (cDFT) 中提出了一种广义微推弹性带 (NEB) 实现，用于计算同时优化原子、晶格和磁自由度的转变路径。与仅限于原子位移的标准固定单元 NEB 计算不同，我们的框架始终耦合原子力、应力和自旋扭矩。该方法通过将磁矩和广义单元坐标合并到 NEB 优化中，支持共线和非共线磁配置的固定和可变单元方法。为了验证该方法，我们提出了代表性磁结构系统的基准计算。具体来说，我们使用可变晶胞形式研究了 Fe 中的 bcc 到 hcp 转变以及 FeRh 中的反铁磁到铁磁转变。此外，我们还研究了二维 CrI3 中的自旋反转过程，以分析自旋轨道耦合对跃迁路径及其相关能垒的影响。

English Abstract

We present a generalized nudged elastic band (NEB) implementation within potential-based constrained density-functional theory (cDFT) for calculating transition pathways that simultaneously optimize atomic, lattice, and magnetic degrees of freedom. Unlike standard fixed-cell NEB calculations restricted to atomic displacements, our framework consistently couples atomic forces, stresses, and spin torques. The method supports fixed- and variable-cell approaches for collinear and non- collinear magnetic configurations, by incorporating magnetic torques and generalized cell coordinates within the NEB optimization. To validate the methodology, we present benchmark calculations for representative magnetostructural systems. Specifically, we investigate the bcc-to-hcp transformation in Fe and the antiferromagnetic-to-ferromagnetic transition in FeRh using the variable-cell formalism. Furthermore, we examine spin-reversal processes in two-dimensional CrI3 to analyze the influence of spin-orbit coupling on transition paths and their associated energy barriers.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

非晶体旋转对称性提供了一条超越周期性晶体限制的非常规自旋分裂的途径。我们证明，圆偏振光在没有自旋轨道耦合的共线反铁磁彭罗斯准晶体中诱发奇宇称 h 波磁性。由虚拟两步过程产生的光诱导手性跳跃与交错交换相结合，以提高非驱动系统的自旋简并性。准晶自旋群对称性强制奇宇称自旋分裂，并选择 h 波作为其主导角谐波。有效分析准带理论将这种分裂与局部跳跃路径的几何形状联系起来，并预测其在切换光螺旋性时的反转。动量分辨光谱计算揭示了特征性的交替自旋对比度，为此我们提出自旋分辨时间和角度分辨光电子能谱作为实验探针。我们的研究结果建立了一种光学控制高次谐波奇宇称磁性的机制，并证明了准晶序如何将 Floquet 工程扩展到晶体学对称性约束之外。

English Abstract

Noncrystallographic rotational symmetry offers a route to unconventional spin splitting beyond the constraints of periodic crystals. We show that circularly polarized light induces odd-parity h-wave magnetism in a collinear antiferromagnetic Penrose quasicrystal without spin-orbit coupling. Light-induced chiral hopping, generated by virtual two-step processes, combines with staggered exchange to lift the spin degeneracy of the undriven system. Quasicrystalline spin-group symmetry enforces odd-parity spin splitting and selects h-wave as its leading angular harmonic. An analytical effective quasi-band theory relates this splitting to the geometry of local hopping paths and predicts its reversal upon switching the light helicity. Momentum-resolved spectral calculations reveal the characteristic alternating spin contrast, for which we propose spin-resolved time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy as an experimental probe. Our results establish a mechanism for optically controlling higher-harmonic odd-parity magnetism and demonstrate how quasicrystalline order extends Floquet engineering beyond crystallographic symmetry constraints.

扭曲三层结构中的多重莫尔偏振织构#

原题：Multiple moiré polarization textures in twisted trilayers

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

扭曲范德华层因莫尔调制堆叠和遗传的滑动铁电性而呈现出非平凡的极化织构。然而，在任何扭曲双层中，由于给定材料固有的扭曲堆叠格局，极化织构的拓扑结构基本上是固定的。在这里，在一些扭曲三层结构中克服了这一限制，其中相对莫尔相成为额外的结构自由度。以具有不同对称性的代表性滑动铁电体CrI₃和BN为例，阐明了相对莫尔相如何通过层滑动重塑三层堆叠形貌并调节净极化织构。多重莫尔极化织构可归因于两个双层子系统的原始莫尔条纹的干涉。

English Abstract

Twisted van der Waals layers exhibit nontrivial polarization textures from moiré-modulated stacking and the inherited sliding ferroelectricity. However, in any twisted bilayer, the topology of polarization texture is essentially fixed due to the inherent twist-stacking landscape for a given material. Here, this constraint is overcome in some twisted trilayers, where the relative moiré phase emerges as an additional structural degree of freedom. Using CrI₃ and BN as representative sliding ferroelectrics with distinct symmetries, it is clarified how the relative moiré phases reshape the trilayer stacking landscapes and tune the net polarization texture by layer sliding. The multiple moiré polarization textures can be attributed to the interference of original moiré patterns of the two bilayer subsystems.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们从理论上研究了具有可调球形（各向同性）自旋轨道相互作用的超冷费米气体的强耦合特性。在这个自旋轨道耦合系统中，已知即使在仅配对相互作用不能形成这种二体结合分子的耦合状态下，也可以形成称为 rashbon 的二体结合态。在本文中，我们评估了定体积下的比热 C_V，包括在 Nozières 和 Schmitt-Rink 开发的强耦合理论框架内 SOC 存在时的配对波动，以表明这个热力学量对于了解系统特性如何随着自旋轨道耦合强度的增加从费米气体到拉什邦气体的变化非常有用。在超流体相变温度 Tᵣₘ c 下，发现 C_V 在弱耦合费米气体状态和强 SOC rashbon 气体状态之间的中间自旋轨道耦合状态中表现出峰结构。在 Tᵣₘ c 以上的正常状态下，通过对 C_V 温度依赖性的详细分析，我们还确定了系统属性由 (1) 费米原子气体、(2) 稳定 rashbon 的玻色气体和 (3) 波动 rashbon 主导的区域。将这些结果与通过调节配对相互作用的强度实现的传统巴丁-库珀-施里弗（BCS）-玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）交叉物理相结合，我们提出了BCS-BEC和BCS-rashbon BEC交叉现象的统一描述。

English Abstract

We theoretically investigate strong-coupling properties of an ultracold Fermi gas with a tunable spherical (isotropic) spin-orbit interaction. In this spin-orbit coupled system, it is known that a two-body bound state, called rashbon, can be formed even in the coupling regime where a pairing interaction alone cannot form such a two-body bound molecule. In this paper, we evaluate the specific heat at constant volume C_V, including pairing fluctuations in the presence of SOC within the framework of the strong-coupling theory developed by Nozières and Schmitt-Rink, to show that this thermodynamic quantity is very useful to see how system properties vary from a Fermi gas to a rashbon gas with increasing spin-orbit coupling strength. At the superfluid phase transition temperature Tᵣₘ c, C_V is found to exhibit a peak structure in the intermediate spin-orbit coupling regime between the weak-coupling Fermi gas regime and a strong-SOC rashbon gas regime. In the normal state above Tᵣₘ c, from detailed analyses of the temperature dependences of C_V, we also identify the regions where system properties are dominated by (1) a Fermi atomic gas, (2) a Bose gas of stable rashbons, and (3) fluctuating rashbons. Combining these results with the conventional Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS)--Bose-Einstein condensation (BEC) crossover physics realized by tuning the strength of a pairing interaction, we present a unified description of the BCS--BEC and BCS--rashbon BEC crossover phenomena.

A plane-wave auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo study of silicon point defects#

作者：Moritz Humer、Faruk Salihbegovic、Martin Schlipf、Zoran Sukurma、Georg Kresse。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-20
⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉2方向匹配8

📄 摘要：Accurately predicting the energetics of point defects remains a formidable challenge in computational materials science. For the tetrahedral silicon self-interstitial, for example, previous periodic coupled-cluster [CCSD…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：point defects、vacancies。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Accurately predicting the energetics of point defects remains a formidable challenge in computational materials science. For the tetrahedral silicon self-interstitial, for example, previous periodic coupled-cluster [CCSD(T)] and diffusion Monte Carlo (DMC) calculations disagree by about 1.2 eV. Here, we present formation energies of silicon self-interstitials and vacancies using auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (AFQMC) in a plane-wave basis formulated within the projector-augmented wave framework, and compare them with the random-phase approximation (RPA) and CCSD(T). Using 16- and 64-atom cells, we demonstrate that an RPA-based correction effectively removes the two-body finite-size errors that remain after twist averaging in AFQMC for bulk silicon and for the dumbbell and hexagonal self-interstitials. With this correction, we obtain formation energies of 4.4 eV and 4.6 eV for these two self-interstitials in 16-atom cells, in good agreement with DMC and transcorrelated coupled-cluster results. For the tetrahedral interstitial and the Jahn--Teller-distorted vacancy, 16-atom cells are too small to obtain realistic formation energies; in 64-atom cells, we obtain 5.7 eV and 5.2 eV, respectively. With identical setups in 16-atom cells, CCSD(T) yields systematically lower formation energies than AFQMC, with the largest difference, 250 meV, for the tetrahedral interstitial. Our results indicate that the earlier overestimation of CCSD(T) for the tetrahedral interstitial stems from the finite-size treatment in the coupled-cluster workflow rather than from multi-configurational character of the defect.

Oxirane Ring-Opening Dynamics Mediated By The First Singlet Excitation Using Time-Dependent GW Molecular Dynamics#

作者：Aaditya Manjanath、Ryoji Sahara、Thi Nu Pham、Yoshiyuki Kawazoe、Kaoru Ohno。arXiv · physics，2026-11-17
⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉2方向匹配8

📄 摘要：Oxirane (ethylene oxide, ceC2H4O) is a prototypical system for investigating photoinduced bond cleavage and ring-opening dynamics. Previous time-dependent density functional theory studies identified the initial HOMO → L…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics、density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Oxirane (ethylene oxide, ceC2H4O) is a prototypical system for investigating photoinduced bond cleavage and ring-opening dynamics. Previous time-dependent density functional theory studies identified the initial HOMO → LUMO (ceS1) excitation as nonreactive and attributed ring opening to higher-energy excitations. Subsequent constrained density functional theory simulations, however, reported ring opening following an ceS2 → ceS1 transition. Here, we investigate photochemistry of oxirane using time-dependent GW molecular dynamics (TDGW-MD), a many-body approach based on extended quasiparticle theory [J. Chem. Phys. 160, 184102 (2024)]. Our simulations reveal ring opening initiated by asymmetric ceC-O bond cleavage following direct HOMO → LUMO (ceS1) excitation. Following the selected surface hop, separate conditional trajectories on the broken-symmetry open-shell singlet and triplet potential energy surfaces lead to distinct products. The singlet trajectory yields acetaldehyde via intramolecular hydrogen transfer, while the triplet trajectory produces formaldehyde and methylene via ceC-C bond fragmentation. These products are consistent with those identified in previous photolysis experiments. Our results demonstrate that direct excitation to the lowest singlet excited state can initiate ring opening within the present TDGW-MD framework. They further reveal distinct subsequent reaction pathways under the selected spin conditions. This study highlights the potential of TDGW-MD for elucidating photoinduced reaction mechanisms from a many-body perspective.

Band-Edge-Extended Heterointerfaces for Perovskite Solar Cells and Tandems#

作者：Bitao Dong、Meijie Cui、Cong Chen、Ruoting Zhao、Huanxin Ju、Jingwei Xue 等 26 人。Nature，2026-11-18
⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉2方向匹配6

📄 摘要：Perovskite solar cells (PSCs) have emerged as a promising photovoltaic technology, with efficiencies rapidly approaching those of established crystalline-silicon solar cells. Further efficiency gains, however, are increa…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Perovskite solar cells (PSCs) have emerged as a promising photovoltaic technology, with efficiencies rapidly approaching those of established crystalline-silicon solar cells. Further efficiency gains, however, are increasingly limited by charge extraction and recombination at the interface between the perovskite absorber and the electron-transport layer. Interfacial modifiers are widely used to suppress these losses, yet many conventional ammonium-based modifiers are weakly electronically coupled to the perovskite surface and can introduce barriers to electron extraction1-5. Here we realize electronically coupled heterointerfaces by introducing a family of heteroatom-containing, fully conjugated formamidinium interfacial modulators, establishing band-edge-extending conjugated heterointerfaces that overcome the bottleneck of inefficient interfacial electron transfer. The resulting band-edge-extended heterointerface establishes delocalized electron connectivity at the perovskite/electron transporting layer (ETL) interface and suppresses interfacial non-radiative recombination. We report a certified power-conversion efficiency (PCE) of 27.96% and a stabilized power output (SPO) of 27.84% for inverted PSCs. The corresponding devices retained over 95% of their initial efficiency after 2000 h continuous maximum-power-point tracking under 1-sun irradiation (ISOS-L). Extending this strategy to monolithic perovskite–silicon tandems enables a certified SPO of 34.71%, establishing band-edge extension as a broadly applicable approach to overcoming the conventional trade-off between interfacial passivation and charge extraction.

Fully integrated perovskite active polaritonic circuits#

作者：Antonio Fieramosca、Vincenzo Ardizzone、Rosanna Mastria、Eugenio Maggiolini、Laura Polimeno、Umberto Dellasette 等 23 人。Nature，2026-11-18
⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉2方向匹配6

📄 摘要：Photonic integrated circuits are emerging as a key technology for compact, analogue and energy-efficient optical information processing1. Yet, their practical implementation remains limited by the intrinsically weak opti…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Photonic integrated circuits are emerging as a key technology for compact, analogue and energy-efficient optical information processing1. Yet, their practical implementation remains limited by the intrinsically weak optical nonlinearities of conventional materials, which demand high power and large device footprints to achieve substantial nonlinear responses2. Exciton–polaritons, hybrid light–matter excitations of semiconducting materials, offer a promising route to overcome these limitations by combining strong optical nonlinearities with the high speed and scalability typical of photonic platforms3,4,5. However, despite their potential, fully integrated on-chip polaritonic systems simultaneously delivering room-temperature waveguide lasing, controllable optical routing and amplification have remained elusive. Here we demonstrate a fully integrated perovskite polaritonic circuit that brings these functionalities together in a single-step-grown platform. We realize precise and scalable polariton circuits with integrated gratings that act as both couplers and reflectors, enabling in-plane Fabry–Pérot cavities. These structures support robust in-plane polariton lasing with coherent guided emission at a geometry-defined wavelength, optical amplification of propagating polaritons and nonlinear optical phase control exceeding π, all within the same on-chip architecture. Our results establish perovskite polariton circuits as a scalable platform for active and interferometric integrated photonics.

反铁磁双层拓扑的压力和磁场控制#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

控制磁性半导体中的能带拓扑需要将电子结构与实验上可达到的自由度耦合的策略。在这里，我们提出了一种基于 A 型反铁磁双层中的压力和磁重定向的协作方法。反平行层磁化强度抑制了交换极化带边态之间的杂化，限制了它们对层间压缩的响应。对齐层矩的磁场消除了这种自旋失配，从而使压力增强杂交能够驱动能带反转。然后，磁化方向控制反转能带的对称性，以及自旋轨道耦合是否产生陈绝缘间隙或对称保护的节点交叉。双层 CrSBr 的第一性原理计算说明了压缩正交结构模型中的这种机制。面外磁化产生具有手性边缘态的陈绝缘体，而面内磁化则支持具有节点连接边缘态的二维韦尔半金属。连续自旋倾斜进一步证明了磁重取向如何在完全磁饱和之前驱动转变并稳定陈相。尽管特定材料的转变条件取决于压缩下的电子相关性和结构稳定性，但基本策略将层间杂化确定为磁序和能带拓扑之间的联系。这些结果为层状反铁磁半导体中的外部可调谐拓扑相提供了设计原理。

English Abstract

Controlling band topology in magnetic semiconductors requires strategies that couple electronic structure to experimentally accessible degrees of freedom. Here we propose a cooperative approach based on pressure and magnetic reorientation in A-type antiferromagnetic bilayers. Antiparallel layer magnetizations suppress hybridization between exchange-polarized band-edge states, limiting their response to interlayer compression. A magnetic field that aligns the layer moments removes this spin mismatch, enabling pressure-enhanced hybridization to drive a band inversion. The magnetization direction then controls the symmetries of the inverted bands and whether spin--orbit coupling produces a Chern-insulating gap or symmetry-protected nodal crossings. First-principles calculations for bilayer CrSBr illustrate this mechanism within a compressed orthorhombic structural model. Out-of-plane magnetization yields a Chern insulator with a chiral edge state, whereas in-plane magnetization supports a two-dimensional Weyl semimetal with node-connecting edge states. Continuous spin canting further demonstrates how magnetic reorientation can drive the transition and stabilize the Chern phase before full magnetic saturation. Although material-specific transition conditions depend on electronic correlations and structural stability under compression, the underlying strategy identifies interlayer hybridization as a link between magnetic order and band topology. These results provide a design principle for externally tunable topological phases in layered antiferromagnetic semiconductors.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：skyrmion。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们研究了在方格上的非中心对称局域磁矩模型中，晶体场自由度在稳定方形和矩形斯格明子晶格方面的作用。磁性相通过自洽平均场计算来确定。我们首先考虑具有交换和极性型Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用的单个晶体场二重态。我们发现了具有不同磁周期的方形斯格明子晶格，以及自发破缺面内四重旋转对称性的矩形斯格明子晶格。它们的稳定性以及磁场诱导的相序受到晶体场波函数的强烈控制，包括矩形相的重入行为。次近邻交换进一步改变了这些态之间的竞争。当保留第二个晶体场二重态时，会出现具有不同调制模式的额外矩形斯格明子晶格，以及轴向单Q态和各种双Q态。这些结果表明，晶体场自由度为调节多Q磁态的稳定性、空间对称性和拓扑结构提供了一种微观机制。

English Abstract

We investigate the role of crystal-field degrees of freedom in stabilizing square and rectangular skyrmion lattices in a noncentrosymmetric localized-moment model on a square lattice. The magnetic phases are determined using self-consistent mean-field calculations. We first consider a single crystal-field doublet with exchange and polar-type Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interactions. We find square skyrmion lattices with distinct magnetic periods, as well as a rectangular skyrmion lattice that spontaneously breaks the in-plane fourfold rotational symmetry. Their stability and field-induced phase sequences are strongly controlled by the crystal-field wave function, including reentrant behavior of the rectangular phase. Second-neighbor exchange further modifies the competition among these states. When a second crystal-field doublet is retained, additional rectangular skyrmion lattices with distinct modulation patterns emerge, together with axial single-Q states and various double-Q states. These results demonstrate that crystal-field degrees of freedom provide a microscopic mechanism for tuning the stability, spatial symmetry, and topology of multiple-Q magnetic states.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：effective hamiltonian、perovskite、magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

磁序可能涉及更高阶的矩，例如八极子，这在强烈扭曲的晶体中很难识别。在这里，我们提出了单斜黑钨矿 MgReO₄ 和 ZnReO₄ 中主要是八极有序的证据。 μ 子自旋光谱分别检测到 83 K 和 126 K 附近的磁跃迁，两种化合物具有相似的进动频率。从头算有效的哈密顿量表明，强晶体场分裂了自旋轨道四重态，隔离了具有弱偶极特性的低能双态。交换在不同的共线反铁磁结构中选择八极主导的顺序，其中 MgReO₄ 中的力矩沿 b 方向，ZnReO₄ 中的力矩在 ac 平面内倾斜。估计的偶极矩分别为 0.06 μB/Re 和 0.12 μB/Re，这与中子粉末衍射中缺乏解析的磁贡献一致。单离子模型说明了八极态如何产生净偶极矩消失的局部场。这些发现支持了超越双钙钛矿的晶体场途径来实现隐藏磁性。

English Abstract

Magnetic order can involve higher-rank moments such as octupoles, which are difficult to identify in strongly distorted crystals. Here we present evidence for predominantly octupolar order in the monoclinic wolframites MgReO₄ and ZnReO₄. Muon spin spectroscopy detects magnetic transitions near 83 and 126 K, respectively, with similar precession frequencies in both compounds. An ab initio effective Hamiltonian shows that strong crystal fields split the spin--orbit quartet, isolating low-energy doublets with weak dipolar character. Exchange selects octupole-dominated order in distinct collinear antiferromagnetic structures, with moments along b in MgReO₄ and tilted within the ac plane in ZnReO₄. The estimated dipole moments of 0.06 and 0.12 μB per Re, respectively, are consistent with the absence of a resolved magnetic contribution in neutron powder diffraction. A single-ion model illustrates how octupolar states generate local fields with a vanishing net dipole moment. These findings support a crystal-field route to hidden magnetism beyond double perovskites.

从高 Trm_C 自旋无隙半导体到自旋玻璃：无序在 NiMnCrAl 中的作用#

原题：From a High-Trm_C Spin-Gapless Semiconductor to a Spin-Glass: The Role of Disorder in NiMnCrAl
作者：Arka Patra、Tufan Roy、Nilofar Kurawle、Tamaghna Maitra、Souvik Banerjee、Mantu Modak 等 17 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-18

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们将第一性原理计算与结构、磁性和输运测量相结合，研究化学无序对四元 Heusler 合金 NiMnCrAl 的影响。对于有序 Y 型结构，密度泛函理论计算预测总磁矩为 1.99,μB/f.u 的亚铁磁自旋无间隙半导体状态。平均场有序温度约为1300~K。多晶 NiMnCrAl 形成单一平均立方相；然而，组合的 X 射线衍射、中子衍射和扩展 X 射线吸收精细结构测量揭示了明显的反位无序，其特征主要是 B2 类无序以及额外的跨亚晶格混合。在 700~K 温度以下没有观察到长程亚铁磁转变。相反，该化合物在 37~K 附近表现出广泛的磁异常、频率相关的交流磁化率、缓慢的拉伸指数弛豫以及场冷却和零场冷却记忆效应，从而建立了玻璃磁态。电阻率为金属性，塞贝克系数为负，与理想自旋无隙电子结构的损失一致。交换相互作用计算表明，反位无序引入了短的 Mn-Mn 分离和竞争性反铁磁 Mn-Mn 和 Mn-Cr 相互作用，为磁挫败提供了微观基础。这些结果表明 NiMnCrAl 的预测自旋无隙和亚铁磁态对化学无序具有很强的敏感性。

English Abstract

We combine first-principles calculations with structural, magnetic, and transport measurements to investigate the influence of chemical disorder on the quaternary Heusler alloy NiMnCrAl. For the ordered Y-type structure, density-functional-theory calculations predict a ferrimagnetic spin-gapless-semiconducting state with a total magnetic moment of 1.99,μB/f.u. and a mean-field ordering temperature of approximately 1300~K. Polycrystalline NiMnCrAl forms in a single average cubic phase; however, combined x-ray diffraction, neutron diffraction, and extended x-ray absorption fine-structure measurements reveal substantial antisite disorder, characterized by predominantly B2-like disorder with additional cross-sublattice mixing. No long-range ferrimagnetic transition is observed up to 700~K. Instead, the compound exhibits a broad magnetic anomaly near 37~K, frequency-dependent ac susceptibility, slow stretched-exponential relaxation, and field-cooled and zero-field-cooled memory effects, establishing a glassy magnetic state. The electrical resistivity is metallic and the Seebeck coefficient is negative, consistent with the loss of the ideal spin-gapless electronic structure. Exchange-interaction calculations show that antisite disorder introduces short Mn--Mn separations and competing antiferromagnetic Mn--Mn and Mn--Cr interactions, providing a microscopic basis for magnetic frustration. These results demonstrate the strong sensitivity to the predicted spin-gapless and ferrimagnetic states of NiMnCrAl to chemical disorder.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：polaron。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

非晶氮化硅是领先的低损耗、CMOS 兼容平台，适用于晶圆级集成光子学。在该平台内封装过渡金属二硫属化物 (TMD) 单层为可扩展的 TMD 光子电路提供了一条有前途的途径。然而，这种非晶电介质对单层光学响应的影响仍然知之甚少。在这里，我们使用微分反射率和光致发光将封装在非晶氮化硅中的单层 MoSe2 与封装在六方氮化硼中的参考进行比较。虽然参考由中性激子主导，但氮化物封装的单层显示出由 sim31~meV 分隔的吸引和排斥极化子共振，发射由吸引分支主导。将随温度变化的反射率与微观有限温度费米极化子理论进行定量比较表明，氮化物在单层中诱导了大量的电子掺杂，电子密度为(0.8--0.9)×10²~cm⁻²。使用提取的密度和三重子结合能作为唯一的输入参数，该理论再现了高达 sim80 K 的光致发光光谱，与实验具有良好的定量一致性，从而证实了所观察到的激子响应的费米极化子描述。

English Abstract

Amorphous silicon nitride is the leading low-loss, CMOS-compatible platform for wafer-scale integrated photonics. Encapsulating transition-metal dichalcogenide (TMD) monolayers within this platform offers a promising route towards scalable TMD-photonic circuits. Yet the effect of this amorphous dielectric on the optical response of the monolayer remains poorly understood. Here, we compare monolayer MoSe₂ encapsulated in amorphous silicon nitride with a hexagonal boron nitride-encapsulated reference, using differential reflectivity and photoluminescence. Whereas the reference is dominated by the neutral exciton, the nitride-encapsulated monolayer shows attractive and repulsive polaron resonances separated by sim31~meV, with emission dominated by the attractive branch. A quantitative comparison of temperature-dependent reflectance with a microscopic finite-temperature Fermi-polaron theory shows that the nitride induces substantial electron doping in the monolayer, with an electron density of (0.8--0.9)×10¹²~cm⁻². Using the extracted density and trion binding energy as the only input parameters, the theory reproduces the photoluminescence spectra up to sim80 K in excellent quantitative agreement with experiments, thus corroborating the Fermi-polaron description of the observed excitonic response.

当边界改变体积时：动态量子相变中的拓扑边缘模式#

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：对于开链中的动态量子相变 (DQPT)，人们注意到边界可以改变体积：在 DQPT 限制的时间窗口中，Loschmidt 矩阵的指数局域边缘模式会产生密集返回率的有限变化。这些模式归因于初始和最终哈密顿量的拓扑性质。在这里，我们证明保护这些边缘模式的不是哈密顿量的拓扑，而是非厄米洛施密特矩阵的拓扑。特别是，我们证明了拓扑边缘模式可以存在于 A 类中，其中两个哈密顿量都是拓扑平凡的。对于 A、BDI 和 AIII 类，我们证明这些模式受到…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

对于开链中的动态量子相变 (DQPT)，人们注意到边界可以改变体积：在 DQPT 限制的时间窗口中，Loschmidt 矩阵的指数局域边缘模式会产生密集返回率的有限变化。这些模式归因于初始和最终哈密顿量的拓扑性质。在这里，我们证明保护这些边缘模式的不是哈密顿量的拓扑，而是非厄米洛施密特矩阵的拓扑。特别是，我们证明了拓扑边缘模式可以存在于 A 类中，其中两个哈密顿量都是拓扑平凡的。对于 A、BDI 和 AIII 类，我们证明这些模式受到 Toeplitz 算子的 Z 索引的保护，并且 K 分裂定理提供了精确的体边界对应。在 D 类中，它们受到 Z2 索引的保护，该索引记录淬灭是否翻转了边缘的费米子宇称。 D 类的体积不变量可以表示为周期性和反周期边界条件的洛施密特振幅之比，也可以表示为计算动量和时间中的相位涡旋的绕数。我们讨论示例并提供所有四个拓扑类别的数值数据。

English Abstract

For dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPTs) in open chains it has been noted that the boundary can change the bulk: in time windows bounded by DQPTs, exponentially localized edge modes of the Loschmidt matrix produce a finite change in the intensive return rate. These modes have been attributed to topological properties of the initial and final Hamiltonians. Here we show that it is not the topology of the Hamiltonians but rather that of the non-Hermitian Loschmidt matrix which protects these edge modes. In particular, we show that topological edge modes can exist in class A, where both Hamiltonians are topologically trivial. For classes A, BDI, and AIII we show that these modes are protected by Z indices of a Toeplitz operator and that K-splitting theorems provide a precise bulk-boundary correspondence. In class D, they are protected instead by a Z₂ index that records whether the quench has flipped the fermion parity at the edge. The bulk invariant for class D can be formulated either as a ratio of the Loschmidt amplitudes for periodic and anti-periodic boundary conditions or as a winding number that counts phase vortices in momentum and time. We discuss examples and provide numerical data for all four topological classes.

BCS-BEC 跨界交变磁力#

原题：Altermagnetism across the BCS-BEC crossover
作者：Iogann Tolbatov、Luca Salasnich。arXiv · cond-mat.supr-con，2026-11-16

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们研究了一个二维配对费米系统，其中交替磁性产生取决于动量的自旋分裂。相互作用强度通过二体结合能来描述，使得化学势和配对间隙自洽地从弱耦合巴丁-库珀-施里弗（BCS）体系演化到固定密度下的强耦合玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚态（BEC）体系。均匀配对态的稳定性通过给配对一个小的质心动量并跟踪由此产生的自由能变化来检查。我们表明，在零温度下，在整个完全有间隙的交叉状态下，相位刚度遵循通用二次抑制，J/J₀ = 1 - (α/α₀)²。这种关系的偏差仅出现在 BCS 侧，当无间隙 Bogoliubov 口袋打开时，这会迅速降低刚度，并可能引发有限动量配对的不稳定。在 BEC 体系中，这种通用二次校正对应于紧密结合的复合玻色子的有效质量的改变。因此，该模型提供了一个简单的设置来比较交叉的 BCS 和 BEC 侧的交流磁对断裂。

English Abstract

We study a two-dimensional paired Fermi system in which altermagnetism produces a spin splitting that depends on momentum. The interaction strength is described through the two-body binding energy, so that the chemical potential and pairing gap evolve self-consistently from the weak-coupling Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) regime to the strong-coupling Bose-Einstein-condensate (BEC) regime at fixed density. The stability of the uniform paired state is examined by giving the pairs a small center-of-mass momentum and following the resulting change in free energy. We show that at zero temperature, the phase stiffness follows a universal quadratic suppression, J/J₀ = 1 - (α/α₀)², across the entire fully gapped crossover regime. Deviations from this relation emerge only on the BCS side upon the opening of gapless Bogoliubov pockets, which rapidly reduce the stiffness and can trigger an instability toward finite-momentum pairing. In the BEC regime, this universal quadratic correction corresponds to the altered effective mass of the tightly bound composite bosons. The model therefore provides a simple setting in which to compare altermagnetic pair breaking on the BCS and BEC sides of the crossover.

01

高效的机器学习原子间势#

原题：Efficient Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials
作者：Yury Lysogorskiy、Anton Bochkarev、Ralf Drautz。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-18
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 的精度接近量子力学参考计算，而成本仅为其一小部分。效率是多层投资计划的目的，但高效多层投资计划的策略尚未得到系统的审查。本次审查以效率为指导方针。为了在共同基础上比较 MLIP，我们用完整的基础扩展、原子簇扩展 (ACE) 及其图泛化 (GRACE) 来表示它们。该展开式是规范的，由单粒子函数的乘积构建。经典的原子间势（例如嵌入原子方法）和许多现代 MLIP（包括等变变压器）可以在此展开中表示。因此，ML…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 的精度接近量子力学参考计算，而成本仅为其一小部分。效率是多层投资计划的目的，但高效多层投资计划的策略尚未得到系统的审查。本次审查以效率为指导方针。为了在共同基础上比较 MLIP，我们用完整的基础扩展、原子簇扩展 (ACE) 及其图泛化 (GRACE) 来表示它们。该展开式是规范的，由单粒子函数的乘积构建。经典的原子间势（例如嵌入原子方法）和许多现代 MLIP（包括等变变压器）可以在此展开中表示。因此，MLIP 架构的许多明显多样性反映了如何处理非线性、层耦合和优化，而不是模型可以表示哪些交互。我们展示了 MLIP 的成本如何在系统规模上以及在张量分解的固定秩下在模型复杂度上变得线性，并且我们讨论了机器学习的作用，它提供了非线性参数化和优化。由于效率仅在给定精度下才有意义，因此我们还比较了精度和成本。在 Matbench Discovery 排行榜上，三到五层的模型接近更深层次模型的精度，而一个基础（沿着误差和速度的 Pareto 前沿截断）的成本跨越两个数量级以上。最终，训练数据决定了任何 MLIP 的质量。

English Abstract

Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) reach an accuracy close to quantum mechanical reference calculations at a small fraction of the cost. Efficiency is the purpose of MLIPs, yet strategies for efficient MLIPs have not been reviewed systematically. This review takes efficiency as its guideline. To compare MLIPs on a common footing, we represent them in a complete basis expansion, the Atomic Cluster Expansion (ACE) and its graph generalization (GRACE). The expansion is canonical, built from products of single-particle functions. Classical interatomic potentials, such as the embedded-atom method, and many modern MLIPs, including equivariant transformers, can be represented within this expansion. Much of the apparent diversity of MLIP architectures therefore reflects how non-linearity, layer coupling and optimization are handled rather than which interactions the models can represent. We show how the cost of an MLIP becomes linear in system size and, at fixed rank of the tensor decomposition, in model complexity, and we discuss the role of machine learning, which provides non-linear parameterizations and optimization. Because efficiency is meaningful only at a given accuracy, we also compare accuracy and cost. On the Matbench Discovery leaderboard, models with three to five layers approach the accuracy of deeper models, and one basis, truncated along the Pareto front of error and speed, spans more than two orders of magnitude in cost. Ultimately, training data determine the quality of any MLIP.

02

AdsNet：用于直接吸附能量预测的图神经网络#

原题：AdsNet: A Graph Neural Network for Direct Adsorption Energy Prediction
作者：Changzhi Ai、Frank Abild-Pedersen、Johannes Voss。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-20
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：快速准确地预测吸附能对于高通量计算催化剂筛选至关重要，现有方法在评估速度（全图机器学习潜力）或几何灵敏度（基于协调的模型，如 alpha 方案）上进行了妥协。在这里，我们提出了 AdsNet，一种等变图神经网络，可以直接预测单次前向传递中的吸附能，该网络在仅限于吸附原子及其在截止半径内的直接表面邻居的局部图上运行。这种局部图公式将计算复杂性与系统总尺寸解耦，同时对连续几何特征（原子间距离和方向向量）进行编码，从而能够对局部键合环境中的细…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural network、equivariant graph neural network、machine learning potential、density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

快速准确地预测吸附能对于高通量计算催化剂筛选至关重要，现有方法在评估速度（全图机器学习潜力）或几何灵敏度（基于协调的模型，如 alpha 方案）上进行了妥协。在这里，我们提出了 AdsNet，一种等变图神经网络，可以直接预测单次前向传递中的吸附能，该网络在仅限于吸附原子及其在截止半径内的直接表面邻居的局部图上运行。这种局部图公式将计算复杂性与系统总尺寸解耦，同时对连续几何特征（原子间距离和方向向量）进行编码，从而能够对局部键合环境中的细微变化敏感。 AdsNet 采用消息传递架构，在多个交互层上具有标量和向量节点特征。该模型在 AdsTraj（包含 33,920 个密度泛函理论 (DFT) 计算结构的数据集，涵盖 26 种过渡金属和 6 种吸附物（H*、C*、O*、CO*、OH*、HOCO*））上进行训练，实现了 0.032 eV 的吸附能均方根误差 (RMSE) 和 0.047 eV/A 的力 RMSE。通过自动微分获得的解析力可以通过具有自适应线搜索的投影梯度下降算法实现结构松弛，从而消除整个几何优化。此外，AdsNet 独特地将预测的吸附能分解为局部吸附图中各个原子的贡献——这是传统机器学习潜力无法实现的可解释性特征。因此，AdsNet 为各种材料表面的大规模吸附能预测和局部界面弛豫建立了一个准确、高效和可解释的框架。

English Abstract

Fast and accurate prediction of adsorption energies is critical for high-throughput computational catalyst screening, existing methods compromise either on evaluation speed (full-graph machine learning potentials) or geometric sensitivity (coordination-based models like the alpha-scheme). Here, we present AdsNet, an equivariant graph neural network that directly predicts adsorption energies in a single forward pass, which operates on a local graph restricted only to adsorbate atoms and its immediate surface neighbors within a cutoff radius. This local graph formulation decouples computational complexity from total system size while encoding continuous geometric features (interatomic distances and directional vectors) enabling sensitivity to subtle variations in local bonding environments. AdsNet adopts a message-passing architecture with scalar and vector node features over multiple interaction layers. Trained on AdsTraj, a dataset of 33,920 density functional theory (DFT) calculated structures spanning 26 transition metals and 6 adsorbates (H*, C*, O*, CO*, OH*, HOCO*), the model achieves an adsorption energy root-mean-square error (RMSE) of 0.032 eV and a force RMSE of 0.047 eV/A. Analytic forces obtained via automatic differentiation enable structure relaxation through a projected gradient descent algorithm with adaptive line search, eliminating entire geometry optimizations. Additionally, AdsNet uniquely decomposes predicted adsorption energies into contributions from individual atoms within the local adsorbate graph - an interpretability feature unattainable with conventional machine-learning potentials. AdsNet thus establishes an accurate, highly efficient, and interpretable framework for large-scale adsorption energy prediction and local interface relaxation across various material surfaces.
