阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基础机器学习原子间势（MLIP）有望在化学和材料科学的广泛领域实现接近密度泛函理论（DFT）的精度。然而，它们在描述与多相催化相关的体系和过程方面的性能仍未得到充分探索。本文介绍了HetCat26，这是一个基准测试集，旨在评估预训练的MLIP在催化建模的关键方面（包括表面能、金属-金属氧化物相互作用、吸附和催化反应网络）的表现。在对十八个基础模型进行评估后，我们发现，在现有通用材料基准上的表现只能弱弱地预测在HetCat26上的表现；无法从这些通用基准推断出对异相催化的可迁移性。在所有基准测试中，当前模型对表面能量学的描述都很好，而且也许令人惊讶的是，对反应势垒的描述也很好，但在吸附方面则存在较大的误差，而且DFT位点偏好往往无法重现。然而，eSEN-30M-OAM、SevenNet-Omni-i12和MACE-MH-1-OMAT这三种模型在所评估的性质方面均实现了高准确性。除了模型基准测试之外，HetCat26还强调了训练数据一致性的重要性：PBE和PBE+U计算不应在同一个训练集中混用。总体而言，我们确定了限制当前基础MLIP向异相催化以及更广泛地向界面化学反应迁移的挑战，为下一代基础模型的开发提供了指导。

English Abstract

Foundation machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) promise near-density functional theory (DFT) accuracy across broad areas of chemistry and materials science. However, their performance in describing systems and processes relevant to heterogeneous catalysis remains underexplored. Here, we introduce HetCat26, a collection of benchmark tests designed to assess pre-trained MLIPs across key aspects of catalytic modeling, including surface energetics, metal-metal oxide interactions, adsorption, and catalytic reaction networks. Evaluating eighteen foundation models, we find that performance on existing general materials benchmarks is only weakly predictive of performance on HetCat26; transferability to heterogeneous catalysis cannot be inferred from these general benchmarks. Across the benchmark tests, current models describe surface energetics and, perhaps surprisingly, reaction barriers well, whereas larger errors are observed for adsorption, and DFT site preferences are often not reproduced. Three models, eSEN-30M-OAM, SevenNet-Omni-i12 and MACE-MH-1-OMAT, nevertheless achieve high accuracy across the properties evaluated. Beyond model benchmarking, HetCat26 highlights the importance of training data consistency: PBE and PBE+U calculations should not be mixed within a training set. Overall, we identify challenges limiting the transferability of current foundation MLIPs to heterogeneous catalysis and, more broadly, to chemical reactions at interfaces, providing guidance for the development of the next generation of foundation models.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势（MLIP）现在以一小部分成本接近密度泛函理论（DFT）精度，但实际效益受到软件碎片化的限制：每个最先进的架构都作为单独的代码库发布，具有自己的数据格式、配置系统、训练循环和分子动力学接口。因此，在同一数据集上比较两种架构，或者将经过训练的模型转移到生产模拟中，都需要大量的重新设计。我们引入了 IANN（InterAtomic Neural Network），这是一个开源 PyTorch 框架，它重新实现了等变图神经网络 - PaiNN、NequIP、MACE、Allegro、EquiformerV2、EquiformerV3 和 UMA - 背后有统一的数据对象、统一的训练器和单一的部署路线，因此该架构是配置字典中的一个单词的变化。对于每种结构，力、应力和维里都是通过能量相对于原子图边缘向量的一次解析微分获得的，在周期性边界条件下是精确的。分布式训练是从 SLURM、PBS 或 MPI 环境自动配置的，因此相同的脚本从工作站到多节点 GPU 分配都无需更改即可运行。自描述检查点直接用作 ASE 计算器、报告不确定性的集合，或通过捆绑的 C++ 对样式与单 GPU 和多 GPU 推理在 LAMMPS 内部运行的 TorchScript 模型。在包含数百万个结构的通用数据集上进行训练，可以在相同的条件下对架构进行比较。我们进一步发布了 PaiNN 基础模型，该模型在来自 MPtrj、Alexandria、OMat24、MatPES、OC20、OC22、OC25、MPr2SCAN 和 MatPESr2SCAN 的 4190 万个精选 DFT 结构上进行训练，涵盖 PBE、RPBE 和 r2SCAN 泛函。此外，IANN 还提供代理接口以促进与自治代理的集成。

English Abstract

Machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) now approach density functional theory (DFT) accuracy at a fraction of its cost, but the practical benefit is limited by software fragmentation: each state-of-the-art architecture is released as a separate codebase, with its own data format, configuration system, training loop and molecular-dynamics interface. Comparing two architectures on the same dataset, or moving a trained model into a production simulation, therefore requires substantial re-engineering. We introduce IANN (InterAtomic Neural Network), an open-source PyTorch framework that reimplements equivariant graph neural networks - PaiNN, NequIP, MACE, Allegro, EquiformerV2, EquiformerV3 and UMA - behind a unified data object, a unified trainer and a single deployment route, so that the architecture is a one-word change in a configuration dictionary. Forces, stresses and virials are obtained for every architecture from one analytic differentiation of the energy with respect to the edge vectors of the atomic graph, exact under periodic boundary conditions. Distributed training is configured automatically from the SLURM, PBS or MPI environment, so the same script runs unchanged from a workstation to a multi-node GPU allocation. Self-describing checkpoints serve directly as ASE calculators, as ensembles reporting uncertainties, or as TorchScript models run inside LAMMPS through bundled C++ pair styles with single- and multi-GPU inference. Trained on a common dataset of millions of structures, the architectures can be compared under identical conditions. We further release PaiNN foundation models trained on 41.9 million curated DFT structures from MPtrj, Alexandria, OMat24, MatPES, OC20, OC22, OC25, MPr2SCAN and MatPESr2SCAN, spanning the PBE, RPBE and r2SCAN functionals. Additionally, IANN provides an agent interface to facilitate integration with autonomous agents.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、density functional theory。

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

丙烯酸酯环氧化大豆油 (AESO) 的光聚合是一种柔软的生物基材料中自组织过程的便捷模型：紫外线引发自由基链式反应，不断增长的网络释放热量，分子团的迁移率同时在多个时间尺度上发生变化。我们从两个方面跟踪这个过程。实验上，在间歇开/关照明方案下，以 500-5000 Hz 的频率记录了含有 3 mol% 光引发剂 DMPA 的 AESO:VDM (1:0.5) 混合物的介电常数的实部和虚部，并使用滑动窗口香农熵和 Grassberger-Procaccia 相关积分对所得时间序列进行了分析。这两项测量都表明，固化开始时的短光脉冲会触发强相关的集体松弛，这种松弛会随着网络的形成而消失，只留下局部重新定向活跃。我们将这些重新定向描述为定向缺陷，并使用 AESO 片段的五种旋转模式（α、α/β、β、γ、δ）的松弛二面角扫描，通过 MACE-OFF23 机器学习原子间势来量化其能量。与密度泛函理论 (DFT) 参考剖面相比，MACE 再现了所有五个剖面的形状 (Pearson r=0.95-0.98) 和势垒层次 - 从受约束主链旋转的 2.9 eV，通过丙烯酸酯旋转的 0.54-0.81 eV，到甲基的 0.16-0.18 eV 和羟基旋转的 0.06 eV - 同时低估了势垒高度提高 3-20%。简单的阿伦尼乌斯估计表明，丙烯酸酯旋转是最接近介电测量频率窗口的模式。

English Abstract

Photopolymerization of acrylated epoxidized soybean oil (AESO) is a convenient model of a self-organizing process in a soft, bio-based material: ultraviolet light starts a free-radical chain reaction, the growing network releases heat, and the mobility of molecular groups changes on several time scales at once. We follow this process from two sides. Experimentally, the real and imaginary parts of the dielectric permittivity of an AESO:VDM (1:0.5) mixture with 3 mol% of the photoinitiator DMPA were recorded at 500-5000 Hz under an intermittent on/off illumination protocol, and the resulting time series were analysed with a sliding-window Shannon entropy and the Grassberger-Procaccia correlation integral. Both measures show that short light pulses at the start of curing trigger strongly correlated, collective relaxation, which fades as the network forms, leaving only localized reorientations active. We describe these reorientations as orientational defects and quantify their energetics with the MACE-OFF23 machine learning interatomic potential, using relaxed dihedral scans of five rotational modes (α, α/β, β, γ, δ) of an AESO fragment. Compared with density functional theory (DFT) reference profiles, MACE reproduces the shape of all five profiles (Pearson r=0.95-0.98) and the hierarchy of barriers - from 2.9 eV for the constrained main-chain rotation, through 0.54-0.81 eV for the acrylate rotations, to 0.16-0.18 eV for the methyl and 0.06 eV for the hydroxyl rotation - while underestimating the barrier heights by 3-20%. A simple Arrhenius estimate shows that the acrylate rotations are the modes closest to the frequency window of the dielectric measurement.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势（MLIP）以传统电子结构计算成本的一小部分提供近乎从头算的精度，但其可靠性主要取决于训练数据的质量和多样性。在这里，我们提出了一种用于高效数据集生成的经典到密度泛函理论 (DFT) 多保真工作流程，其中长经典分子动力学轨迹辅以选择性 DFT 优化配置来训练神经网络潜力。我们在四个系统上演示了这种方法：散装水、空气-水界面处的水、散装水中的 CaSO 4 和界面处的 CaSO 4 。在所有情况下，经过训练的模型都会忠实地再现参考 DFT 属性，从而在简单和化学复杂的环境中验证工作流程。我们进一步探讨了模型的外推能力，表明当数据集包含目标增强配置时，批量训练的网络可以推广到不同的温度、压力和界面几何形状。因此，这种多保真度策略能够为复杂的溶剂化和界面系统提供准确且可转移的 MLIP，从而缩小计算效率和第一原理准确性之间的差距。

English Abstract

Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) offer near– ab initio accuracy at a fraction of the cost of conventional electronic structure calculations, but their reliability depends critically on the quality and diversity of training data. Here, we present a classical-to-density functional theory (DFT) multi-fidelity workflow for efficient dataset generation, in which long classical molecular dynamics trajectories are supplemented with selectively DFT-optimized configurations to train neural network potentials. We demonstrate this approach on four systems: bulk water, water at the air–water interface, CaSO 4 in bulk water, and CaSO 4 at the interface. In all cases, the trained models faithfully reproduce reference DFT properties, validating the workflow across both simple and chemically complex environments. We further probe the extrapolation capabilities of the models, showing that bulk-trained networks can generalize to different temperatures, pressures, and interfacial geometries when datasets include targeted augmented configurations. This multi-fidelity strategy thus enables accurate and transferable MLIPs for complex solvated and interfacial systems, bridging the gap between computational efficiency and first-principles accuracy.

用于主动学习原子间势的 Stein 核化分子动力学#

中文摘要

机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 可实现高效、准确的原子模拟，但很大程度上取决于训练数据的质量和多样性。我们引入了 Stein 核化分子动力学 (SKMD)，这是一种增强的采样方法，它使用相互作用的粒子动力学来获取用于微调 MLIP 的信息训练配置。 SKMD 对应于 Stein 变分梯度下降的随机变体，通过合并异步粒子更新和全局原子描述符内核来适应分子动力学，这提供了构型相似性的对称感知测量。与分子动力学中使用的其他增强采样器不同，SKMD 将玻尔兹曼分布保持为适当渐近极限内动力学的平稳分布。这一特性加强了对不同配置的探索和对能源景观高概率区域的吸引力之间的平衡。我们进一步提出了一种使用自适应停止标准来高效在线数据采集的方法，该标准在模拟过程中选择非冗余训练数据。我们展示了 SKMD 用于主动学习 Müller-Brown 势的神经网络模型、丙氨酸二肽的 MACE 原子间势，以及具有 DFT 预言机的 MACE 基础模型，用于预测 Si(111) 表面的 Pandey 重建。与主动学习基线相比，我们的方法在更少的训练迭代中实现了更高的模型准确性。

English Abstract

Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) enable efficient and accurate atomistic simulations but depend critically on the quality and diversity of the training data. We introduce Stein kernelized molecular dynamics (SKMD), an enhanced sampling method that uses interacting particle dynamics to acquire informative training configurations for the fine-tuning of MLIPs. SKMD corresponds to a stochastic variant of Stein variational gradient descent that is adapted for molecular dynamics by incorporating asynchronous particle updates and a kernel of global atomic descriptors, which provides a symmetry-aware measure of configurational similarity. Unlike other enhanced samplers used in molecular dynamics, SKMD maintains the Boltzmann distribution as a stationary distribution of its dynamics in the appropriate asymptotic limit. This property enforces a balance between the exploration of diverse configurations and attraction toward high-probability regions of the energy landscape. We further propose an approach to efficient online data acquisition using an adaptive stopping criterion that selects non-redundant training data over the course of simulation. We demonstrate SKMD for the active learning of a neural network model of the Müller--Brown potential, a MACE interatomic potential for alanine dipeptide, and a MACE foundation model with a DFT oracle for predicting the Pandey reconstruction of the Si(111) surface. Compared to active learning baselines, our method achieves higher model accuracy in fewer training iterations.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

氮化铝（AlN）是一种重要的热管理材料，其高晶格热导率受到氧杂质的强烈抑制。我们使用预先训练的通用机器学习原子间势 (MLIP)、分子动力学 (MD) 和声子玻尔兹曼输运计算来研究与氧相关的缺陷引起的声子散射。几个预训练的 MLIP 以声子色散和原始热导率的密度泛函理论为基准。为了平衡精度和计算速度，我们使用紧凑 SevenNet-Nano 模型的微调版本进行 MD 模拟。蒙特卡罗退火支持结合 VAₗ(O_N)₃ 配合物的形成，其散射与其孤立成分的散射不同。从过量光谱能量密度 (SED) 线宽中提取的缺陷散射率与低氧含量下的谐波 T 矩阵预测相当一致，支持独立散射体近似。将这些速率与玻色-爱因斯坦统计数据结合到迭代玻尔兹曼传输方程中，可得到与实验值相当的热导率，并捕获观察到的热导率随氧含量的变化。然而，在高浓度下，散射率偏离与氧含量的线性比例，这表明独立缺陷和原始声子描述的局限性。这些结果证明了一种使用预训练的 MLIP 来量化缺陷限制热传输的有效方法，并提供了对稀缺陷近似之外的杂质影响的深入了解。

English Abstract

Aluminum nitride (AlN) is an important thermal management material whose high lattice thermal conductivity is strongly suppressed by oxygen impurities. We investigate phonon scattering by oxygen-related defects using pretrained universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs), molecular dynamics (MD), and phonon Boltzmann transport calculations. Several pretrained MLIPs are benchmarked against density functional theory for phonon dispersions and pristine thermal conductivity. To balance accuracy and computational speed, we use a fine-tuned version of the compact SevenNet-Nano model for MD simulations. Monte Carlo annealing supports the formation of bound VAₗ(O_N)₃ complexes, whose scattering differs from that of their isolated constituents. Defect scattering rates extracted from excess spectral energy density (SED) linewidths agree reasonably with harmonic T-matrix predictions at low oxygen contents, supporting the independent-scatterer approximation. Incorporating these rates into an iterative Boltzmann transport equation with Bose--Einstein statistics yields thermal conductivities comparable to experimental values and captures their observed decrease with oxygen content. At high concentrations, however, the scattering rate deviates from linear scaling with oxygen content, suggesting limitations of independent-defect and pristine-phonon descriptions. These results demonstrate an efficient approach using pretrained MLIPs to quantify defect-limited thermal transport and provide insights into impurity effects beyond the dilute-defect approximation.

FiuMD：具有神经网络势的粗粒度分子动力学的异质批处理#

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：neural network potential、molecular dynamics。

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

FiuMD 是一款 GPU 分子动力学引擎，即使系统在大小、拓扑、力场参数、周期盒、时间步长或恒温器参数方面存在差异，也可以将许多独立的生物分子模拟作为单个批次进行推进。所有系统都连接成全局张量，因此可以同时评估整个批次的经典交互项和共享的 TorchMD-Net 神经网络潜力，同时每个系统保持自己的积分和恒温器。 FiuMD 最初是 TorchMD 的一个分支，并重新设计了其系统表示、力评估和集成，以处理此类异构批次。我们使用 chignolin、Trp-cage、BBL 和 λ-repressor 的粗粒度 Cα 模型对其进行测试。在双精度下，异质批次中的力与单独的 FiuMD 和 TorchMD 评估一致，在重复评估的精度范围内，60 个系统中的最大相对差异为 7×10⁻16。在没有恒温器的情况下，超过 1000 个步骤，即使周期性图像位于神经网络截止范围内，批处理轨迹与孤立轨迹的偏差最多为 2.5×10⁻11 埃。由于系统中每个系统的目标温度不同，每个系统的动态温度的平均值和宽度与其自身恒温器的预期值相匹配。我们证明，当单个模拟太小而无法填充 GPU 时，批处理可以将吞吐量提高数倍于 TorchMD。与顺序 TorchMD 模拟相比，在单个 GPU 上批处理四种不同的蛋白质系统，每步速度可提高 4.6-7.8 倍。与连续同质 FiuMD 批次相比，异构批次可为较小批次提供高达 4 倍的加速，同时保持较大批次的可比性能。

English Abstract

FiuMD is a GPU molecular dynamics engine that advances many independent biomolecular simulations as a single batch, even when the systems differ in size, topology, force-field parameters, periodic box, timestep, or thermostat parameters. All systems are concatenated into global tensors, so the classical interaction terms and a shared TorchMD-Net neural network potential are evaluated for the whole batch at once, while each system keeps its own integration and thermostat. FiuMD started as a fork of TorchMD and reworks its system representation, force evaluation, and integration to handle such heterogeneous batches. We test it with coarse-grained Cα models of chignolin, Trp-cage, BBL, and λ-repressor. In double precision, forces within a heterogeneous batch agree with isolated FiuMD and TorchMD evaluations to within the precision of repeated evaluations, with a maximum relative difference of 7×10⁻¹⁶ across 60 systems. Over 1000 steps without a thermostat, batched trajectories deviate by at most 2.5×10⁻¹¹ Angstrom from isolated ones, even when periodic images lie within the neural network cutoff. With a different target temperature for each system of the ensemble, the mean and width of each system's kinetic temperature match the values expected from its own thermostat. We show that when individual simulations are too small to fill the GPU, batching can improve throughput several-fold over TorchMD. On a single GPU, batching four different protein systems achieves a 4.6-7.8-fold speedup per step compared with sequential TorchMD simulations. Compared with sequential homogeneous FiuMD batches, heterogeneous batching provides up to a 4-fold speedup for smaller batches while maintaining comparable performance for larger ones.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

从粉末 X 射线衍射 (PXRD) 中确定独特的原子坐标仍然具有挑战性，因为峰重叠、轻元素的弱散射、具有相似原子序数的元素之间有限的散射对比度以及实验噪声使得逆问题无法确定。在这里，我们提出了 XCAPE（XRD 约束加速预测引擎），这是一种晶体结构预测 (CSP) 框架，它将实验得出的成分、晶胞参数、空间群和 PXRD 数据集成到对称感知进化搜索中。 XCAPE 使用定制的突变操作探索威科夫位点配置，通过能量稳定性和衍射一致性评估候选者，并使用通用机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 加速几何优化。该框架从模拟的 PXRD 图案中恢复了 77 个数据库衍生的基准结构中的 71 个（92.2%），并从实验 PXRD 图案中恢复了所有 12 个参考结构。它还进一步确定了 12 个候选结构分配，这些结构分配与实验 PXRD 模式一致，在无机晶体结构数据库 (ICSD) 中缺乏匹配条目，并且在 MLIP 水平上具有热力学竞争力。密度泛函理论 (DFT) 计算表明，这 12 个候选者中有 10 个位于凸包的 10 meV/原子范围内。通过连接实验衍射数据和物理上合理的原子模型，XCAPE 可以提供对以前未解决的材料的结构洞察，并支持自动化研究工作流程中加速材料发现。

English Abstract

Determining unique atomic coordinates from powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) remains challenging because peak overlap, weak scattering from light elements, limited scattering contrast between elements with similar atomic numbers, and experimental noise render the inverse problem underdetermined. Here, we present XCAPE (XRD-Constrained Accelerated Prediction Engine), a crystal structure prediction (CSP) framework that integrates experimentally derived composition, unit cell parameters, space group, and PXRD data into a symmetry-aware evolutionary search. XCAPE explores Wyckoff-site configurations using tailored mutation operations, evaluates candidates by both energetic stability and diffraction agreement, and accelerates geometry optimization using a universal machine-learning interatomic potential (MLIP). The framework recovered 71 of 77 database-derived benchmark structures (92.2%) from simulated PXRD patterns and all 12 reference structures from experimental PXRD patterns. It further identified 12 candidate structure assignments that are consistent with experimental PXRD patterns, lack matching entries in the Inorganic Crystal Structure Database (ICSD), and are thermodynamically competitive at the MLIP level. Density functional theory (DFT) calculations showed that 10 of these 12 candidates lie within 10 meV/atom of the convex hull. By bridging experimental diffraction data and physically plausible atomistic models, XCAPE can provide structural insight into previously unresolved materials and support accelerated materials discovery in automated research workflows.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

相对于密度泛函理论，预训练的通用机器学习力场可以降低有限温度分子动力学的成本。然而，大多数基准测试仅测试静态属性，并且不评估这些模型是否再现了正确的相变序列。在这里，我们使用声子色散计算和可变单元等温等压分子动力学对氧化锆 (ZrO2) 的十个开源通用机器学习力场进行基准测试。氧化锆是一种有用的测试，因为它在熔化前具有三个热力学稳定的结晶相，遵循单斜晶系-四方晶系-立方晶系的顺序。四个模型重现了此序列：7net-l3i5、MatterSim-v1.0.0-1M、MACE-mpa 和 MACE-D3。正交四方能量误差高于约 10 meV/原子的模型（7net-0、CHGNet 和 MACE）反而会产生正交中间体，并预测加热后单斜方四方立方液体。基于 ORB 的模型无法维持稳定的可变晶胞动力学，因为它们的声子频率相对于密度泛函理论参考压缩了 30-60%。在所有十个模型中，两种基态诊断预测有限温度行为。相对多晶型能量学应与匹配的密度泛函理论参考一致在约 6 meV/原子内，并且声子谱不应显示系统软化。这些诊断是评估相变预测的预训练力场的实用筛选指标。

English Abstract

Pretrained universal machine-learning force fields can reduce the cost of finite-temperature molecular dynamics relative to density functional theory. However, most benchmarks test only static properties and do not assess whether these models reproduce the correct phase-transition sequence. Here, we benchmark ten open-source universal machine-learning force fields for zirconia (ZrO₂) using phonon dispersion calculations and variable-cell isothermal-isobaric molecular dynamics. Zirconia is a useful test because it has three thermodynamically stable crystalline phases prior to melting, following the sequence monoclinic-tetragonal-cubic. Four models reproduce this sequence: 7net-l3i5, MatterSim-v1.0.0-1M, MACE-mpa, and MACE-D3. Models with orthorhombic-tetragonal energy errors above about 10 meV/atom (7net-0, CHGNet, and MACE) instead produce an orthorhombic intermediate and predict monoclinic-orthorhombic-tetragonal-cubic-liquid upon heating. ORB-based models fail to sustain stable variable-cell dynamics because their phonon frequencies are compressed by 30-60% relative to the density functional theory reference. Across all ten models, two ground-state diagnostics predict finite-temperature behavior. Relative polymorph energetics should agree with the matched density functional theory reference within about 6 meV/atom, and the phonon spectrum should not show systematic softening. These diagnostics are practical screening metrics for assessing pretrained force fields for phase-transition predictions.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

离解吸水是多相催化和电化学催化的关键步骤，但在有限的镍纳米系统中，OH^*和H^*碎片可以占据表面位点的多种组合，这使得穷举密度泛函理论（DFT）筛选不切实际。我们引入了一种具有无吸附物表示的图神经网络（GNN）框架：候选O和H结合位点由一组Ni原子（锚点）定义，每个位点对被编码为仅由这些锚点及其局部Ni环境构成的图。仅使用MACE弛豫的裸Ni几何结构作为输入，独立的GNN预测OH^*+H^*构型的DFT参考吸附能，以及所提出的结合位点在DFT弛豫后是否会保留，从而消除了在筛选过程中构建或弛豫吸附质的必要性。该能量模型在镍纳米团簇和Ni(111)/Ni(100)表面上进行了训练，在内部验证集上实现了0.031 eV的平均绝对误差，并展示了对未见过的Ni₅₅、Ni₉₁和Ni₁₃₀团簇以及更大表面单元的可迁移性，特别是在相对能量排序方面，尽管定量精度在不同体系之间存在差异。这些独立测试也支持将保留分类器用作保守筛选器，以选择可能保留其拟议结合位点的构型。将其应用于含有多达约3000个原子的镍纳米颗粒，可识别出有利的共吸附基元，并表明较低的O位配位通常能稳定离解态，而H位配位的影响较弱。因此，该框架为筛选吸附能提供了一条高效途径，并有可能扩展到其他金属和吸附质组合。

English Abstract

Dissociative water adsorption is a key step in heterogeneous and electrochemical catalysis, but on finite Ni nanosystems the OH^* and H^* fragments can occupy many combinations of surface sites, making exhaustive density functional theory (DFT) screening impractical. We introduce a graph neural network (GNN) framework with an adsorbate-free representation: candidate O- and H-binding sites are defined by sets of Ni atoms (anchors), and each site pair is encoded as a graph constructed solely from these anchors and their local Ni environment. Using only MACE-relaxed bare Ni geometries as input, separate GNNs predict the DFT-reference adsorption energies of OH^*+H^* configurations and whether the proposed binding sites would be retained upon DFT relaxation, eliminating the need to construct or relax adsorbates during screening. Trained across Ni nanoclusters and Ni(111)/Ni(100) surfaces, the energy model achieves a mean absolute error of 0.031 eV on the internal validation set and demonstrates transferability to unseen Ni₅₅, Ni₉₁, and Ni₁₃₀ clusters and larger surface cells, particularly for relative-energy ranking, although quantitative accuracy varies across systems. These independent tests also support the retention classifier's use as a conservative filter for selecting configurations likely to preserve their proposed binding sites. Application to Ni nanoparticles containing up to sim3000 atoms identifies favorable co-adsorption motifs and shows that lower O-site coordination generally stabilizes the dissociated state, whereas H-site coordination has a weaker influence. The framework thus offers an efficient route to screening adsorption energetics, with potential extensions to other metals and adsorbate combinations.

通过局部扰动验证图神经网络特征归因：CoMoFeNiCu 分解焓的研究#

原题：Validating Graph Neural Network Feature Attribution Through Local Perturbations: A Study on CoMoFeNiCu Decomposition Enthalpy

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural network、graph neural networks。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

图神经网络（GNN）显示出预测材料特性的前景，但可解释性仍然是一个挑战，特别是理解归因特征重要性是否反映了物理现实或统计伪影。在这里，我们提出了一种方法验证研究，该研究故意过度拟合物理感知的 GNN 集合，以探讨特征排序方法的稳健性。使用具有三种化学计量的 CoMoFeNiCu 高熵合金系统，我们训练 GNN 来预测 DFT 计算结构的分解焓，同时显式约束输出权重以编码特征重要性。根据设计，我们的数据集是有限的（大约 900 个二元分解路径和 4,500 个三元分解路径），以控制成分复杂性并能够精确询问局部化学效应。我们验证特征排名通过一系列原子交换扰动捕获有意义的局部环境信息：扰动单个原子位点和重新排名特征揭示了与 GNN 交互截止一致的空间衰减，确认权重编码邻壳效应而不是单独编码全局化学计量伪影。结果表明，只要验证严格，特征归因方法即使在过度拟合的情况下也可以保持物理上的可解释性。这项工作为材料 GNN 的可信解释性奠定了基础，并为扩展到多种化学物质和测试跨组合空间的泛化奠定了基础。

English Abstract

Graph neural networks (GNNs) show promise for predicting materials properties, but interpretability remains a challenge, particularly understanding whether attributed feature importance reflects physical reality or statistical artifacts. Here, we present a methods validation study that deliberately overfits a physics-aware GNN ensemble to probe the robustness of feature-ranking approaches. Using the CoMoFeNiCu high-entropy alloy system with three stoichiometries, we train GNNs to predict decomposition enthalpies from DFT-calculated structures while explicitly constraining output weights to encode feature importance. By design, our dataset is limited (approximately 900 binary and 4,500 ternary decomposition pathways) to control compositional complexity and enable precise interrogation of local chemistry effects. We validate that feature rankings capture meaningful local-environment information through a series of atomic swap perturbations: perturbing individual atomic sites and re-ranking features reveals spatial decay consistent with the GNN's interaction cutoff, confirming that weights encode neighbor-shell effects rather than global stoichiometric artifacts alone. Results demonstrate that feature attribution methods can remain physically interpretable even under overfitting, provided validation is rigorous. This work establishes a foundation for trustworthy interpretability in materials GNNs and sets the stage for scaling to multiple chemistries and testing generalization across compositional spaces.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

分子动力学（MD）模拟可以捕获原子水平上的纳米级断裂物理，但其计算成本阻碍了许多应用所需的长度尺度和时间尺度的预测。另一方面，机器学习方法已被提议作为材料断裂的启发式替代方法，但它们无法保证预测响应的物理一致性。为了解决这个缺点，我们提出了中尺度神经算子（MNO）：一种数据驱动的非局部本构模型，直接从MD加载和位移数据中学习。经过训练后，它可以用于更大域和网格尺寸的连续断裂模拟。 MNO 的关键思想是利用非局部模型的内置特征长度（称为地平线）来跨尺度移动。跨尺度的物理一致性是通过在 MNO 中构建两种结构成分来获得的：标准化内核，使应变能密度在地平线重新缩放下保持不变，以及重新缩放的临界键拉伸，使能量释放率在跨尺度上保持一致。通过对合成数据的测试，我们表明 MNO 及其学习流程确实发现了非局部核和非线性本构关系形式的非局部连续多尺度模型。然后，我们将该框架应用于粗粒度 MD 数据，发现在测试问题中，学习模型与 MD 应变能密度和临界能量释放率的匹配误差在 3% 以内。它还预测分布外拉伸测试中的峰值传递力在 6% 以内。作为进一步演示，该模型应用于面积为训练样本面积 4000 倍且包含初始缺陷的板。在这个问题中，模型再现了动态脆性断裂的主要特征，包括分支和级联分支网络。

English Abstract

Molecular dynamics (MD) simulations can capture the nanoscale physics of fracture at the atomistic level, but their computational cost prohibits predictions at the length scales and time scales needed for many applications. Machine learning approaches, on the other hand, have been proposed as heuristic surrogates for material fracture, yet they cannot guarantee the physical consistency of the predicted response. To address this shortcoming, we propose the mesoscale neural operator (MNO): a data-driven nonlocal constitutive model that is learned directly from MD loading and displacement data. After training, it can be used in continuum fracture simulations on much larger domains and grid sizes. The key idea of the MNO is to exploit the built-in characteristic length of a nonlocal model, called the horizon, to travel across scales. Physical consistency across scales is obtained by building two structural ingredients into the MNO: a normalized kernel that renders the strain energy density invariant under horizon rescaling, and a rescaled critical bond stretch that keeps the energy release rate consistent across scales. Through tests on synthetic data, we show that the MNO and its learning workflow indeed discover the nonlocal continuum multiscale model in the form of the nonlocal kernel and the nonlinear constitutive relation. We then apply the framework to coarse-grained MD data and find that in a test problem, the learned model matches the MD strain energy density and critical energy release rate within 3%. It also predicts the peak transmitted forces within 6% in out-of-distribution tension tests. As a further demonstration, the model is applied to a plate with 4000 times the area of the training specimens and containing an initial defect. In this problem, the model reproduces the main features of dynamic brittle fracture, including branch and cascading branch networks.

中文摘要

现代等变机器学习原子间势（MLIP）极大地提高了原子模拟的准确性和可达到的尺度，但将这些势转化为有限温度相稳定性预测所需的热力学采样方法在计算方面却未能跟上。我们提出了 GPU 原生的大规模并行嵌套采样框架 JAXRENS，它在 JAX 之上实现，消除了阻止原子嵌套采样与最先进的 MLIP 相结合的实际瓶颈。该框架提供分布式多 GPU 执行、步行者矢量化集合以及与现代力场的直接互操作性。我们展示了 JAXRENS 在硬件加速器上的非常有益的扩展，并将其应用于基于 MACE 基础模型的 ZnS 相图模拟，这是以前无法通过计算实现的过程。尽管需要进行 1010 个模型评估，最终的收敛模拟还是在 4 个 GPU 上于六天内完成，这标志着朝着使用机器学习原子间势对有限温度相稳定性进行常规、第一性原理质量预测迈出了重要一步。

English Abstract

Modern equivariant machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have dramatically improved the accuracy of and the scales accessible to atomistic simulation, yet the thermodynamic sampling methods needed to turn those potentials into finite-temperature phase stability predictions have not kept pace computationally. We present the GPU-native, massively parallel nested sampling framework JAXRENS, implemented on top of JAX, which removes the practical bottlenecks preventing atomistic nested sampling from being combined with state-of-the-art MLIPs. The framework provides distributed multi-GPU execution, vectorised ensembles of walkers, and direct interoperability with modern force fields. We demonstrate the highly beneficial scaling of JAXRENS on hardware accelerators, and apply it to the simulation of the ZnS phase diagram based on a MACE foundation model, a procedure previously computationally inaccessible. Despite requiring in the order of 10¹⁰ model evaluations, the final converged simulation completed within six days on four GPUs, marking an important step towards routine, first-principles-quality predictions of finite-temperature phase stability using machine-learned interatomic potentials.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

原子机器学习可以用其计算成本的一小部分来近似昂贵的量子力学计算，但准确性在很大程度上取决于三维原子环境的表示方式。不变表示可以实现快速计算，但可能无法区分几何上不同的配置，尤其是在高度对称的环境中。我们引入了一个轻量级基准来评估 n 重对称配置的表现力，并用它来揭示不变和等变架构中的简并性。在这些结果的指导下，我们为 HIP-HOP 构建了一个新的不变基础，解决了大多数已识别的故障。在甲烷模拟数据集上，扩展基础实现了 HIP-HOP 变体中的最低误差，并且具有与 EquiformerV3 相似的精度，同时推理速度提高了 3.2 倍。这些结果表明，精心构造的不变表示可以减轻关键的表达限制，同时平衡准确性和计算效率。

English Abstract

Atomistic machine learning can approximate expensive quantum mechanical calculations at a fraction of their computational cost, but accuracy depends strongly on how the three-dimensional atomic environments are represented. Invariant representations enable fast computation but can fail to distinguish geometrically distinct configurations, especially in highly symmetric environments. We introduce a lightweight benchmark to evaluate expressivity on n-fold symmetric configurations, and use it to expose degeneracies in both invariant and equivariant architectures. Guided by these results, we construct a new invariant basis for HIP-HOP which resolves most of the identified failures. On a methane simulation dataset, the extended basis achieves the lowest error among the HIP-HOP variants and has similar accuracy to EquiformerV3 while running 3.2× faster at inference. These results show that carefully constructed invariant representations can mitigate key expressivity limitations while balancing accuracy and computational efficiency.

张量积路径何时重要？等变原子间势的群论分析#

原题：When Do Tensor Product Paths Matter? A Group-Theoretic Analysis of Equivariant Interatomic Potentials
作者：Jiawei Wang、Sai Gu、Laurence T. Yang、Junfu Tan、Yemin Yu、Ziheng Lu。arXiv · physics，2026-11-18

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：等变原子间势中的张量积 (TP) 层通过允许对称的路径对角度相互作用进行编码。然而，本地站点对称性可能会导致允许的路径在邻居聚合后不承载任何信号。我们称这种效应为聚合取消。在标量输入第一层中，完整位点对称轨道上的同种邻居共享标量和径向因子，从而减少了它们对球谐轨道和的角度贡献。如果受限的 l 度表示没有微不足道的分量，则对于任何学习的径向权重，该总和以及相应的路径都会消失。对于理想的 Oₕ 八面体轨道，这会删除 l_edge=1,2,3…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

等变原子间势中的张量积 (TP) 层通过允许对称的路径对角度相互作用进行编码。然而，本地站点对称性可能会导致允许的路径在邻居聚合后不承载任何信号。我们称这种效应为聚合取消。在标量输入第一层中，完整位点对称轨道上的同种邻居共享标量和径向因子，从而减少了它们对球谐轨道和的角度贡献。如果受限的 l 度表示没有微不足道的分量，则对于任何学习的径向权重，该总和以及相应的路径都会消失。对于理想的 Oₕ 八面体轨道，这会删除 l_edge=1,2,3 类。在第一层之后，因式分解不再成立，因此不再保证取消；相反，我们要问的是，平均而言，受影响的路径是否仍然不那么重要。我们在 NequIP 中分别测试这两个水平：匹配半径 TiO₆ 探针检查第一层抵消，而模型内 CaTiO₃ 比较和六个 OMat24 模型检查重要性偏差。然后，我们对训练帧上的路径进行排名，在验证帧上选择修剪分数，并在保留测试中评估冻结掩模。在最高评估分数（174 条路径中的 60%）下，CaTiO₃、OMat24 Oₕ-立方和 OMat24 低对称性数据的力平均绝对误差 (MAE) 变化分别为 1.42%、0.04% 和 1.83%。这些掩模意味着 TP 操作潜在减少 67.12-71.94%，边缘多层感知器 (MLP) 潜在减少 59.77%；这些是结构代理，而不是测量的加速。结果将局部对称性与 NequIP 中的路径冗余和修剪鲁棒性联系起来；转移到其他架构仍然开放。

English Abstract

Tensor-product (TP) layers in equivariant interatomic potentials encode angular interactions through symmetry-allowed paths. Yet local site symmetry can make an allowed path carry no signal after neighbor aggregation. We call this effect Aggregation Cancellation. In a scalar-input first layer, equal-species neighbors on a complete site-symmetry orbit share scalar and radial factors, reducing their angular contribution to a spherical-harmonic orbit sum. If the restricted degree-l representation has no trivial component, this sum and the corresponding path vanish for any learned radial weight. For an ideal Oₕ octahedral orbit, this removes the l_edge=1,2,3 classes. Beyond the first layer, the factorisation no longer holds, so cancellation is no longer guaranteed; we instead ask whether affected paths remain less important on average. We test the two levels separately in NequIP: matched-radius TiO₆ probes examine first-layer cancellation, whereas a within-model CaTiO₃ comparison and six OMat24 models examine the importance bias. We then rank paths on training frames, choose the pruning fraction on validation frames, and evaluate frozen masks on held-out tests. At the highest evaluated fraction (60% of 174 paths), force mean absolute error (MAE) changes are 1.42%, 0.04%, and 1.83% for CaTiO₃, OMat24 Oₕ-cubic, and OMat24 low-symmetry data, respectively. The masks imply 67.12-71.94% potential TP-operation reduction and 59.77% potential edge multilayer perceptron (MLP) reduction; these are structural proxies, not measured speedups. The results connect local symmetry to path redundancy and pruning robustness in NequIP; transfer to other architectures remains open.

小粘度下纳维-斯托克斯方程的单层神经网络#

原题：Singular layer neural networks for the Navier-Stokes equations at small viscosity
作者：Teng-Yuan Chang、Gung-Min Gie、Youngjoon Hong、Chang-Yeol Jung。arXiv · physics，2026-11-18

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：我们开发了小粘度 ε > 0 下二维不可压缩纳维-斯托克斯方程的神经网络方法，其精度在 ε 中是均匀的，并且其残差是在不解析涡度边界层的网格上评估的。在具有滑移边界条件的涡度流函数公式中，沿壁形成宽度 sqrtε 的涡度层。奇异层物理信息神经网络（sl-PINN）的试验函数包含渐近分析的校正器，以及固定其在壁上的振幅的定律。幅度与网络本身的壁迹相关，因此该方法不需要极限解的先验知识。对于 Stokes 和 Navier-Stokes 方…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们开发了小粘度 ε > 0 下二维不可压缩纳维-斯托克斯方程的神经网络方法，其精度在 ε 中是均匀的，并且其残差是在不解析涡度边界层的网格上评估的。在具有滑移边界条件的涡度流函数公式中，沿壁形成宽度 sqrtε 的涡度层。奇异层物理信息神经网络（sl-PINN）的试验函数包含渐近分析的校正器，以及固定其在壁上的振幅的定律。幅度与网络本身的壁迹相关，因此该方法不需要极限解的先验知识。对于 Stokes 和 Navier-Stokes 方程，sl-PINN 在 6 个十年内将误差保持在 ε 范围内，尽管在后者中，壁迹随流动传播且事先未知。使用薄尺度但保留层的轮廓和振幅的富集随着ε→0而恶化。由于校正器以分析方式承载该层，因此残差中的拉普拉斯算子可以通过粗网格上的有限体积通量差来离散化。由此产生的单层有限体积神经网络 (sl-FVNN) 在随机种子的分散范围内获得与 sl-PINN 相同的统一精度，而训练步骤的成本约为训练步骤的一半。渐近校正器与粗网格有限体积离散化的结合是本文的主要成果。

English Abstract

We develop neural network methods for the two-dimensional incompressible Navier-Stokes equations at a small viscosity ε > 0, whose accuracy is uniform in ε and whose residual is evaluated on a grid that does not resolve the vorticity boundary layer. In the vorticity-stream function formulation with the slip boundary conditions, a vorticity layer of width sqrtε forms along the walls. The trial function of the singular layer Physics-Informed Neural Network (sl-PINN) contains the corrector of the asymptotic analysis, together with the law that fixes its amplitude at the wall. The amplitude is tied to the wall trace of the network itself, so that the method requires no a priori knowledge of the limit solution. The sl-PINN keeps the error uniform in ε over six decades, for the Stokes and the Navier-Stokes equations alike, although in the latter the wall trace travels with the flow and is not known in advance. An enrichment that uses the thin scale but leaves the profile and the amplitude of the layer free deteriorates as ε→0. Since the corrector carries the layer analytically, the Laplacian in the residual can be discretized by a finite volume flux difference on a coarse grid. The resulting singular layer Finite Volume Neural Network (sl-FVNN) attains the same uniform accuracy as the sl-PINN, to within the scatter of the random seed, at about half the cost of a training step. The combination of the asymptotic corrector with the coarse-grid finite volume discretization is the main result of this article.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们引入了一种多输出前馈神经网络代理，在耦合多重反射和双温度模型上进行训练，以将晶体硅薄膜的双脉冲飞秒激光激发（1030 nm）映射到熔融石英上，跨越薄膜厚度（50-500 nm）和脉冲间延迟（0-10ps）。通过对 2000 个物理模拟进行 Levenberg-Marquardt 优化训练，双隐藏层网络（[40,30] 神经元）同时预测六个强耦合光热可观测值、峰值电子温度、峰值晶格温度、峰值载流子密度、最小和最大吸收率以及最小折射率，在所有目标上实现大于 0.9998 的决定系数 R2。在毫秒内评估密集的 200 x 200 设计网格可解决明显的薄膜干涉条纹和载流子引起的折射率调制，确定 d=156 nm 和 Dt=4.572 ps 时的最佳光热耦合机制。这种统一的机器学习工作流程消除了实时激光加工控制和反光子薄膜设计中计算成本高昂的多物理运行时瓶颈。

English Abstract

We introduce a multi-output feedforward neural network surrogate trained on a coupled multiple-reflection and two-temperature model to map double-pulse femtosecond laser excitation (1030 nm) of crystalline silicon films on fused silica across film thicknesses (50-500 nm) and inter-pulse delays (0-10ps). Trained via Levenberg-Marquardt optimization on 2000 physical simulations, the dual-hidden-layer network ([40,30] neurons) simultaneously predicts six strongly coupled optothermal observables, peak electron temperature, peak lattice temperature, peak carrier density, minimum and maximum absorptivity and minimum refractive index achieving coefficients of determination R2 larger than 0.9998 across all targets. Evaluating a dense 200 by 200 design grid in milliseconds resolves pronounced thin-film interference fringes and carrier-induced index modulation, identifying an optimal optothermal coupling regime at d=156 nm and Dt=4.572 ps. This unified machine-learning workflow eliminates computationally expensive multi-physics runtime bottlenecks for real-time laser processing control and inverse photonic thin-film design.

通用机器学习原子间势中的局部拓扑幻觉#

原题：Local topology hallucinations in universal machine-learned interatomic potentials
作者：Xiaoqing Liu、Yangshuai Wang、Lei Shen。arXiv · physics，2026-11-17

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

使用通用机器学习原子间势来预测亚稳态需要它们保留参考势能表面的最小值和鞍座。在这里，我们识别了局部拓扑幻觉，其中不同的模型最小值松弛到相同的参考盆地中。此故障是由 MACE、CHGNet 和 M3GNet 共享的模式选择性曲率符号错误引起的。三个模型中的两个模型预测的曲率符号在 57 个正案例中的 55 个中与密度泛函计算结果一致，但在 56 个负案例中只有 5 个与密度泛函计算结果一致。在 LiGe2S 中，通过经过验证的鞍座连接的两个 MACE 最小值松弛到一个密度函数盆地中。参考松弛同样将十个选定的模型最小值中的九个返回到它们的参考盆地。使用两个位移参考计算进行的定向测试拒绝了 12 个前瞻性测试模型不稳定性中的 9 个。因此，仅模型协议无法确定预测的最小值是不同的亚稳态。

English Abstract

Predicting metastable states with universal machine-learned interatomic potentials requires that they preserve the minima and saddles of the reference potential-energy surface. Here we identify local topology hallucination, in which distinct model minima relax into the same reference basin. This failure arises from mode-selective curvature-sign errors shared by MACE, CHGNet and M3GNet. Curvature signs predicted by two of the three models agreed with density-functional calculations in 55 of 57 positive but only 5 of 56 negative cases. In LiGe₂S, two MACE minima connected by a verified saddle relaxed into one density-functional basin. Reference relaxation likewise returned nine of ten selected model minima to their reference basins. A directional test with two displaced reference calculations rejected 9 of 12 prospectively tested model instabilities. Model agreement alone therefore cannot establish that predicted minima are distinct metastable states.

自注意力是多体机器学习的戴森方程#

原题：Self-attention is a Dyson equation for many-body machine learning
作者：Jarvist Moore Frost。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-17
🤖 AI/机器学习

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：为什么对称求和池 (DeepSets) 可以将基态电势预测到亚兆伏级精度，但无法预测相关波函数和激发态？和池是一种平均场 (Hartree) 模型：其 O(1/N) 非对角雅可比行列式足以满足近视基态能量，但消除了非局部二体相关性。我们证明自注意力是一个实空间戴森迭代，其玻尔兹曼（softmax）内核充当二体自能。由于物理哈密顿量是成对起作用的，Löwdin 的 2-RDM 定理和 Galitskii-Migdal 关系保证了迭代二体传…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

为什么对称求和池 (DeepSets) 可以将基态电势预测到亚兆伏级精度，但无法预测相关波函数和激发态？和池是一种平均场 (Hartree) 模型：其 O(1/N) 非对角雅可比行列式足以满足近视基态能量，但消除了非局部二体相关性。我们证明自注意力是一个实空间戴森迭代，其玻尔兹曼（softmax）内核充当二体自能。由于物理哈密顿量是成对起作用的，Löwdin 的 2-RDM 定理和 Galitskii-Migdal 关系保证了迭代二体传播器可以捕获相关物质的完整能量学。当拟合分形回流行列式时，DeepSets 将节点表面模糊到平均场包络上，而自注意力则恢复分形吸引子。从这个角度来看，注意力的不合理有效性并非偶然；它是一种偶然现象。在由成对相互作用构建的宇宙中，你所需要的就是二体注意力。

English Abstract

Why does symmetric sum-pooling (DeepSets) predict ground-state potentials to sub-meV accuracy, yet fail for correlated wavefunctions and excited states? Sum-pooling is a mean-field (Hartree) model: its O(1/N) off-diagonal Jacobian suffices for nearsighted ground-state energies, but erases nonlocal two-body correlations. We show that self-attention is a real-space Dyson iteration whose Boltzmann (softmax) kernel acts as a two-body self-energy. Because physical Hamiltonians act pairwise, Löwdin's 2-RDM theorem and the Galitskii--Migdal relation guarantee that iterating a two-body propagator captures the full energetics of correlated matter. When fitting a fractal backflow determinant, DeepSets blurs the nodal surface onto a mean-field envelope, whereas self-attention recovers the fractal attractor. Seen through this lens, the unreasonable effectiveness of attention is not an accident; in a universe built from pairwise interactions, two-body attention is all you need.

强度表面的机器学习可实现高正则化长度相场断裂#

原题：Machine learning of strength surfaces enables high regularization length phase-field fracture
作者：S Chockalingam、Aditya Kumar、Adrian Buganza Tepole。arXiv · cond-mat.supr-con，2026-11-16
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配5

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：脆性断裂的经典相场模型捕获了韧性控制的裂纹扩展，但没有捕获控制裂纹形核的材料强度表面。 Kumar 等人的理论。 (2020) 通过成核驱动力合并强度表面，Chockalingam (2025) 导出了由 n 个材料参数描述的任意强度表面的驱动力，其中相场强度表面 (PFSS) 以正则化长度 ε → 0 收敛到材料强度表面，并在任何 ε 的 n 个选定状态下精确匹配。然而，在实践中，强度表面必须以分析形式提供，并根据具体情况进行选择和校…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

脆性断裂的经典相场模型捕获了韧性控制的裂纹扩展，但没有捕获控制裂纹形核的材料强度表面。 Kumar 等人的理论。 (2020) 通过成核驱动力合并强度表面，Chockalingam (2025) 导出了由 n 个材料参数描述的任意强度表面的驱动力，其中相场强度表面 (PFSS) 以正则化长度 ε → 0 收敛到材料强度表面，并在任何 ε 的 n 个选定状态下精确匹配。然而，在实践中，强度表面必须以分析形式提供，并根据具体情况进行选择和校准，并且对于广泛使用的分析表面 (n=2)，精确的强度再现需要 ε 与欧文特征长度 lcₕ 相称，这对大型结构提出了严格的网格要求。我们引入了一种神经网络（NN）强度函数，它直接从强度数据中学习，受到构造的物理约束，并且提供比解析表面更多的校准参数；校准可推广到任意数量的强度状态，并在选定的方向上强制开放。单一架构可捕获 Mohr-Coulomb、Drucker-Prager、Mogi-Coulomb 和 3D Hoek-Brown 表面的合成数据以及石墨、天然橡胶和丁苯橡胶的实验数据。所得的 PFSS 精确度高达 ε = 10,lcₕ，而精确分析表面的性能即使在 ε = 0.5,lcₕ 时也相对较差。有限元模拟验证了跨断裂状态的理论。通过将强度保真度与正则化长度解耦，该方法大大降低了大型结构相场断裂模拟所需的网格分辨率。

English Abstract

Classical phase-field models of brittle fracture capture toughness-controlled crack growth but not the material's strength surface, which governs crack nucleation. The theory of Kumar et al. (2020) incorporates the strength surface through a nucleation driving force, and Chockalingam (2025) derived this driving force for arbitrary strength surfaces described by n material parameters, with which the phase-field strength surface (PFSS) converges to the material strength surface as the regularization length ε → 0 and matches it exactly at n chosen states for any ε. In practice, however, the strength surface must be supplied as an analytic form chosen and calibrated case by case, and for the widely used analytic surfaces (n=2) accurate strength reproduction requires ε commensurate with the Irwin characteristic length lcₕ, imposing prohibitive mesh requirements for large structures. We introduce a neural-network (NN) strength function that is learned directly from strength data, is physics-constrained by construction, and provides far more calibration parameters than analytic surfaces; the calibration is generalized to any number of strength states and to enforce openness in chosen directions. A single architecture captures synthetic data from the Mohr-Coulomb, Drucker-Prager, Mogi-Coulomb and 3D Hoek-Brown surfaces and experimental data for graphite, natural rubber and styrene-butadiene rubber. The resulting PFSS is accurate up to ε = 10,lcₕ, whereas that of the exact analytic surfaces performs comparatively poorly even at ε = 0.5,lcₕ. Finite-element simulations validate the theory across fracture regimes. By decoupling strength fidelity from the regularization length, the approach substantially reduces the mesh resolution required for phase-field fracture simulations of large structures.

用于多模态 X 射线和中子散射反演的组合神经网络#

原题：Compositional neural networks for multimodal X-ray and neutron scattering inversion
作者：Valentin Munteanu、Alexander Hinderhofer、Dmitry Lapkin、Vladimir Starostin、Frank Schreiber。arXiv · physics，2026-11-18

AI×科学交叉8方向匹配5

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：X 射线和中子散射对于凝聚态物理和材料科学的结构表征是不可或缺的。然而，由于散射实验通常记录没有相位信息的强度，不同的结构可以产生相似的散射图案，从而使得相应的反演问题不唯一。通过组合互补测量可以减少这种模糊性，但将它们集成到机器学习框架中仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们介绍了一种基于先验摊销流量匹配的概率多测量分析的模块化框架。单观测后验估计器针对各个观测类型或物理探针单独进行训练，并仅在推理时通过组合采样器进行组合。这种后期融合方法允许…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

X 射线和中子散射对于凝聚态物理和材料科学的结构表征是不可或缺的。然而，由于散射实验通常记录没有相位信息的强度，不同的结构可以产生相似的散射图案，从而使得相应的反演问题不唯一。通过组合互补测量可以减少这种模糊性，但将它们集成到机器学习框架中仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们介绍了一种基于先验摊销流量匹配的概率多测量分析的模块化框架。单观测后验估计器针对各个观测类型或物理探针单独进行训练，并仅在推理时通过组合采样器进行组合。这种后期融合方法允许重复使用预训练的单观察模型，而无需专门的联合再训练。作为一个恰当的例子，我们考虑反射测量法，它提供有关薄膜和界面的深度分辨纳米级信息。我们的单次观测演示涵盖 X 射线、飞行时间中子和偏振中子反射计。在单独的协同细化研究中，任务匹配的估计器可以实现跨多个中子对比的均匀成分以及 X 射线和中子测量的异构多模态成分。总之，这些结果将成分流匹配建立为不确定性感知反演和协同细化的模块化方法，并具有对其他散射问题的潜在适用性，在其他散射问题中，多个观测值约束共同的物理结构，同时保留特定于测量的参数。

English Abstract

X-ray and neutron scattering are indispensable for structural characterization across condensed-matter physics and materials science. Yet, because scattering experiments generally record intensities without phase information, distinct structures can yield similar scattering patterns, rendering the corresponding inverse problems non-unique. This ambiguity can be reduced by combining complementary measurements, but their integration into machine-learning frameworks remains challenging. Here we introduce a modular framework for probabilistic multi-measurement analysis based on prior-amortized flow matching. Single-observation posterior estimators are trained separately for individual observation types or physical probes and combined only at inference time through a compositional sampler. This late fusion approach allows pretrained single-observation models to be reused without dedicated joint retraining. As a case in point, we consider reflectometry, which provides depth-resolved, nanometer-scale information about thin films and interfaces. Our single-observation demonstrations span X-ray, time-of-flight neutron and polarized neutron reflectometry. In separate co-refinement studies, task-matched estimators enable homogeneous composition across multiple neutron contrasts and heterogeneous multimodal composition of X-ray and neutron measurements. Together, these results establish compositional flow matching as a modular approach to uncertainty-aware inversion and co-refinement, with potential applicability to other scattering problems in which multiple observations constrain a common physical structure while retaining measurement-specific parameters.

通过基于神经网络的多信号谐波处理增强超光谱纳米FTIR成像#

原题：Enhanced Hyperspectral Nano-FTIR Imaging by Neural-Network-Based Processing of Multiple Signal Harmonics
作者：Pablo Calvo-Barlés、Sergio G. Rodrigo、Luis Martín-Moreno、Martin Schnell、Rainer Hillenbrand。arXiv · physics，2026-11-16

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：纳米 FTIR 光谱将红外光谱与纳米级空间分辨率相结合，实现高光谱纳米成像。然而，定量测量通常依赖于高次谐波近场信号，这些信号基本上没有背景，但信噪比 (SNR) 较低。较低的谐波可提供更高的信噪比，但包含显着的背景影响，会扭曲近场响应并阻碍定量分析。在这里，我们引入了一种交叉谐波神经网络（CHNN），它利用多个谐波通道中编码的互补信息来对三次谐波高光谱数据进行去噪。 CHNN 直接在实验纳米 FTIR 数据集上进行训练，使用一次和二次…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

纳米 FTIR 光谱将红外光谱与纳米级空间分辨率相结合，实现高光谱纳米成像。然而，定量测量通常依赖于高次谐波近场信号，这些信号基本上没有背景，但信噪比 (SNR) 较低。较低的谐波可提供更高的信噪比，但包含显着的背景影响，会扭曲近场响应并阻碍定量分析。在这里，我们引入了一种交叉谐波神经网络（CHNN），它利用多个谐波通道中编码的互补信息来对三次谐波高光谱数据进行去噪。 CHNN 直接在实验纳米 FTIR 数据集上进行训练，使用一次和二次谐波光谱来提高三次谐波响应的 SNR。应用于 PMMA/PS 聚合物混合物时，它可以显着降低噪声，同时保留光谱特征和纳米级空间对比度。与使用自动编码器的传统单谐波去噪相比，CHNN 实现了优异的噪声抑制。这些结果证明了低谐波信号在不增加采集时间的情况下改善高光谱纳米 FTIR 成像的潜力。

English Abstract

Nano-FTIR spectroscopy combines infrared spectroscopy with nanometer-scale spatial resolution and enables hyperspectral nanoimaging. However, quantitative measurements typically rely on higher-harmonic near-field signals, which are largely background-free but suffer from low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Lower harmonics provide substantially higher SNR but contain significant background contributions that distort the near-field response and hinder quantitative analysis. Here, we introduce a cross-harmonic neural network (CHNN) that exploits the complementary information encoded in multiple harmonic channels to denoise third-harmonic hyperspectral data. Trained directly on experimental nano-FTIR datasets, the CHNN uses first- and second-harmonic spectra to improve the SNR of the third-harmonic response. Applied to a PMMA/PS polymer blend, it substantially reduces noise while preserving spectral features and nanoscale spatial contrast. Compared with conventional single-harmonic denoising using an autoencoder, the CHNN achieves superior noise suppression. These results demonstrate the potential of lower-harmonic signals for improving hyperspectral nano-FTIR imaging without increasing acquisition time.

原题：Fine-tuned atomistic foundation model for accurate sodium-carbon chemistry in Na-ion batteries
作者：Andres Felipe Usuga、Ihor Radchenko、Yuxin Tang、Oleksandr I. Malyi。arXiv · physics，2026-11-16

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：原子基础模型已成为复杂化学环境下材料模拟的 DFT 级精度的有前途的途径。然而，它们在竞争键合体系中的可靠性，以及更重要的是，复杂键合如何影响材料性能的预测仍不清楚。在具有竞争键合机制的材料中，硬碳中的钠存储提出了重大挑战。硬碳具有多种局部结构图案，包括有缺陷和弯曲的石墨区域、无定形区域和闭孔/开孔。由于其对钠离子电池的重要性，我们基于不同局部 Na/C 主题的 MACE 架构系统地评估了现有的预训练基础模型。我们发现预训练模型无法准确…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

原子基础模型已成为复杂化学环境下材料模拟的 DFT 级精度的有前途的途径。然而，它们在竞争键合体系中的可靠性，以及更重要的是，复杂键合如何影响材料性能的预测仍不清楚。在具有竞争键合机制的材料中，硬碳中的钠存储提出了重大挑战。硬碳具有多种局部结构图案，包括有缺陷和弯曲的石墨区域、无定形区域和闭孔/开孔。由于其对钠离子电池的重要性，我们基于不同局部 Na/C 主题的 MACE 架构系统地评估了现有的预训练基础模型。我们发现预训练模型无法准确捕获石墨烯上金属钠簇的 Na-Na 和 Na-C 相互作用。为了恢复 DFT 水平的能量描述，我们采用了有针对性的微调，表明它可以描述局部图案的能量稳定性，并实现对不可见的 Na/C 环境的足够可转移性。通过开发的微调模型，我们建立了硬碳闭孔半径与平台电压之间的相关性，表明小孔比大孔在更大的电压下贡献，从而表明开发的微调基础模型如何将原子信息与复杂键合环境的材料设计联系起来。

English Abstract

Atomistic foundation models have emerged as a promising route to DFT-level accuracy for materials simulation with complex chemical environments. However, their reliability across competing bonding regimes and, more importantly, how complex bonding affects predictions of material properties remain unclear. Among materials with competing bonding regimes, sodium storage in hard carbon poses a substantial challenge. Hard carbon features diverse local structural motifs, including defective and curved graphitic regions, amorphous regions, and closed/open pores. Motivated by its importance for Na-ion batteries, we systematically evaluate existing pretrained foundation models based on the MACE architecture across different local Na/C motifs. We find that the pretrained models fail to accurately capture Na-Na and Na-C interactions for metallic Na clusters on graphene. To recover a DFT-level energetic description, we employ targeted fine-tuning, showing that it can describe the energetic stability of local motifs and achieve sufficient transferability to unseen Na/C environments. With the developed fine-tuned model, we establish a correlation between closed-pore radius in hard carbon and plateau voltage, showing that the small pores contribute at larger voltages than large ones, suggesting thus how the developed fine-tuned foundation model can link atomistic information with materials design for complex bonding environments.

用于晶体生成模型推理时间自适应的扩散树采样#

原题：Diffusion Tree Sampling for Inference Time Adaptation of Crystal Generative Models
作者：Daniel T. Levy、Vineet Jain、Tara Akhound-Sadegh、Sékou-Oumar Kaba、Siamak Ravanbakhsh。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-11-16

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：基于扩散的生成模型已经成为优于合理晶体结构的强大先验，但将先验转变为有希望的候选模型需要同时转向几个相互冲突的属性。微调成本高昂，并且会为每个新目标生成一个新模型，而可微分指导不适用于许多现实世界的任务。相反，我们使用扩散树采样（DTS）在推理时调整这些生成模型，这是一种针对去噪轨迹的蒙特卡洛树搜索过程。我们在 DTS 之上做出了两项与 AI 科学应用特别相关的技术贡献。首先，我们展示如何使用现有的属性条件生成模型（例如，为无分类器指导…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基于扩散的生成模型已经成为优于合理晶体结构的强大先验，但将先验转变为有希望的候选模型需要同时转向几个相互冲突的属性。微调成本高昂，并且会为每个新目标生成一个新模型，而可微分指导不适用于许多现实世界的任务。相反，我们使用扩散树采样（DTS）在推理时调整这些生成模型，这是一种针对去噪轨迹的蒙特卡洛树搜索过程。我们在 DTS 之上做出了两项与 AI 科学应用特别相关的技术贡献。首先，我们展示如何使用现有的属性条件生成模型（例如，为无分类器指导而微调的模型）作为 DTS 内的指导建议，而不扭曲目标分布。其次，我们将 DTS 扩展到多目标采样，以便单个搜索产生跨越整个 Pareto 前沿的样本。我们实例化了从头晶体生成的两种适应：提高不同模型生成的晶体的稳定性，使用预训练的条件模型生成属于稀有空间群或具有高体积模量的晶体，并沿着高介电常数和高带隙材料的帕累托前沿生成晶体。在这些任务中，基于 DTS 的推理时间自适应成功地改进了模型输出，而无需修改模型本身。

English Abstract

Diffusion-based generative models have become strong priors over plausible crystal structures, but turning that prior into promising candidates requires steering toward several conflicting properties at once. Fine-tuning is expensive and produces a new model for every new objective, while differentiable guidance is not applicable to many real-world tasks. We instead adapt these generative models at inference time using Diffusion Tree Sampling (DTS), a Monte Carlo tree search procedure over the denoising trajectory. We make two technical contributions on top of DTS that are particularly relevant for AI-for-science applications. First, we show how to use existing property-conditional generative models (e.g., models fine-tuned for classifier-free guidance) as guided proposals inside DTS, without distorting the target distribution. Second, we extend DTS to multi-objective sampling, so that a single search produces samples spanning an entire Pareto front. We instantiate both adaptations on de novo crystal generation: improving the stability of crystals generated by different models, using pretrained conditional models to generate crystals belonging to rare space groups or with high bulk modulus, and generating crystals along a Pareto front of high dielectric constant and high bandgap materials. Across these tasks, DTS-based inference-time adaptation successfully improves model outputs without modifying the models themselves.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

由多伦多大学航空航天团队的空间系统部门开发的用于农作物残留物高光谱测绘（FINCH）的现场成像纳米卫星的光谱范围对高光谱分解以确定农作物残留物覆盖分数丰度提出了重大挑战。标准指数的严重缺乏需要使用复杂的、数据驱动的分解模型。复杂的分解模型需要密集、分布均匀的流形，而真实数据集稀疏且昂贵。为了更好地利用现有地面实况数据集中的信息内容，本研究提出了一个框架，通过中间条件数据生成器将插值和丰度映射估计解耦，与传统的单模型分解管道形成对比。这种解耦使得能够包含有关光谱的物理先验知识，而这些知识本身对于分解模型来说是不可访问的，从而人为地扩展数据集以充当总体风险的蒙特卡洛近似。为了测试这个假设，我们提出了两个生成器模型：具有一致性层的一维条件 U-Net (GD-Streamline) 和双路径变换器条件变分自动编码器 (TCVAE)，以及两个分解模型：多层感知器 (MLP) 和傅立叶神经算子 (FNO)。研究结果表明，需要在所提出的解耦框架范围内严格集成和引入谱先验，以防止域和模式崩溃以及生成器模型的幻觉；否则，这会导致错误地近似数据流形，最终导致解混性能不令人满意且超出分布。

English Abstract

The spectral range of the Field Imaging Nanosatellite for Crop residue Hyperspectral Mapping (FINCH), developed by the University of Toronto Aerospace Team's Space System Division, poses significant challenges for hyperspectral unmixing to determine crop residue cover fractional abundances. The severe lack of standard indices necessitates the use of complex, data-driven unmixing models. Complex unmixing models require dense, well-distributed manifolds, whereas ground-truth datasets are sparse and expensive. To better leverage the information content in existing ground-truth datasets, this study presents a framework that decouples interpolation and abundance-mapping estimation via an intermediary conditional data generator, contrasting with traditional single-model unmixing pipelines. This decoupling enables the inclusion of physical prior knowledge about spectra, which is not natively accessible to unmixing models, thereby artificially expanding the dataset to serve as a Monte Carlo approximation of the population risk. To test this hypothesis, we have proposed two generator models: a 1D Conditional U-Net with Conformer Layers (GD-Streamline) and a Dual-Path Transformer Conditional Variational AutoEncoder (TCVAE), and two unmixing models: Multi-Layered Perceptrons (MLP) and Fourier Neural Operators (FNO). The results of the study indicate a need for rigorous integration and introduction of spectral priors within the scope of the proposed decoupling framework to prevent domain and mode collapse and hallucination by the generator models; otherwise, this leads to falsely approximated data manifolds, ultimately resulting in unsatisfactory and out-of-distribution unmixing performance.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

难熔高熵合金（RHEA）因其在高温下保持强度的卓越能力而受到越来越多的关注。然而，目前的研究主要集中在接近等摩尔合金的成分上，而很大一部分成分空间尚未探索。在这里，我们开发了一个基于数据驱动的位错基因组方法的多尺度框架，该方法为跨不同成分的边缘位错运动构建能量景观。来自能量景观的局部构型力被纳入位错动力学模拟中，揭示了连接宏观尺度强度和受局部构型力波动控制的原子尺度局部阻力的标度律。基于该比例定律，开发了一种新的 BCC 合金中刃位错固溶强化模型，随后对考虑强度和延展性的难熔合金进行了数据驱动的探索。所开发的方法和理论具有很强的适应性，可以扩展到其他 BCC 合金系统并指导高通量合金设计。

English Abstract

Refractory high-entropy alloys (RHEAs) have attracted increasing attention for their exceptional ability to retain strength at high temperatures. However, current research mainly concentrates on compositions close to equimolar alloys, leaving a large part of the composition space unexplored. Here, we develop a multiscale framework based on a data-driven dislocation genome method that constructs energy landscapes for edge dislocation motion across various compositions. The local configurational force derived from the energy landscape is incorporated into dislocation dynamics simulations, revealing a scaling law connecting macroscale strength and atomic-scale local resistance governed by fluctuations in the local configurational force. Based on this scaling law, a new solid-solution strengthening model for edge dislocation in BCC alloys is developed, followed by a data-driven exploration of refractory alloys considering both strength and ductility. The developed method and theory are adaptable, allowing for extension to other BCC alloy systems and guiding high-throughput alloy design.

极端条件下氧化铁的机器学习潜力#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

可靠的相位稳定性需要精确的相对自由能，仅靠较小的力误差并不能保证这一点。我们开发了从 Fe2O 到 Fe2O₃ 的五种铁氧化物的原子团膨胀势，涵盖 140--260 GPa 和 1000--11000 K 下的晶态和液态，电子和离子温度匹配。力主导的训练在结构族之间留下高达 41,meV/原子的系统能量偏移，尽管有额外的训练数据，但这种能量偏移仍然存在。更强的能量加权和更弱的正则化在很大程度上消除了这些偏移，而收敛的 k 点采样可以防止进一步的与成分相关的偏差。对于 5000--9000,K 的液体，最终电势实现了 2.0--5.5,meV/atom 的保留能量误差和 3.4,meV/atom 以内的一阶自由能误差。然而，尽管外推等级较低，但未经训练的 NiAs 型 FeO 相仍表现出 57,meV/原子的自由能误差。这些结果表明，可靠的相图计算需要控制相对能量偏移并对每个竞争相进行明确的自由能验证。

English Abstract

Reliable phase stability requires accurate relative free energies, which small force errors alone do not guarantee. We develop atomic-cluster-expansion potentials for five iron oxides from Fe₂O to Fe₂O₃, covering crystalline and liquid states at 140--260,GPa and 1000--11000,K, with electronic and ionic temperatures matched. Force-dominated training leaves systematic energy offsets between structure families of up to 41,meV/atom that persist despite additional training data. Stronger energy weighting and weaker regularization largely remove these offsets, while converged k-point sampling prevents further composition-dependent biases. The final potentials achieve held-out energy errors of 2.0--5.5,meV/atom and first-order free-energy errors within 3.4,meV/atom for liquids at 5000--9000,K. However, an untrained NiAs-type FeO phase exhibits a free-energy error of 57,meV/atom despite a low extrapolation grade. These results show that reliable phase-diagram calculations require control of relative energy offsets and explicit free-energy validation of every competing phase.

quantEM：从可微分物理到神经先验的定量电子显微镜分析#

原题：quantEM: Quantitative Electron Microscopy Analysis from Differentiable Physics to Neural Priors
作者：Colin Ophus、Arthur RC McCray、Georgios Varnavides、Stephanie M Ribet。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-18

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：定量电子显微镜包括成像、衍射、光谱和断层扫描测量，这些测量的分析越来越需要用物理正演模型解决反演问题。开源软件已使许多此类分析得到广泛应用，但大多数包都是围绕单一模态组织的，因此多模态分析需要在包之间移动数据，并且新方法无法轻松重用现有方法的物理模型和优化器。在这里，我们展示了 quantEM，这是一个用于定量电子显微镜的开源 Python 包，它使用 PyTorch 张量进行计算和自动微分，并使用 Zarr 进行存储。我们通过编写可微…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

定量电子显微镜包括成像、衍射、光谱和断层扫描测量，这些测量的分析越来越需要用物理正演模型解决反演问题。开源软件已使许多此类分析得到广泛应用，但大多数包都是围绕单一模态组织的，因此多模态分析需要在包之间移动数据，并且新方法无法轻松重用现有方法的物理模型和优化器。在这里，我们展示了 quantEM，这是一个用于定量电子显微镜的开源 Python 包，它使用 PyTorch 张量进行计算和自动微分，并使用 Zarr 进行存储。我们通过编写可微分的前向模型并从组件库中选择表示、损失函数、约束和优化器来实现每种分析方法，以便每种方法都在 CPU 和 GPU 上运行，可以保存和恢复，并使用相同的校准数据结构和可视化工具。我们描述了当前的模块，包括方向和应变映射、直接和迭代叠层成像、具有神经表示的断层扫描以及 X 射线和电子能量损失光谱，并展示了我们如何从现有组件中组装组合方法，例如叠层成像。 QuantEM 可在 MIT 许可下使用，并附有文档、教程和精选示例数据集。

English Abstract

Quantitative electron microscopy includes imaging, diffraction, spectroscopy, and tomography measurements, and the analysis of these measurements increasingly requires solving inverse problems with physical forward models. Open-source software has made many of these analyses widely available, but most packages are organized around a single modality, so that multimodal analyses require moving data between packages, and new methods cannot easily reuse the physical models and optimizers of existing ones. Here we present quantEM, an open-source Python package for quantitative electron microscopy that uses PyTorch tensors for computation and automatic differentiation, and Zarr for storage. We implement each analysis method by writing a differentiable forward model and choosing a representation, loss function, constraints, and optimizer from a library of components, so that every method runs on CPUs and GPUs, can be saved and resumed, and uses the same calibrated data structures and visualization tools. We describe the current modules, including orientation and strain mapping, direct and iterative ptychography, tomography with neural representations, and X-ray and electron energy-loss spectroscopy, and show how we assemble combined methods such as ptychographic tomography from existing components. quantEM is available under the MIT license, together with documentation, tutorials, and curated example datasets.

基于大语言模型的智能代理，用于锂离子电池的自适应快速充电控制#

原题：Large Language Model-Empowered Intelligent Agent for Adaptive Fast-Charging Control of Lithium-Ion Batteries
作者：Joey Chan、Haoyu Pan、Zhen Chen、ErShun Pan。arXiv · cs.AI，2026-11-16
🤖 AI/机器学习

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：锂离子电池的快速充电是一个多目标控制问题，需要减少充电时间并维持高能量吞吐量，同时抑制加速降解和安全关键的电化学反应。传统协议易于实施，但适应电池化学、操作条件和老化状态变化的能力有限。基于模型的优化和传统的数据驱动控制通常需要准确的模型、昂贵的迭代搜索或特定于任务的策略。我们开发了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能代理，用于自适应电池快速充电控制。在这个闭环框架中，代理解释结构化的电池状态，生成可执行的充电操作，观察由此产生的电池响应…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

锂离子电池的快速充电是一个多目标控制问题，需要减少充电时间并维持高能量吞吐量，同时抑制加速降解和安全关键的电化学反应。传统协议易于实施，但适应电池化学、操作条件和老化状态变化的能力有限。基于模型的优化和传统的数据驱动控制通常需要准确的模型、昂贵的迭代搜索或特定于任务的策略。我们开发了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能代理，用于自适应电池快速充电控制。在这个闭环框架中，代理解释结构化的电池状态，生成可执行的充电操作，观察由此产生的电池响应，并不断调整后续决策。我们使用 PyBaMM 构建了一个涵盖 5 种电池化学成分和 11 个参数集的大规模模拟语料库，并将所得的状态-动作-响应轨迹转换为用于代理训练的结构化过程数据。我们的两阶段策略结合了监督微调（SFT）和组相对策略优化（GRPO）。 SFT 从仿真衍生的演示中建立电池控制语义和可行的决策先验。 GRPO 使代理与涵盖能源吞吐量、退化缓解和操作约束的多目标控制目标保持一致。闭环模拟表明，在匹配的能量吞吐量要求下，该代理在异构电池配置中比传统充电基线和基于优化的策略实现了更长的循环寿命。这项研究为使用 LLM 代理作为电池管理中的顺序决策者提供了基于模拟的概念证明，并朝着支持物理能源系统的语言模型控制迈出了一步。

English Abstract

Fast charging of lithium-ion batteries is a multi-objective control problem that requires reducing charging time and sustaining high energy throughput while suppressing accelerated degradation and safety-critical electrochemical reactions. Conventional protocols are easy to implement but have limited ability to adapt to variations in battery chemistry, operating conditions, and aging state. Model-based optimization and conventional data-driven control often require accurate models, costly iterative search, or task-specific policies. We develop a large language model (LLM)-empowered intelligent agent for adaptive battery fast-charging control. In this closed-loop framework, the agent interprets structured battery states, generates executable charging actions, observes the resulting battery response, and continuously adjusts subsequent decisions. We construct a large-scale simulation corpus with PyBaMM across five battery chemistries and 11 parameter sets and convert the resulting state-action-response trajectories into structured process data for agent training. Our two-stage strategy combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). SFT establishes battery-control semantics and feasible decision priors from simulation-derived demonstrations. GRPO aligns the agent with a multi-objective control objective covering energy throughput, degradation mitigation, and operational constraints. Closed-loop simulations show that, under matched energy-throughput requirements, the agent achieves longer cycling life than conventional charging baselines and optimization-based strategies across heterogeneous battery configurations. This study provides a simulation-grounded proof of concept for using LLM agents as sequential decision makers in battery management and takes a step toward language-model-enabled control of physical energy systems.

English Abstract

The adsorption energy serves as a crucial descriptor for the large-scale screening of catalysts. Nevertheless, the limited distribution of training data for the extensively utilised machine learning interatomic potential, predominantly sourced from near-equilibrium structures, results in unreliable adsorption structures and consequent adsorption energy predictions. In this context, we present DBCata, a deep generative model that integrates a periodic Brownian-bridge framework with an equivariant graph neural network to establish a low-dimensional transition manifold between unrelaxed and DFT-relaxed structures, without requiring explicit energy or force information. Upon training, DBCata effectively generates high-fidelity adsorption geometries, achieving an interatomic distance mean absolute error of 0.035 Å on theMamunHighT2019dataset from Catalysis-Hub, which is approximately 70% lower than that of the current state-of-the-art machine learning potential models. Moreover, the corresponding DFT accuracy can be improved within 0.1 eV in 94% of instances by identifying and refining anomalous predictions through a hybrid chemical-heuristic and self-supervised outlier detection approach. We demonstrate that DBCata enables accelerated high-throughput computational screening for alloy catalysts in the oxygen reduction reaction, highlighting the potential of DBCata as a powerful tool for catalyst design and optimisation.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

过渡态控制着化学反应速率和结果，但定位它们仍然是计算化学中的一个核心挑战。双端搜索方法在给定反应物和产物结构的情况下寻找过渡态，但通常依赖于为密度泛函理论开发的离散路径表示，其中势能表面评估的成本很高。机器学习原子间势（MLIP）极大地降低了这种成本，并利用自动微分来有效地访问高阶导数。我们介绍了 Popcornn，这是一个通过使用并行自适应数值积分器优化 MLIP 上的连续神经网络路径来查找过渡状态的框架。这种组合消除了路径离散伪影，降低了超参数敏感性，并支持非局部优化以使用二阶势能表面信息克服局部特征。在数千个有机反应的 MLIP 中，Popcornn 始终能够比标准方法（例如轻推松紧带和增长弦）恢复更正确的过渡态，使其成为一种强大的、自动化的高通量过渡态搜索方法。 Popcornn 的基础设施在路径目标、集成策略和优化算法方面非常灵活，使其成为研究基于 MLIP 的路径优化中的开放问题的实用工具和平台。

English Abstract

Transition states govern chemical reaction rates and outcomes, yet locating them remains a central challenge in computational chemistry. Double-ended search methods find transition states given reactant and product structures, but generally rely on discretized path representations developed for density functional theory, where potential energy surface evaluations are expensive. Machine learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) dramatically reduce this cost and leverage automatic differentiation to access higher-order derivatives efficiently. We introduce Popcornn, a framework to find transition states by optimizing continuous neural network paths atop an MLIP with a parallelized adaptive numerical integrator. This combination eliminates path discretization artifacts, reduces hyperparameter sensitivity, and enables non-local optimization to overcome local features using second-order potential-energy-surface information. Popcornn consistently recovers more correct transition states than standard methods such as nudged elastic band and growing string, across MLIPs on thousands of organic reactions, establishing it as a robust, automation-ready method for high-throughput transition state search. Popcornn's infrastructure is flexible in path objective, integration strategy, and optimization algorithm, making it a practical tool and platform for investigating open questions in MLIP-based path optimization.

基础原子间势的突现疲劳动力学#

原题：Emergent Fatigue Dynamics from Foundational Interatomic Potentials

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基础机器学习原子间势是在大型、多样化的 DFT 数据集上进行训练的，但它们的验证依赖于平衡状态，这并不能保证大规模分子动力学中正确的涌现行为。在没有直接实验验证的情况下，与物理激励潜力的比较对于获得洞察力很有价值。我们区分静态属性（可热访问）和紧急属性（需要外部疲劳应力来驱动罕见事件），并比较两者的潜力。 EAM 势和 NequIP 基础势均与许多近平衡特性的参考数据非常一致，但 NequIP 预测异常低的本征堆垛层错能（比 EAM 低 sim7 倍），因此预测在经典格里菲斯裂纹问题建模的纳米级缺陷的疲劳循环下会有更大的塑性变形。加载后的每原子能量分布表明，两种电势在远高于热可达到的能量时差异最大。我们还记录了与内存相关的崩溃和无声推断，作为这些基础模型形式的实践者的实际问题。

English Abstract

Foundational machine-learned interatomic potentials are trained on large, diverse DFT datasets, but their validation rests on equilibrium states, which does not guarantee correct emergent behavior in large-scale molecular dynamics. Where no direct experimental validation is available, comparisons to physically motivated potentials are valuable for drawing insight. We distinguish static properties, which are thermally accessible, from emergent properties, which require work from an external fatigue stress to drive rare events, and compare the potentials on both. An EAM potential and the NequIP foundational potential both agree closely with reference data for many near-equilibrium properties, but NequIP predicts an anomalously low intrinsic stacking fault energy (sim7 times lower than EAM) and thus predicts far more plastic deformation under fatigue cycling of a nanoscale flaw modeled after the classical Griffith crack problem. Per-atom energy distributions after loading show that the two potentials differ most at energies far above what is thermally accessible. We also document memory-related crashes and silent extrapolation as practical concerns for practitioners of these foundational model forms.

原题：Synthetic Crystal Data Generation for Diffusion Pretraining in Material Property Prediction
作者：Aamod Varma、Shuyi Jia、Victor Fung。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-18

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural networks、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基于扩散的预训练已成为一种强大的策略，用于学习图神经网络中的可转移表示以进行材料属性预测，而无需每个预训练结构的能量、力和应力等量子力学标签。然而，该方法保留了一个关键的依赖性：它需要位于势能表面局部最小值附近的结构，这些结构传统上是通过已知或物理合成化合物的昂贵的 DFT 弛豫获得的。这种依赖性限制了可用预训练数据的规模和结构多样性，导致晶体结构空间的大片区域未被探索。在这项工作中，我们将基于扩散的预训练与合成结构生成联系起来，以减少这一瓶颈。我们引入了一种综合数据生成管道，结合了三种互补策略：扰动和放松、通过 MatterGen 生成采样以及原型结构枚举。在需要结构弛豫的情况下，我们使用快速机器学习的原子间势，而不是对每个生成的结构执行新的 DFT 弛豫。我们使用随机初始化的 Orb-v2 主干网络进一步将坐标去噪控制与连续时间方差保持 (VP) 和子 VP 扩散目标进行比较。源比较涵盖七个 MatBench 属性预测任务，第八个任务 textttmp_gap 包含在原型结构缩放研究中。在所有七个源比较任务上，每个合成预训练条件在数值上都优于随机初始化。在固定的 400 epoch 计划下将原型语料库从 20k 扩展到 165k，在大多数任务上在数字上匹配或超过 MP-20k 预训练；由于该协议增加了数据多样性和优化更新，因此结果反映了数据和计算的联合扩展。

English Abstract

Diffusion-based pretraining has emerged as a powerful strategy for learning transferable representations in graph neural networks for materials property prediction without requiring quantum-mechanical labels such as energies, forces, and stresses for every pretraining structure. However, the method retains a critical dependency: it requires structures residing near local minima of a potential energy surface, which have traditionally been obtained through costly DFT relaxations of known or physically synthesized compounds. This reliance constrains both the scale and the structural diversity of available pretraining data, leaving large regions of crystal structure space unexplored. In this work, we connect diffusion-based pretraining with synthetic structure generation to reduce this bottleneck. We introduce a synthetic data generation pipeline combining three complementary strategies: perturb-and-relax, generative sampling via MatterGen, and protostructure enumeration. Where structural relaxation is required, we use fast machine-learned interatomic potentials instead of performing a new DFT relaxation for each generated structure. We further compare a coordinate-denoising control with continuous-time variance-preserving (VP) and sub-VP diffusion objectives using a randomly initialized Orb-v2 backbone. The source comparison covers seven MatBench property-prediction tasks, and an eighth task, textttmp_gap, is included in the protostructure scaling study. Every synthetic-pretraining condition numerically outperforms random initialization on all seven source-comparison tasks. Scaling the protostructure corpus from 20k to 165k under a fixed 400-epoch schedule numerically matches or exceeds MP-20k pretraining on most tasks; because this protocol increases both data diversity and optimization updates, the result reflects joint data-and-compute scaling.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

蒙特卡罗/分子动力学 (MCMD) 模拟广泛用于研究介观尺度材料的特性。数十万个原子的模拟通常依赖于经验势，因为具有机器学习原子间势的 MCMD 在现有实现中在这种规模上是不切实际的。在这里，我们介绍了一种用于 MCMD 的混合 MPI/OpenMP 方案，在 LAMMPS 中具有原子簇扩展 (ACE) 潜力。蒙特卡罗移动根据局部能量变化进行评估，并在 MPI 等级上并行执行，而分子动力学则在 OpenMP 线程上并行执行。在 CPU 数量固定的情况下，蒙特卡洛移动的挂起时间减少的因子等于 MPI 等级的数量。更大的模拟单元还可以减少自由能计算的统计误差，因为热波动随着系统尺寸的增加而减小。我们通过计算强非谐 Hf--Ti 系统中 HCP 和 BCC 相的自由能以及模拟 670,000 个原子的纳米晶铜样品中的锆偏析来演示该方案。对于 Cu--Zr，ACE 电势和经验电势预测相似的晶界成分，但溶解在晶粒中的锆量不同。该方案使得具有机器学习原子间势的 MCMD 模拟在微观结构的长度尺度上变得实用。

English Abstract

Monte Carlo/molecular dynamics (MCMD) simulations are widely used to study the properties of materials at the mesoscale. Simulations with hundreds of thousands of atoms usually rely on empirical potentials because MCMD with machine-learning interatomic potentials is impractical at this scale in existing implementations. Here, we introduce a hybrid MPI/OpenMP scheme for MCMD with Atomic Cluster Expansion (ACE) potentials in LAMMPS. Monte Carlo moves are evaluated from local energy changes and carried out in parallel over MPI ranks, while molecular dynamics is parallelized over OpenMP threads. At a fixed number of CPUs, the wall time of the Monte Carlo moves decreases by a factor equal to the number of MPI ranks. Larger simulation cells also reduce the statistical errors of free energy calculations because thermal fluctuations decrease with system size. We demonstrate the scheme by calculating free energies of the HCP and BCC phases in the strongly anharmonic Hf--Ti system and by simulating zirconium segregation in a nanocrystalline copper sample of 670,000 atoms. For Cu--Zr, an ACE potential and an empirical potential predict similar grain boundary compositions but different amounts of zirconium dissolved in the grains. The scheme makes MCMD simulations with machine-learning interatomic potentials practical at the length scales of microstructure.

中文摘要

由于成分空间较大，无法通过实验或从头计算进行测试，因此在高熵陶瓷中寻找最佳成分具有挑战性。在这里，我们提出了 IVB 和 VB 族过渡金属（Ti、Zr、Hf、V、Nb 和 Ta）中等摩尔和非等摩尔高熵碳化物 (HEC) 的高通量筛选。大型原子模型 MACE 和基于从头开始的结构数据集训练的基于深度学习的原子间势 DeePMD 用于有效筛选 Ti、Zr、Hf、V、Nb 和 Ta 过渡金属高熵碳化物中的数千种潜在非等摩尔成分。预测一组等摩尔和非等摩尔 HEC 是稳定的，以便根据预测进一步合成这些组合物。

English Abstract

Search for optimal compositions among high-entropy ceramics are challenging due to large composition space, which are unable to test either experimentally or by ab initio calculations. Here we present a high-throughput screening for equimolar and non-equimolar high-entropy carbides (HEC) among group IVB and VB transition metals (Ti, Zr, Hf, V, Nb and Ta). A set of both equimolar and non-equimolar HECs are predicted to be stable in order to further synthesize those compositions based on the predictions.

原题：Out-of-training properties in frozen interatomic potential embeddings are decodable and ordered by physical scale

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势（MLIP）接受能量、力和应力的训练，但它们的冻结表示可以预测其他材料特性。我们跨层、读出和等变通道探测 ORB-v3 和 UMA-S，以了解 154,875 个材料项目晶体的九个属性。两种模型在所有目标上都优于仅组合描述符，其中包括训练目标之外的五个目标；物理单位的错误有助于评估潜在的筛选应用。最佳深度因属性而异，与物理尺度排序一致。随机权重控制揭示了预训练的强烈依赖于属性的好处。 UMA-S 的旋转不变角度通道改善了晶体系统分类，而其全张量的较高分数在旋转下消失；匹配模型控制不会孤立等方差作为解释。不同探针的准确度各不相同，但最近邻预测通常接近 XGBoost 性能，即使材料不会根据其属性形成不同的簇。我们还测试在一个模型的嵌入上训练的线性预测器是否可以在学习线性变换后使用另一个模型的嵌入来预测属性。尽管两个模型靠近放置的原子存在差异，但预测准确性在很大程度上得到了保留。这些结果指导重新使用冻结的 MLIP 表示进行材料预测。

English Abstract

Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are trained on energies, forces and stresses, yet their frozen representations can predict other materials properties. We probe ORB-v3 and UMA-S across layers, read-outs and equivariant channels for nine properties of 154,875 Materials Project crystals. Both models outperform composition-only descriptors on all targets, including five outside their training objectives; errors in physical units help assess potential screening applications. Optimal depth varies by property, consistent with a physical-scale ordering. Random-weight controls reveal strongly property-dependent benefits of pretraining. UMA-S's rotation-invariant angular channels improve crystal-system classification, while higher scores from its full tensor disappear under rotation; matched-model controls do not isolate equivariance as the explanation. Accuracy varies across different probes, but nearest-neighbour prediction often approaches XGBoost performance, even though materials do not form distinct clusters according to their properties. We also test whether a linear predictor trained on one model's embeddings can predict properties using the other model's embeddings after a learned linear transformation. Predictive accuracy is largely preserved, despite differences in which atoms the two models place close together. These results guide the reuse of frozen MLIP representations for materials prediction.
